# Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot

ברטנורא על משנה תרומות

Link: [https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot)

חיבור התשתית הקדום של חז״ל, המתעד מגוון דעות הלכתיות ממסורת התורה שבעל פה. נחתם בשנת 200 לספירה לערך.

16th-century commentary printed in most editions of the Mishnah, including summaries of talmudic discussions and legal conclusions.

Sections: Chapter-Mishnah-Comment

Location: Mishnah/Rishonim on Mishnah/Bartenura/Seder Zeraim

Originally from [Sefaria](https://www.sefaria.org) via [TorahLM.org](https://torahlm.org) — see [licensing](https://torahlm.org/sefaria_license).

Version (He): On Your Way (Segments: 409, License: Public Domain)

Version (En): Bartenura on Mishnah, trans. by Rabbi Robert Alpert, 2020 (Segments: 403)


## Chapter 1


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 1:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/1:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/1:1:1)


**חֲמִשָּׁה לֹא יִתְרֹמוּ.** מִנְיָנָא לְמִעוּטֵי הָא דְאָמַר רַבִּי יְהוּדָה לְקַמָּן קָטָן שֶׁלֹּא הֵבִיא שְׁתֵּי שְׂעָרוֹת תְּרוּמָתוֹ תְרוּמָה, קָא מַשְׁמַע לָן דְּאֵין תְּרוּמָתוֹ תְרוּמָה:


חמשה לא יתרומו – A number to exclude that which Rabbi Yehuda says further on (Mishnah 3), “A minor who did not bring forth two [pubic] hairs, his [separation of] heave-offering/Terumah/priest’s due is not a heave-offering, which tells us that his [separation of] heave-offering is not Terumah (the 2% initial sacred gift that one gives to the Kohen).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 1:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/1:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/1:1:2)


**הַחֵרֵשׁ וְהַשּׁוֹטֶה וְהַקָּטָן כוּ'.** כֻּלְּהוּ מֵחַד קְרָא נַפְקָא לָן, דִּכְתִיב (שמות כח) דַּבֵּר אֶל בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל וְיִקְחוּ לִי תְרוּמָה מֵאֵת כָּל אִישׁ אֲשֶׁר יִדְּבֶנּוּ לִבּוֹ. אֶל בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל פְּרָט לְנָכְרִי, מֵאֵת כָּל אִישׁ פְּרָט לְקָטָן, אֲשֶׁר יִדְּבֶנּוּ לִבּוֹ פְּרָט לְחֵרֵשׁ וְשׁוֹטֶה שֶׁאֵין בָּהֶם דַּעַת לִהְיוֹת נוֹדְבִין. וְקָטָן לֹא מָצֵי מַמְעִיט מֵאֲשֶׁר יִדְּבֶנּוּ לִבּוֹ, דְּאִיכָּא קָטָן שֶׁהִגִּיעַ לְעוֹנַת נְדָרִים שֶׁיֵּשׁ לוֹ דַּעַת לִהְיוֹת נוֹדֵב. וְזֹאת הַתְּרוּמָה אֲשֶׁר תִּקְחוּ מֵאִתָּם פְּרָט לְתוֹרֵם שֶׁאֵינוֹ שֶׁלּוֹ. וְשׁוֹטֶה, הַמְאַבֵּד מַה שֶּׁנּוֹתְנִים לוֹ קָרוּי שׁוֹטֶה:


החרש והשוטה והקטן – All of them are derived from one Biblical verse, as it is written (Exodus 25:2): “Tell the Israelite people to bring Me gifts; [you shall accept for Me] from every person whose heart so moves him.” [The words] "אל בני ישראל" /”to the Israelite people” -except for a heathen; [the words] מאת כל איש/“from every person” – except for a minor; [the words] "אשר ידבנו לבו"/”whose heart so moves him” – except for a deaf-mute and imbecile, who lack the temperament to be able to donate. And a minor is not able [even] to do a little, from [the words] "אשר ידבנו לבו"/”whose heart so moves him,” for there is a minor who arrived at the season of taking vows, who has the temperament to be able to donate. [The words] "וזאת התרומה אשר תקחו מאתם"/”And these are the gifts that you shall accept from them” (verse 3), excluding to the person who donates what is not his. And the “imbecile”- who loses what they give him is called an imbecile/"שוטה".


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 1:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/1:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/1:1:3)


**אֲפִלּוּ בִרְשׁוּת.** שֶׁל יִשְׂרָאֵל, שֶׁעֲשָׂאוֹ יִשְׂרָאֵל שְׁלוּחוֹ לִתְרֹם, אֵין תְּרוּמָתוֹ תְרוּמָה, דִּכְתִיב (במדבר יח) כֵּן תָּרִימוּ גַם אַתֶּם, גַּם לְרַבּוֹת שְׁלוּחֲכֶם, מָה אַתֶּם בְּנֵי בְרִית אַף שְׁלוּחֲכֶם בְּנֵי בְרִית:


אפילו ברשות – [even with the permission] of a Jew as an Israelite made him his agent to give Terumah/the heave-offering, his donation of Terumah is not a donation, as it is written (Numbers 18:28) “So shall you on your part set aside a gift [for the LORD from all the tithes that you receive from the Israelites]…” – also to include your agents. Just as you are members of the covenant, so also your agents [must be] members of the covenant (“Jews”).



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 1:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/1:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/1:2:1)


**לֹא יִתְרֹם.** לְכַתְּחִלָּה, דְּצָרִיךְ לְהַשְׁמִיעַ לְאָזְנוֹ כְּשֶׁיְּבָרֵךְ:


לא יתרום – ab initio for one needs to let his ears hear as he makes the blessing (see Mishnah Berakhot, Chapter 3, Mishnah 3 which also deals with the issue of “letting his ears hear”/"צריך להשמיע לאזניו" .


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 1:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/1:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/1:2:2)


**חֵרֵשׁ שֶׁדִּבְּרוּ בוֹ חֲכָמִים בְּכָל מָקוֹם וְכוּ'.** לָאו כְּלָלָא הוּא, דִּבְרֵישׁ חֲגִיגָה תָּנֵי חֵרֵשׁ, וּמְדַבֵּר וְאֵינוֹ שׁוֹמֵעַ בִּכְלָל, וּבְפֶרֶק מִצְוַת חֲלִיצָה לְעִנְיַן חֲלִיצָה:


חרש שדברו בו חכמים – it is not a general principle, for at the beginning of Tractate Hagigah it is taught that a deaf person who speaks but does not hear is included, and in the chapter מצות חליצה/The command of Halitzah (see specifically, Mishnah 4), regarding the subject of “removing the shoe” [of the dead husband’s brother who refuses to perform a levirate marriage].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 1:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/1:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/1:2:3)


**שֶׁאֵינוֹ שׁוֹמֵעַ וְאֵינוֹ מְדַבֵּר.** שֶׁנּוֹלַד חֵרֵשׁ מִמְּעֵי אִמּוֹ, וְכֵיוָן שֶׁלֹּא שָׁמַע מֵעוֹלָם מַה שֶּׁמְּדַבְּרִים לוֹ אִי אֶפְשָׁר לוֹ שֶׁיְּדַבֵּר:


שאינו שומע ואינו מדבר – who born deaf from his mother’s womb, and since he never heard what they are saying to him, it is impossible for him that he could speak.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 1:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/1:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/1:3:1)


**רַבִּי יְהוּדָה אוֹמֵר תְּרוּמָתוֹ תְרוּמָה.** בְּדִיעֲבַד, דְּלֵית לֵיהּ לְרַבִּי יְהוּדָה הַךְ דְּרָשָׁא מֵאֵת כָּל אִישׁ פְּרָט לְקָטָן:


רבי יהודה אומר: תרומתו תרומה – de facto, for Rabbi Yehuda does not [hold] by this interpretation of מאת כל איש/”from every person” (Exodus 25:2) -except for a minor.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 1:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/1:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/1:3:2)


**עַד שֶׁלֹּא בָא לְעוֹנַת נְדָרִים.** הַזָּכָר בֶּן שְׁתֵּים עֶשְׂרֵה שָׁנָה וְיוֹם אֶחָד, וְהַנְּקֵבָה בַּת אַחַת עֶשְׂרֵה שָׁנִים וְיוֹם אֶחָד. וּמֵאוֹתָהּ עוֹנָה וְאֵילָךְ אִם תָּרַם תְּרוּמָתוֹ תְרוּמָה. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי:


עד שלא בא לעונת נדרים – A male at the age of twelve years and one day and a female at the age of eleven years and one day, and from that period onward, if he donated the Terumah/the sacred heave-offering (2% gift to the Kohen), what he set aside is Terumah. And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yosi.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 1:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/1:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/1:4:1)


**אֵין תּוֹרְמִין זֵיתִים עַל הַשֶּׁמֶן.** מִי שֶׁיֵּשׁ לוֹ שֶׁמֶן שֶׁחַיָּב לְהַפְרִישׁ מִמֶּנּוּ תְּרוּמָה, וְזֵיתִים שֶׁחַיָּב לְהַפְרִישׁ מֵהֶן תְּרוּמָה, אֵינוֹ יָכוֹל לִתְרֹם מִן הַזֵּיתִים כְּפִי הַשִּׁעוּר שֶׁיֵּשׁ לוֹ לִתְרֹם עַל הַזֵּיתִים וְעַל הַשֶּׁמֶן, וְלִפְטֹר הַשֶּׁמֶן בִּתְרוּמַת הַזֵּיתִים, דִּכְתִיב (במדבר יח) כַּדָּגָן מִן הַגֹּרֶן, מִן הַגָּמוּר עַל הַגָּמוּר, וְלֹא מִמַּה שֶּׁאֵינוֹ גָמוּר עַל הַגָּמוּר:


אין תורמין זיתים על השמן – He who has [olive] oil that he is liable to separate from it the [sacred] heave-offering (i.e., the 2% for the Kohen) and olives that he is liable to separate from them the [sacred] heave-offering is not able to give from [sacred] heave-offering from the olives according to the measure that he has to donate for the olives and the oil [together], and to be exempt from [donating] the oil with the heave-offering of the olives, as it is written (Numbers 18:27): “[This shall be accounted to you as your gift.] As with the new grain from the threshing floor [or the flow from the vat],” – from what is completed [you donate as heave-offering] for what is completed but you do not [donate as heave-offering] from what is not complete on what is complete.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 1:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/1:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/1:4:2)


**וְאִם תָּרַם בֵּית שַׁמַּאי אוֹמְרִים תְּרוּמַת עַצְמָן יֵשׁ בָּהֶן.** כְּלוֹמַר אֵין כָּאן תְּרוּמָה אֶלָּא עַל הַזֵּיתִים בִּלְבָד, אֲבָל לֹא עַל הַשֶּׁמֶן:


ואם תרם בית שמאי אומרים: תרומת עצמן יש להן – that is to say, there is no [sacred] heave-offering here, other than only on the olives, but not on the oil.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 1:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/1:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/1:4:3)


**וּבֵית הִלֵּל אוֹמְרִים אֵינָהּ תְּרוּמָה.** הוֹאִיל וְנִתְכַּוֵּן לִתְרֹם גַּם עַל הַשֶּׁמֶן אֵין כָּאן תְּרוּמָה לֹא עַל הַזֵּיתִים וְלֹא עַל הַשֶּׁמֶן, וַהֲרֵי הוּא כְּאִלּוּ לֹא תָרַם כְּלָל:


ובית הלל אומרים: אינה תרומה – for since he had the intention of donating [the heave-offering/priest’s due] also on the oil, there is no Terumah/heave-offering – neither on the olives nor on the oil, and it is as if he had not donated the [sacred] heave-offering at all.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 1:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/1:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/1:5:1)


**אֵין תּוֹרְמִין מִן הַלֶּקֶט מִן הַשִּׁכְחָה וּמִן הַפֵּאָה.** מִי שֶׁיֵּשׁ לוֹ כְּרִי שֶׁל טֶבֶל אֵינוֹ יָכוֹל לְהַפְרִישׁ עָלָיו תְּרוּמָה מִלֶּקֶט וְשִׁכְחָה וּפֵאָה שֶׁבְּיָדוֹ, שֶׁאֵלּוּ מַתְּנוֹת עֲנִיִּים הֵן וְאֵינוֹ יָכוֹל לְתַקֵּן טִבְלוֹ בְּמַתְּנוֹת עֲנִיִּים:


אין תורמין מן הלקט מן השכחה ומן הפאה – whomever has a heap of produce, pending the separation of sacred gifts to the Kohanim and Levites may not set aside upon it priest’s due from produce that fell, was forgotten [in the field] or from the corner of the field that is in his hand, as these are gifts to the poor and one cannot to make his heaps of produce pending separation of the sacred gifts legally fit for use by giving the priestly due with the gifts to the poor.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 1:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/1:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/1:5:2)


**הַהֶפְקֵר.** דִּכְתִיב (דברים יד) וּבָא הַלֵּוִי כִּי אֵין לוֹ חֵלֶק וְנַחֲלָה עִמָּךְ, מִמַּה שֶּׁיֵּשׁ לְךָ וְאֵין לוֹ אַתָּה חַיָּב לִתֵּן, יָצָא הֶפְקֵר שֶׁיָּדְךָ וְיָדוֹ שָׁוִין בּוֹ:


Concerning that which is הפקר/ownerless, it is written (Deuteronomy 14:29): “Then the Levite, who has not hereditary portion as you have….” from what you have and that he lacks, you are required to give, excluding that which is ownerless, for both your hand and his hand are equivalent [concerning this].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 1:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/1:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/1:5:3)


**וְלֹא מִמַּעֲשֵׂר רִאשׁוֹן שֶׁנִּטְּלָה תְּרוּמָתוֹ.** אֵין תּוֹרֵם מִמֶּנּוּ תְּרוּמָה גְדוֹלָה עַל כְּרִי שֶׁל טֶבֶל שֶׁבְּיָדוֹ. וְאִיצְטְרִיךְ לְאַשְׁמוּעִינַן הֵיכָא שֶׁהִקְדִּימוֹ בֶן לֵוִי לַכֹּהֵן בַּשִּׁבֳּלִים, שֶׁהוּא פָּטוּר מִתְּרוּמָה גְדוֹלָה, וְדַוְקָא שֶׁנִּטְּלָה תְּרוּמָתוֹ, אֲבָל לֹא נִטְּלָה תְּרוּמָתוֹ יָכוֹל לִתְרֹם, דְּתַנְיָא בְּסִפְרִי מִנַּיִן לְבֶן לֵוִי שֶׁרָצָה לַעֲשׂוֹת מִמַּעֲשֵׂר רִאשׁוֹן שֶׁלּוֹ תְּרוּמָה גְדוֹלָה, מִנַּיִן שֶׁעוֹשֶׂה, תַּלְמוּד לוֹמַר (במדבר יח) כִּי אֶת מַעְשַׂר בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל אֲשֶׁר יָרִימוּ לַה'‏ תְּרוּמָה, מִכָּאן שֶׁאִם רָצָה לַעֲשׂוֹתוֹ תְּרוּמָה לַאֲחֵרִים עוֹשֶׂה, יָכוֹל אַף לְאַחַר שֶׁנִּטְּלָה תְּרוּמָתוֹ יְהֵא עוֹשֶׂה אוֹתוֹ תְּרוּמָה לַאֲחֵרִים, תַּלְמוּד לוֹמַר (שם) אֶת מִקְדְּשׁוֹ מִמֶּנּוּ, בִּזְמַן שֶׁקָּדְשׁוֹ בְּתוֹכוֹ הוּא עוֹשֶׂה אוֹתוֹ תְּרוּמָה לַאֲחֵרִים, אֵין קָדְשׁוֹ בְּתוֹכוֹ אֵין עוֹשֶׂה אוֹתוֹ תְּרוּמָה לַאֲחֵרִים:


ולא ממעשר ראשון שנטלה תרומתו – We do not donate from it the Kohen’s share of the produce (2%) on a pile of produce requiring separation of Levitical and Priestly tithes that is in his hand, and it was necessary for us to teach where they placed the son of a Levite ahead of a Kohen with regard to sheaves that are exempt from the “great Terumah”[the 2% that goes to the Kohen], and specifically where he took his priest’s due , but where he did not take his priest’s due, he can make the donation as it is taught in [Midrash] Sifrei [Bemidbar/Numbers, Piska 119] : From where does the son of a Levite who wanted to take from his First Tithe – the Great Terumah (the 2% for the Kohen), from where [do we learn] that he may do this? As the inference teaches us: (Numbers 18:24): “For it is the tithes set aside by the Israelites as a gift to the LORD [that I gave to the Levites their share]...,” that if he wanted to make it priest’s due for others, he may do so; he may, even after he has taken his priest’s due – he may make it priest’s due for others, as the inference teaches us (Numbers 18:29): “the part that of that is to be consecrated.” As long as its holiness is within it, he can make it Terumah/priest’s due for others; but if its holiness is not within it, he cannot make it Terumah for others.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 1:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/1:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/1:5:4)


**וְלֹא מִמַּעֲשֵׂר שֵׁנִי וְהֶקְדֵּשׁ שֶׁנִּפְדּוּ.** הַאי תַּנָּא סָבַר מַעֲשֵׂר שֵׁנִי מָמוֹן גָּבוֹהַּ הוּא. וְהֵיכָא דְּלֹא נִפְדּוּ פְּשִׁיטָא דְּאֵינוֹ יָכוֹל לְתַקֵּן טִבְלוֹ בְּמָמוֹן שֶׁל גָּבוֹהַּ, אֲבָל הֵיכָא דְנִפְדּוּ סָלְקָא דַעְתָּךְ אֲמֵינָא דְּיָכוֹל לַעֲשׂוֹתָן תְּרוּמָה גְדוֹלָה עַל טִבְלוֹ. וְכִי תֵימָא הָא נַמִּי פְּשִׁיטָא, דְּחֻלִּין גְּמוּרִים הוּא, אִצְטָרִיךְ הֵיכָא דְּהִקְדִּימוֹ בַשִּׁבֳּלִים, דְּפָטוּר מִתְּרוּמָה גְדוֹלָה כְּדִפְרִישִׁית בְּמַעֲשֵׂר רִאשׁוֹן. וְהֶקְדֵּשׁ שֶׁנִּפְדָּה נַמִּי אִצְטָרִיךְ, כְּשֶׁהִקְדִּישׁ טֶבֶל וּמְרָחוֹ הַגִּזְבָּר וְאַחַר כָּךְ פְּדָאוֹ, דְּפָטוּר מִן הַתְּרוּמָה:


ולא ממעשר שני והקדש שנפדו – This Tanna holds that Second Tithe [belongs] to On-High (God), and where it wasn’t redeemed, it is obvious that he cannot to make things legally fit for use by giving the priestly dues with his pile of produce which is forbidden to be eaten pending the separation of priestly gifts with the monies of On-High (God); but, where they had been redeemed, I might think I would say, that he is able to make them “the great Terumah’ (the 2% gift to the Kohen) on his produce which is forbidden to be eaten pending the separation of priestly gifts, and should you say, that this also is obvious, for that which is completely non-sacred is what is necessary, for where they were zealous with the sheaves, where he is exempt from [donating] the priest’s due/Terumah Gedolah , as we explained for First Tithe. והקדש שנפדה – It is also necessary when he sanctifies (i.e., as a gift to the Temple) produce which is forbidden to be eaten pending the separation of priestly gifts, and the treasure has smoothed out the pile and afterwards redeemed it, that it is exempt from Terumah/priest’s due. -


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 1:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/1:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/1:5:5)


**מִן הַפָּטוּר עַל הַחִיּוּב.** כְּגוֹן מִפֵּרוֹת שֶׁלֹּא הֵבִיאוּ שְׁלִישׁ, שֶׁהֵן פְּטוּרִין מִן הַמַּעַשְׂרוֹת, עַל הַפֵּרוֹת שֶׁהֵבִיאוּ שְׁלִישׁ, שֶׁהֵן חַיָּבִין בְּמַעַשְׂרוֹת:


מן הפטור על החיוב – such as from produce that had not yet grown one-third (i.e., not one-third ripe), which are exempt from tithes on the produce instead of from produce that had grown one-third (i.e., that was one-third ripe).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 1:5:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/1:5:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/1:5:6)


**וְלֹא מִן הַתָּלוּשׁ עַל הַמְחֻבָּר.** דִּכְתִיב (במדבר יח) וַהֲרֵמוֹתֶם מִמֶּנּוּ, שֶׁלֹּא יִתְרֹם מִן הַתָּלוּשׁ עַל הַמְחֻבָּר וְלֹא מִן הַמְחֻבָּר עַל הַתָּלוּשׁ, דִּלְשׁוֹן הֲרָמָה לֹא שַׁיָּךְ אֶלָּא בְּתָלוּשׁ, וּמִמֶּנּוּ מַשְׁמַע שֶׁהַתְּרוּמָה וְהַחֻלִּין שְׁנֵיהֶם שָׁוִין:


ולא מן התלוש על המחובר – as it is written (Numbers 18:26): “you shall set aside from them [one-tenth of the tithe as a gift to the LORD],” you shall not give from what is detached instead of what is attached, and not what is attached instead of what is detached, for the language הרמה/separating the priest’s gift does not belong other than with detached [produce], and from it, it implies, that the priest’s due and the non-sacred produce are similar.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 1:5:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/1:5:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/1:5:7)


**מִן הֶחָדָשׁ עַל הַיָּשָׁן.** מִפֵּרוֹת שָׁנָה זוֹ עַל פֵּרוֹת שָׁנָה שֶׁעָבְרָה, וְלֹא מִפֵּרוֹת שָׁנָה שֶׁעָבְרָה עַל פֵּרוֹת שָׁנָה זוֹ, דִּכְתִיב (דברים יד) הַיּוֹצֵא הַשָּׂדֶה שָׁנָה שָׁנָה. אֲבָל שָׂדֶה שֶׁעוֹשָׂה שְׁנֵי גְרָנוֹת בְּשָׁנָה, כְּגוֹן שְׂדֵה בֵית הַשְּׁלָחִין, תּוֹרְמִים וּמְעַשְּׂרִים מִזֶּה עַל זֶה:


מן החדש על הישן – from the produce of this year instead of the produce from the previous year nor the produce from the previous year instead of from the produce of this year, as it is written (Deuteronomy 14:22): “that is brought from the field every year,” but a field that produces two harvesting seasons in a year such as a field which needs irrigation you make the gift of priest’s due from this [year] instead of from the produce of the previous year.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 1:5:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/1:5:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/1:5:8)


**וְלֹא מִפֵּרוֹת הָאָרֶץ עַל פֵּרוֹת חוּצָה לָאָרֶץ.** דִּכְתִיב (ויקרא כז) וְכָל מַעְשַׂר הָאָרֶץ מִזֶּרַע הָאָרֶץ, גָּלֵי קְרָא בְּמַעֲשֵׂר, וְהוּא הַדִּין לִתְרוּמָה. וְכֵן אֵין תּוֹרְמִין מִפֵּרוֹת הָאָרֶץ עַל פֵּרוֹת שֶׁבְּסוּרְיָא, וְלֹא מִפֵּרוֹת שֶׁבְּסוּרְיָא עַל פֵּרוֹת שֶׁבָּאָרֶץ:


ולא מפירות הארץ על פירות חוצה לארץ – As it is written (Leviticus 27:30): “All tithes from the land, whether seed from the ground [or fruit from the tree, are the LORD’s…,” the Biblical verse reveals concerning [First] Tithe and the same law applies for Terumah/priest’s due, and similarly, one does not donate as priest’s due from the produce of the Land of Israel instead of the produce that is found in Syria, nor from the produce that is in Syria instead of the produce from the Land of Israel.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 1:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/1:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/1:6:1)


**חֲמִשָּׁה לֹא יִתְרֹמוּ.** מִנְיָנָא לְמִעוּטֵי מִדְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה, דְּאָמַר קָטָן שֶׁתָּרַם תְּרוּמָתוֹ תְּרוּמָה:


חמשה לא יתרומו – This number excludes from what Rabbi Yehuda said [in Mishnah 3] that a minor who donated the priest’s due/heave-offering, his donation is an offering.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 1:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/1:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/1:6:2)


**הָאִלֵּם.** וְהוּא שֶׁשּׁוֹמֵעַ אֶלָּא שֶׁנִּשְׁתַּתֵּק מֵחֲמַת חֹלִי, לֹא יִתְרֹם, לְפִי שֶׁאֵינוֹ יָכוֹל לְבָרֵךְ. וְכֵן הֶעָרוֹם אֵינוֹ יָכוֹל לְבָרֵךְ, דִּכְתִיב (דברים כג) וְלֹא יִרְאֶה בְךָ עֶרְוַת דָּבָר, בְּשָׁעָה שֶׁאַתָּה מְדַבֵּר בּוֹ לֹא יִרְאֶה בְךָ עֶרְוָה:


האלם – and he hears but stopped speaking as a result of illness, he should not donate priest’s due because he is unable to recite the Blessing. And similarly, is unable to recite the blessing , as it is written (Deuteronomy 23:15): “[Since the LORD your God moves about in your camp to protect you…]; let Him not find anything unseemly among you [and turn away from you];” at the time when you speak about it, He will not find anything unseemly.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 1:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/1:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/1:6:3)


**וּבַעַל קֶרִי.** נַמִּי בִּזְמַן הַתַּקָּנָה מִקַּמֵּי דְּבִטְּלוּהָ לִטְבִילוּתָא לֹא הֲוָה מָצֵי לְבָרֵךְ, דִּתְנַן בַּעַל קֶרִי מְהַרְהֵר בְּלִבּוֹ:


ובעל קרי – also at the time of the ordinance in His presence for since his ritual immersion has been nullified, he was not able to recite the blessing as it is taught in the Mishnah [i.e. Berakhot, Chapter 3, Mishnah 4] that one to whom a pollution (i.e., nocturnal emission) occurred, only thinks (i.e., recites the Shema] in his heart (which, according to Talmud Berakhot 20b – that reviewing a passage in one’s mind is as good as loud recitation).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 1:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/1:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/1:6:4)


**וְהַשִּׁכּוֹר וְהַסּוּמָא.** אֵינָם יוֹדְעִים לִבְרֹר מִן הַיָּפֶה, וַאֲנַן בָּעִינַן שֶׁיְּהֵא הַתּוֹרֵם בּוֹרֵר וְתוֹרֵם מִן הַיָּפֶה, דִּכְתִיב (במדבר יח) מִכָּל חֶלְבּוֹ אֶת מִקְדְּשׁוֹ. וְשִׁכּוֹר מִקְרֵי כָּל שֶׁאֵינוֹ יָכוֹל לְדַבֵּר בִּפְנֵי הַמֶּלֶךְ מֵחֲמַת שִׁכְרוּתוֹ:


והשכור והסומא – who do not know how to single out/sift from that which is fine and we require that the person who donates the priest’s due/Terumah sift and donate that which is fine, as it is written (Numbers 18:29): “from each thing its best portion, [the part thereof that is to be consecrated];” and a drunk person is called such as all who are unable to speak in the presence of the King as a result of his drunkenness.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 1:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/1:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/1:7:1)


**אֵין תּוֹרְמִין לֹא בְמִדָּה.** דִּכְתִיב (שם) וְנֶחְשַׁב לָכֶם תְּרוּמַתְכֶם, בְּמַחֲשָׁבָה אַתָּה תּוֹרֵם, כְּלוֹמַר מֵאֹמֶד, וְאִי אַתָּה תּוֹרֵם לֹא בְמִדָּה וְלֹא בְמִשְׁקָל וְלֹא בְמִנְיָן. וְאַף עַל גַּב דְּהַאי קְרָא בִּתְרוּמַת מַעֲשֵׂר הוּא דִכְתִיב, אִם אֵינוֹ עִנְיָן לִתְרוּמַת מַעֲשֵׂר, שֶׁהֲרֵי נָתְנָה בוֹ תּוֹרָה שִׁעוּר מַעֲשֵׂר מִן הַמַּעֲשֵׂר וְצָרִיךְ שֶׁיִּמְדֹּד כְּדֵי שֶׁיִּתֵּן הַשִּׁעוּר הָרָאוּי, תְּנֵהוּ עִנְיָן לִתְרוּמָה גְדוֹלָה שֶׁלֹּא נָתְנָה בוֹ תּוֹרָה שִׁעוּר:


אין תורמין לא במדה – As it is written (Numbers 18:27): “ This shall be accounted to you as your gift…” by thought, you donate [the sacred gift], that is to say, by estimation, but you do not donate neither by measure nor by weight nor by number. And even though this Biblical verse is written concerning the tithe of the tithe which the Levite owes to the Kohen (Numbers 18:26 – which is 10% of what he had received from Israelites -תרומת מעשר ), if it has no relation to the tithe of the tithe, for the Torah indeed had given it a measure, and he must measure in order that he gives the appropriate size/limit.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 1:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/1:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/1:7:2)


**אֲבָל תּוֹרֵם הוּא אֶת הַמָּדוּד.** שֶׁמּוֹדֵד הוּא אֶת טִבְלוֹ, אוֹ מוֹנֶה וְשׁוֹקֵל אֶת טִבְלוֹ, וּמַכְנִיסוֹ לְתוֹךְ בֵּיתוֹ, וּכְשֶׁמַּפְרִישׁ הַתְּרוּמָה גְדוֹלָה מִמֶּנּוּ מַפְרִישׁ מֵאֹמֶד וְלֹא יִמְדֹּד וְלֹא יִשְׁקֹל וְלֹא יִמְנֶה:


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 1:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/1:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/1:7:3)


**אֵין תּוֹרְמִין בְּסַל וּבְקֻפָּה שֶׁהֵם שֶׁל מִדָּה.** שֶׁהָרוֹאֶה אוֹמֵר בְּמִדָּה הוּא תּוֹרֵם:


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 1:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/1:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/1:7:4)


**אֲבָל תּוֹרֵם הוּא בָהֶן חֶצְיָן וּשְׁלִישָׁן.** דְּכֵיוָן דְּאֵינוֹ מְמַלֵּא אוֹתָן לֵיכָּא לְמִגְזָר:


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 1:7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/1:7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/1:7:5)


**שֶׁחֶצְיָהּ מִדָּה.** שֶׁיֵּשׁ בַּסְּאָה פְּעָמִים סִימָנִין עַד מְקוֹם חֶצְיָהּ, וְאִיכָּא לְמִגְזָר דִּלְמָא אָתוּ לִתְרֹם בְּמִדָּה:



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 1:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/1:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/1:8:1)


**הַזֵּיתִים הַנִּכְתָּשִׁים.** רְגִילִים הָיוּ לִכְתֹּשׁ הַזֵּיתִים בַּמַּכְתֶּשֶׁת לְהוֹצִיא שַׁמְנָן:


הזיתים הנכתשים – it was customary to crush olives in a mortar-shaped cavity to remove their oil.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 1:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/1:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/1:8:2)


**תְּרוּמָתוֹ תְרוּמָה.** גְּמוּרָה הִיא מִדְּאוֹרַיְתָא:


תרומתו תרומה – it is completed, from the Torah.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 1:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/1:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/1:8:3)


**וְיַחֲזֹר וְיִתְרֹם.** מִדְּרַבָּנָן, לְאַחַר שֶׁיַּשְׁלִים כְּתִישַׁת אוֹתָן הַזֵּיתִים וּדְרִיכַת הָעֲנָבִים יַחֲזֹר וְיוֹצִיא תְּרוּמָה שֵׁנִית כַּשִּׁעוּר הַצָּרִיךְ לַשֶּׁמֶן וְלַיַּיִן שֶׁיָּצָא:


ויחזור ויתרום – from the perspective of the Rabbis, after he has completed the pounding of those olives and the treading of the grapes, he will go back and remove the priest’s due a second time according to the measure that is needed for oil and win that he has removed.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 1:8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/1:8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/1:8:4)


**הָרִאשׁוֹנָה מְדַמַּעַת.** אִם נָפְלָה לְפָחוֹת מִמֵּאָה חֻלִּין נַעֲשָׂה הַכֹּל מְדֻמָּע וְאָסוּר לְזָרִים, וְיִמְכֹּר הַכֹּל לַכֹּהֵן, דִּתְרוּמָה גְּמוּרָה הִיא מִדְּאוֹרַיְתָא:


הראשונה מדמעת – if it fell to less than one-hundred [parts] non-holy produce, everything is made subject to the law of Terumah through an admixture and it is prohibited to “foreigners” (i.e., non-Kohanim) and everything is sold to a Kohen – for it is complete priest’s due according to the Torah.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 1:8:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/1:8:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/1:8:5)


**וְחַיָּבִין עָלֶיהָ חֹמֶשׁ.** זָר הָאוֹכְלָהּ בִּשְׁגָגָה מְשַׁלֵּם קֶרֶן וְחֹמֶשׁ, כְּדִין כָּל זָר אוֹכֵל תְּרוּמָה:


וחייבין עליה חומש – a foreigner (i.e., non-Kohen) who eats it inadvertently pays the principle and [an additional] one-fifth according to the law regarding all foreigners who eat priest’s due.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 1:8:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/1:8:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/1:8:6)


**אֲבָל לֹא שְׁנִיָּה.** לְפִי שֶׁאֵינָהּ אֶלָּא מִדְּרַבָּנָן, לְפִיכָךְ אֵינָהּ נֶאֱכֶלֶת עַד שֶׁיַּפְרִישׁוּ עָלֶיהָ תְּרוּמָה וּמַעַשְׂרוֹת מִמָּקוֹם אַחֵר, דְּמִדְּאוֹרַיְתָא הִיא טֶבֶל גָּמוּר. וְאִי קַשְׁיָא מַאי שְׁנָא הָכָא בְּתוֹרֵם שֶׁמֶן עַל הַזֵּיתִים הַנִּכְתָּשִׁים דִּתְרוּמָתוֹ תְרוּמָה וְיַחֲזֹר וְיִתְרֹם, וּמַאי שְׁנָא לְעֵיל גַּבֵּי הַתּוֹרֵם זֵיתִים עַל הַשֶּׁמֶן וַעֲנָבִים עַל הַיַּיִן דְּאֵין תְּרוּמָתוֹ תְרוּמָה, וְיֵשׁ לוֹמַר דְּהַתּוֹרֵם זֵיתִים עַל הַשֶּׁמֶן אִיכָּא פְּסֵידָא לַכֹּהֵן, לְפִיכָךְ אֵין תְּרוּמָתוֹ תְרוּמָה, אֲבָל בְּתוֹרֵם שֶׁמֶן עַל הַזֵּיתִים הַנִּכְתָּשִׁים דְּלֵיכָּא פְּסֵידָא לַכֹּהֵן תְּרוּמָתוֹ תְרוּמָה, אֶלָּא דְּרַבָּנָן הוּא דְּגָזוּר דְּיַחֲזֹר וְיִתְרֹם:


אבל לא שניה – because it is not other than a Rabbinic ordinance; therefore it is not eaten until priest’s due and tithes are separated from it from another placer, since from the Torah, it is completely produce that cannot be eaten until Levitical and Priestly gifts have been separated, and if you ask – what is the difference here when one separates priest’s due from [olive] oil instead of from pounded olives, that his separated priest’s due is priest’s due, and then separates priest’s due again and what is the difference above concerning one who separates priest’s due olives instead of [olive] oil and grapes instead of wine where his separation of priest’s due is not priest’s due? And one can say that regarding a person who separates priest due from olives instead of [olive] oil, where there is a loss to the Kohen, his separating priest’s due is priest’s due/Terumah, but that the Rabbis are they who decreed that he should go back and separate priest’s due once again.



### Mishnah 9

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 1:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/1:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/1:9:1)


**זֵיתִים הַנִּכְבָּשִׁים.** זֵיתִים מְלוּחִים וּכְבוּשִׁים בְּיַיִן אוֹ בְחֹמֶץ כְּדֵי שֶׁיִּתְקַיְּמוּ, וַחֲשִׁיבֵי דָּבָר שֶׁנִּגְמְרָה מְלַאכְתּוֹ, הִלְכָּךְ תּוֹרְמִין מִן הַשֶּׁמֶן עֲלֵיהֶם, דְּדַוְקָא שֶׁמֶן עַל זֵיתִים הָעוֹמְדִים לְהוֹצִיא שַׁמְנָן. וְכֵן יַיִן עַל עֲנָבִים הָעוֹמְדוֹת לִדָּרֵךְ הוּא דְּאָסוּר לִתְרֹם, אֲבָל יַיִן עַל צִמּוּקִים וְשֶׁמֶן עַל זֵיתִים הַנִּכְבָּשִׁים שָׁרֵי. וּכְשֶׁהוּא תּוֹרֵם מִן הַשֶּׁמֶן עַל הַזֵּיתִים הַנִּכְבָּשִׁים וּמִן הַיַּיִן עַל הַצִּמּוּקִים תּוֹרֵם לְפִי הַשֶּׁמֶן הָרָאוּי שֶׁיֵּצֵא מִן הַזֵּיתִים הַנִּכְבָּשִׁים וּלְפִי הַיַּיִן הָרָאוּי שֶׁיֵּצֵא מִן הַצִּמּוּקִים:


זיתים הנכבשים – olives that are salty or pickled in wine or vinegar in order that they would be preserved and would be consider as something where its preparation had been completed; therefore, we separate priest’s due from the [olive] oil on them, and especially, [olive] oil instead of the olives which are about to have their oil removed from them, and similar wine in place of grapes which are about to be trodden upon – that is what is prohibited to from it the priest’s due, but wine in place of raisins, and [olive] oil in place of olives that are pickled is permitted, and when he separates the priest’s due from the [olive] - instead of the preserved olives, and from the wine instead of the raisins, he separates the priest’s due according to the appropriate oil that came out from the preserved olives and according to the appropriate wine that came out from the grapes.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 1:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/1:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/1:9:2)


**אֵין צָרִיךְ לִתְרֹם.** הוֹאִיל וְכִי קָא תָּרַם שַׁפִּיר קָא תָּרַם, שֶׁהֲרֵי בְּאוֹתָהּ שָׁעָה הָיוּ עוֹמְדוֹת לַאֲכִילָה:


אין צריך לתרום – since when he separated the priest’s due, he did so well, for at that hour, they were meant to be eaten.



### Mishnah 10

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 1:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/1:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/1:10:1)


**אֵין תּוֹרְמִים מִדָּבָר שֶׁנִּגְמְרָה מְלַאכְתּוֹ.** מִדָּבָר שֶׁנִּתְמָרַח בַּכְּרִי:


אין תורמים מדבר שנגמרה מלאכתו – from something that was shaped into an even pile.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 1:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/1:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/1:10:2)


**עַל דָּבָר שֶׁלֹּא נִגְמְרָה מְלַאכְתּוֹ.** שֶׁלֹּא נִתְמָרַח בַּכְּרִי, דִּכְתִיב (במדבר יח) כַּדָּגָן מִן הַגֹּרֶן וְכַמְלֵאָה מִן הַיָּקֶב, מִן הַגָּמוּר עַל הַגָּמוּר, וְלֹא מִן הַגָּמוּר עַל שֶׁאֵינוֹ גָמוּר, וְלֹא מִשֶּׁאֵינוֹ גָמוּר עַל הַגָּמוּר:


על דבר שלא נגמרה מלאכתו – that was not shaped into an even pile, as it is written (Numbers 18:27): “As with the new grain from the threshing floor or from the flow from the vat,” that whose preparation is completed with that which is completed and not from that whose preparation has been completed in place of that which is completed and not from what is not completed in place of what is completed.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 1:10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/1:10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/1:10:3)


**וְאִם תָּרַם תְּרוּמָתוֹ תְרוּמָה.** וְדַוְקָא בִּשְׁאָר פֵּרוֹת, אֲבָל הַתּוֹרֵם מִן הַזֵּיתִים עַל הַשֶּׁמֶן, אוֹ מִן הָעֲנָבִים עַל הַיַּיִן, הָא אָמְרִינַן לְעֵיל דְּאֵין תְּרוּמָתוֹ תְּרוּמָה מִשּׁוּם דְּאִית בֵּיהּ פְּסֵידָא לַכֹּהֵן כִּדְאָמְרָן:


ואם תרם תרומתו תרומה – and specifically for the rest of the produce, but when one donates for priest’s due from olives in place of olive oil or from grapes in place of wine, that which we said above (Mishnah 4), that his donation of priest’s due is not a valid gift, because there is a loss to the Kohen as we have said.



## Chapter 2


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 2:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/2:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/2:1:1)


**אֵין תּוֹרְמִין מִטָּהוֹר עַל הַטָּמֵא.** גְּזֵרָה שֶׁמָּא יִתְרֹם שֶׁלֹּא מִן הַמֻּקָּף מִשּׁוּם דְּמִסְתְּפֵי שֶׁמָּא יִגַּע הַטָּמֵא בַּטָּהוֹר וִיטַמְּאֶנּוּ, וַאֲנַן בָּעִינַן שֶׁהַתּוֹרֵם יִתְרֹם מִן הַמֻּקָּף:


אין תורמין מטהור על הטמא – as a decree lest he separate the heave offering that is not brought near because they are afraid lest the impure [produce] will come in contact with the pure and defile it. But we require that the person who separates the heave offering separates from that which is brought near.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 2:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/2:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/2:1:2)


**בֶּאֱמֶת אָמְרוּ.** כָּל הֵיכָא דְּתָנִינַן בֶּאֱמֶת אָמְרוּ כְּאִלּוּ הִיא הֲלָכָה לְמֹשֶׁה מִסִּינַי, אֲבָל לָאו דַּוְקָא הֲלָכָה לְמֹשֶׁה מִסִּינַי, שֶׁהֲרֵי בְּפֶרֶק קַמָּא דְשַׁבָּת שָׁנִינוּ בֶּאֱמֶת אָמְרוּ הַחַזָּן רוֹאֶה הֵיכָן הַתִּינוֹקוֹת קוֹרִין, וּמִדְּרַבָּנָן הִיא:


באמת אמרו – wherever it is taught: "באמת אמרו" /”in fact that they said that,” it is as if it is a usage dating from Moses as delivered from Sinai (i.e., a traditional interpretation of a written law), but not exactly a usage dating from Moses as delivered from Sinai, for in the first chapter of [Tractate] Shabbat [Mishnah 3], we taught: “in fact that they said that the teacher sees [by the light of a lamp] where the children are reading,” and this is from the Rabbis (as opposed to from the Bible).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 2:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/2:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/2:1:3)


**הָעִגּוּל שֶׁל דְּבֵלָה.** אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהַדְּבֵלוֹת מְדֻבָּקוֹת זוֹ בָזוֹ בְּתוֹךְ הָעִגּוּל אֵינָן חֲשׁוּבוֹת חִבּוּר לְעִנְיַן טֻמְאָה, וְאִם נָגְעָה טֻמְאָה בְּאַחַת מִן הַדְּבֵלוֹת לֹא נִטְמֵאת הָאַחֶרֶת:


העגול של דבילה – even though the cakes of pressed figs are glued to each other in the midst of the cake of pressed figs, they are not thought of as attached regarding ritual defilement, and if defilement touched one of the cakes of pressed figs, the other is not ritually defiled.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 2:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/2:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/2:1:4)


**וְכֵן אֲגֻדָּה שֶׁל יָרָק וְכֵן עֲרֵמָה.** צְרִיכֵי, דְּאִי אַשְׁמְעִינַן עִגּוּל הֲוָה אֲמֵינָא עִגּוּל שֶׁכֻּלּוֹ גוּף אֶחָד תּוֹרֵם, אֲגֻדָּה שֶׁאֵינָהּ גּוּף אֶחָד אֵינוֹ תוֹרֵם. וְאִי אַשְׁמְעִינַן אֲגֻדָּה הֲוָה אֲמֵינָא אֲגֻדָּה שֶׁהַכֹּל תְּפוּסָה אַחַת תּוֹרֵם, עֲרֵמָה שֶׁאֵינָהּ תְּפוּסָה אַחַת אֵינוֹ תוֹרֵם, צְרִיכֵי:


וכן אגודה של יקרק וכן ערימה – it is necessary [to mention both], for if it (i.e., the Mishnah) had taught [only] the circular cake of pressed figs, I would have said that the cake of pressed figs which is one substance, he separates the heave-offering, [but] the bunch [of greens] which is not one substance, he does not separate the heave-offering. But if it (i.e., the Mishnah) had taught [only] the bunch [of greens], I would have said, the bunch [of greens] where it is all one possession, he separates the heave-offering, [but] a pile is not one possession, which he doesn’t separate the heave-offering, hence, they are all necessary.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 2:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/2:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/2:1:5)


**רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר אוֹמֵר תּוֹרְמִים.** לְכַתְּחִלָּה מִן הַטָּהוֹר עַל הַטָּמֵא, דְּלֹא גָזַר רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר שֶׁמָּא יָבֹא לִתְרֹם שֶׁלֹּא מִן הַמֻּקָּף. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר:


רבי אליעזר אומר תורמים – ab initio from that which is ritually pure for that which is ritually defiled, for Rabbi Eliezer did not decree lest he come to separate the heave-offering from that which is not brought near , but the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Eliezer.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 2:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/2:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/2:2:1)


**אֵין תּוֹרְמִין מִן הַטָּמֵא עַל הַטָּהוֹר.** מִשּׁוּם פְּסֵידָא דְכֹהֵן:


אין תורמין מן הטמא על הטהור – because of the loss to the Kohen [because ritually impure heave-offering is forbidden to the Kohen].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 2:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/2:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/2:2:2)


**וְאִם תָּרַם שׁוֹגֵג תְּרוּמָתוֹ תְּרוּמָה.** וְדַוְקָא שֶׁהָיְתָה לָהּ שְׁעַת הַכֹּשֶׁר וְהִגִּיעָה לְעוֹנַת מַעַשְׂרוֹת קֹדֶם שֶׁנִּטְמְאָה, דְּמִדְּאוֹרַיְתָא תְּרוּמָה מַעַלְיָתָא הִיא, אֲבָל אִם נִטְמֵאת קֹדֶם שֶׁהִגִּיעַ לְעוֹנַת מַעַשְׂרוֹת לֹא הַוְיָא תְּרוּמָה מִדְּאוֹרַיְתָא, וְאִם תָּרַם אֲפִלּוּ שׁוֹגֵג אֵין תְּרוּמָתוֹ תְרוּמָה:


ואם תרם שוגג תרומתו תרומה – and exactly when it had been fit for use and the season for separating tithes had arrived prior to it becoming ritually impure, because according to the Torah It is perfect heave-offering, but if it he set aside heave-offering, even in advertently, his heave-offering is not a heave-offering.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 2:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/2:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/2:2:3)


**מַעֲשֵׂר טֶבֶל.** מַעֲשֵׂר רִאשׁוֹן שֶׁלֹּא נִטְּלָה מִמֶּנּוּ תְּרוּמַת מַעֲשֵׂר:


מעשר טבל – First Tithe that had not had the Terumah of the tithe taken from it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 2:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/2:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/2:2:4)


**הָיָה מַפְרִישׁ עָלָיו וְהוֹלֵךְ.** הָיָה מַפְרִישׁ מִזֶּה עַל שְׁאָר מַעַשְׂרוֹתָיו:


היה מפריש עליו והולך – he would separate from this on the rest of the tithes.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 2:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/2:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/2:2:5)


**אִם הָיָה יוֹדֵעַ בּוֹ בַּתְּחִלָּה.** שֶׁהוּא טֶבֶל אוֹ שֶׁהוּא טָמֵא, אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁבְּשָׁעָה שֶׁתָּרַם הָיָה שׁוֹגֵג, שֶׁשָּׁכַח שֶׁהוּא טֶבֶל אוֹ שֶׁהוּא טָמֵא, הֲוֵי שׁוֹגֵג קָרוֹב לְמֵזִיד, וְלֹא עָשָׂה וְלֹא כְלוּם. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 2:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/2:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/2:3:1)


**הַמַּטְבִּיל כֵּלִים בְּשַׁבָּת.** שֶׁאָסוּר לְהַטְבִּיל כֵּלִים טְמֵאִים לְהַעֲלוֹתָם מִידֵי טֻמְאָתָן בְּשַׁבָּת, מִפְּנֵי שֶׁנִּרְאֶה כִּמְתַקֵּן כְּלִי:


אם היה יודע בתחלה – that it is eatables forbidden pending the separation of sacred gifts or it is ritually impure, even though that at the time that he separated the heave-offering, it was inadvertent, for he forgot that it is forbidden pending the separation of sacred gifts, or that it is ritually impure, it is inadvertently that is close to willfully committed, and he did not do anything. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehuda.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 2:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/2:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/2:3:2)


**הַמְּעַשֵּׂר.** שֶׁאָסוּר לְהַפְרִישׁ תְּרוּמוֹת וּמַעַשְׂרוֹת בְּשַׁבָּת, מִפְּנֵי שֶׁהוּא מְתַקֵּן:


המטביל כלים בשבת – that it is forbidden to immerse ritually impure utensils, and to raise them from their ritual impurity on the Sabbath because it appears like repairing a utensil.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 2:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/2:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/2:3:3)


**וְהַמְבַשֵּׁל בַּשַּׁבָּת.** בְּשׁוֹגֵג, יֹאכַל אוֹתוֹ תַבְשִׁיל לְמוֹצָאֵי שַׁבָּת, אֲבָל לֹא בְּשַׁבָּת עַצְמוֹ. וּבְמֵזִיד לֹא יֹאכַל הוּא, אֲבָל אֲחֵרִים אוֹכְלִים לְמוֹצָאֵי שַׁבָּת, דִּכְתִיב (שמות לא) וּשְׁמַרְתֶּם אֶת הַשַּׁבָּת כִּי קֹדֶשׁ הִיא, הִיא קֹדֶשׁ וְאֵין מַעֲשֶׂיהָ קֹדֶשׁ:


והמבשל בשבת – [and he who cooks on the Sabbath] inadvertently , he can eat that cooked dish on Saturday night (i.e., after the conclusion of the Sabbath), but not on the Sabbath itself. But [if he cooked it] willfully, he may not eat of it, but others may eat consume it on Saturday night, as it is written (Exodus 31:14): “You shall keep the sabbath, for it is holy;” it is holy, but its actions are not holy.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 2:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/2:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/2:3:4)


**וּבַשְּׁבִיעִית בֵּין שׁוֹגֵג בֵּין מֵזִיד יַעֲקֹר.** שֶׁנֶּחְשְׁדוּ יִשְׂרָאֵל עַל הַשְּׁבִיעִית וְלֹא נֶחְשְׁדוּ עַל הַשַּׁבָּת:


ובשביעית בין שוגג בין מזיד יעקר – that Israelites were suspected about [observance] of the Seventh year, but are not suspected regarding the Sabbath.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 2:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/2:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/2:4:1)


**כָּל מִין חִטִּים אֶחָד.** וְאַף עַל גַּב דְּאִיכָּא שְׁחַמְתִּית וּלְבָנָה:


כל מין חטים אחד – and even though there is reddish, dark-colored wheat and white-colored [wheat].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 2:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/2:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/2:4:2)


**וְכָל מִין תְּאֵנִים אֶחָד.** וְאַף עַל גַּב דְּאִיכָּא שְׁחוֹרוֹת וּלְבָנוֹת. תְּאֵנִים בְּעוֹדָן לַחִים קְרוּיִין תְּאֵנִים, וּכְשֶׁיָּבְשׁוּ קְרוּיִין גְּרוֹגָרוֹת, וּכְשֶׁנִּדְרָסִין בָּעִגּוּל קְרוּיִין עִגּוּל דְּבֵלָה:


וכל מין תאנים אחד – and even though there are black [figs] and white [figs], figs while they are yet moist are called תאנים/figs, and when they have dried, they are called גרוגרות/dried figs, and when they are trodden in circular cake, they are called עגול דבלה/cake of pressed figs.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 2:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/2:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/2:4:3)


**תּוֹרֵם מִן הַיָּפֶה.** מִן הַתְּאֵנִים עַל הַגְּרוֹגָרוֹת, דִּתְאֵנִים יָפִים מִן הַגְּרוֹגָרוֹת:


תורם מן היפה – from the figs on the dried figs, for figs are choicest than dried figs (see also Tractate Bikkurim, Chapter 3, Mishnah 3).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 2:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/2:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/2:4:4)


**תּוֹרֵם מִן הַמִּתְקַיֵּם.** מִן הַגְּרוֹגָרוֹת עַל הַתְּאֵנִים, דִּגְרוֹגָרוֹת מִתְקַיְּמֵי טְפֵי:


תורם מן המתקיים – from the dried figs on the figs, for dried figs endure more.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 2:4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/2:4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/2:4:5)


**לְעוֹלָם הוּא תוֹרֵם מִן הַיָּפֶה.** דִּכְתִיב (במדבר יח) בַּהֲרִימְכֶם אֶת חֶלְבּוֹ, לְעוֹלָם בָּעִינַן חֶלְבּוֹ, וְאִי פָּסֵיד, יִפָּסֵד. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


לעולם הוא תורם מן היפה – as it is written (Numbers 18:30): “When you have removed the best part from it [you Levites may consider it the same as the yield of threshing floor or vat],” we always require the best/choicest part, and if he (i.e., the Kohen) loses, he loses. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehuda.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 2:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/2:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/2:5:1)


**תּוֹרְמִים בָּצָל קָטָן שָׁלֵם.** לְפִי שֶׁהוּא מִתְקַיֵּם:


תורמין בצל קטן שלם – because it endures.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 2:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/2:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/2:5:2)


**וְלֹא חֲצִי בָצָל גָּדוֹל.** אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהוּא יָפֶה מִמֶּנּוּ:


ולא חצי בצל גדול – even though it is nicer than it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 2:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/2:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/2:5:3)


**רַבִּי יְהוּדָה אוֹמֵר לֹא כִי.** רַבִּי יְהוּדָה לְטַעֲמֵיהּ דְּאָמַר לְעוֹלָם הוּא תּוֹרֵם מִן הַיָּפֶה:


רבי יהודה אומר לא כי – Rabbi Yehuda, according to his reasoning, who stated that he separates heave-offering from the choicest [produce].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 2:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/2:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/2:5:4)


**מִבְּנֵי הַמְּדִינָה.** טוֹבִים לַאֲכִילָה מִן הַכּוּפְרִים הַבְּצָלִים שֶׁל בְּנֵי הַכְּפָרִים, אֲבָל שֶׁל בְּנֵי הַכְּפָרִים מִתְקַיְּמִים יוֹתֵר:


מבני המדינה – it is good for eating from those of the villages, the onions of the villages, but that of the villages is preserved better.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 2:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/2:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/2:5:5)


**מִפְּנֵי שֶׁהוּא מַאֲכַל פּוֹלִיטִיקִין.** בְּצָלִים שֶׁל בְּנֵי הַמְּדִינָה הוּא מַאֲכָל לַאֲנָשִׁים חֲשׁוּבִים, וְהֵם יָפִים יוֹתֵר מֵאוֹתָן שֶׁל בְּנֵי הַכְּפָרִים. פּוֹלִיטִיקִין, בְּנֵי פַּלְטְרִין שֶׁל מְלָכִים:


מפני שהא מאכל פולטיקין – onions of the people of the city people which is the food for important people, and they are better/choicest produce than those of the villagers. פולטיקין/city people, the members of the residence of kings.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 2:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/2:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/2:6:1)


**תּוֹרְמִין זֵיתֵי שֶׁמֶן.** הָעוֹמְדִים לְהוֹצִיא שַׁמְנָן:


תורמין זיתי שמן – that stand to remove their oil.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 2:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/2:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/2:6:2)


**עַל זֵיתֵי כֶבֶשׁ.** זֵיתִים שֶׁאֵין עוֹשִׂים שֶׁמֶן, וְכוֹבְשִׁים אוֹתָן בְּיַיִן אוֹ בְחֹמֶץ לְקַיֵּם אוֹתָן לַאֲכִילָה. וְזֵיתֵי שֶׁמֶן יָפִין מִזֵּיתֵי כֶבֶשׁ, הִלְכָּךְ מַפְרִישִׁים מִזֵּיתֵי שֶׁמֶן עַל זֵיתֵי כֶבֶשׁ דַּהֲוָה לֵיהּ מִן הַיָּפֶה עַל הָרַע:


על זיתי כבש – olives that don’t produce oil and they press them with wine or with vinegar to preserve them for eating. But olives for oil are better than olives for pressing, for it should be from the nice ones on the bad ones.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 2:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/2:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/2:6:3)


**וְיַיִן שֶׁאֵינוֹ מְבֻשָּׁל.** יָפֶה לִשְׁתִיָּה מִן הַמְבֻשָּׁל:


ויין שאינו מבושל – good for drinking from that which is boiled.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 2:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/2:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/2:6:4)


**כָּל שֶׁהוּא כִלְאַיִם בַּחֲבֵרוֹ לֹא יִתְרֹם מִזֶּה לָזֶה.** דְּאָמַר קְרָא (במדבר יח) כָּל חֵלֶב יִצְהָר וְכָל חֵלֶב תִּירוֹשׁ וְדָגָן, אָמְרָה תּוֹרָה, תֵּן חֵלֶב לָזֶה וְחֵלֶב לָזֶה:


כל שהוא כלאים בחבירו לא יתרום מזה לזה – as Scripture states (Numbers 18:12): “All the best of the new oil, wine and grain [the choice parts that they present to the LORD – I give to you],” the Torah stated: give oil to this one and oil to that one.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 2:6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/2:6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/2:6:5)


**חוּץ מִן הַזּוּנִין.** בַּעֲרָבִי זוא"ן וּבְלַעַ"ז ויצ"א, וְאֵינוֹ אֹכֶל אָדָם וּמְקַיְּמִים אוֹתוֹ לַיּוֹנִים:


חוץ מן הזונין – in Arabic ZOAN, and in the foreign language, VITZAH, and it is not the food for humans but they keep it for doves (see also Tractate Kilayim, Chapter 1, Mishnah 1).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 2:6:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/2:6:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/2:6:6)


**הַקִּשּׁוּת.** בַּעֲרָבִי פאקו"ס:


הקישות – in Arabic, FAKUS.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 2:6:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/2:6:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/2:6:7)


**וְהַמְּלָפְפוֹן.** בַּעֲרָבִי כייא"ר:


והמלפפון – in Arabic, KIYYAR.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 2:6:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/2:6:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/2:6:8)


**מִין אֶחָד.** וְתוֹרְמִין מִזֶּה עַל זֶה:


מין אחר – and the separate the heave offering from this one on that one.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 2:6:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/2:6:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/2:6:9)


**שְׁנֵי מִינִים.** וְאֵין תּוֹרְמִין מִזֶּה עַל זֶה, וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


שני מינין – but we do not separate the heave-offering from that one for this one, but the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehuda.



## Chapter 3


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 3:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/3:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/3:1:1)


**הַתּוֹרֵם וְכוּ'.** בְּשׁוֹגֵג אַיְרֵי, וַאֲפִלּוּ הָכִי יַחֲזֹר וְיִתְרֹם, דַּהֲוֵי קָרוֹב לְמֵזִיד, מִשּׁוּם דַּהֲוָה לֵיהּ [לְמִטְעַמֵּיהּ]:


התורם וכו' - it is speaking about inadvertently, for nevertheless, it is close to be willfull, because that he should have [tasted it].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 3:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/3:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/3:1:2)


**וְנִמְצֵאת שֶׁל חֹמֶץ כוּ'.** שֶׁהַתּוֹרֵם מִן הַחֹמֶץ עַל הַיַּיִן אֵין תְּרוּמָתוֹ תְרוּמָה, דְּיַיִן וְחֹמֶץ שְׁנֵי מִינִים הֵן:


ונמצאת של חומץ כו' – for a person who separates out heaveoffering from the vinegar on the wine, his heave-offering is not a heave-offering, for wine and vinegar are two [different] species.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 3:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/3:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/3:1:3)


**אִם סָפֵק.** עַד שֶׁלֹּא תְרָמָהּ הֶחֱמִיצָה אוֹ לְאַחַר שֶׁתְּרָמָהּ הֶחֱמִיצָה:


אם ספק – prior to his separating out the heave-offering it had soured or after he separated out the heave offering, it soured.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 3:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/3:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/3:1:4)


**תְּרוּמָה וְיַחֲזֹר וְיִתְרֹם.** וְנוֹתֵן שְׁתֵּיהֶן לַכֹּהֵן, וְכֹהֵן נוֹתֵן לוֹ דְּמֵי הָרִאשׁוֹנָה שֶׁהִיא גְדוֹלָה מִן הַשְּׁנִיָּה, שֶׁהַשְּׁנִיָּה מִתְמַעֶטֶת כְּפִי מַה שֶּׁעוֹלָה תְּרוּמַת הָרִאשׁוֹנָה, וּלְפִי שֶׁהַכֹּהֵן הוּא הַמּוֹצִיא מֵחֲבֵרוֹ וַאֲנַן קַיְמָא לָן בְּכָל דּוּכְתָּא הַמּוֹצִיא מֵחֲבֵרוֹ עָלָיו הָרְאָיָה, לְפִיכָךְ אֵינוֹ לוֹקֵחַ בְּלֹא דָמִים אֶלָּא הַקְּטַנָּה, וְצָרִיךְ שֶׁיִּתֵּן לוֹ דְּמֵי הַגְּדוֹלָה שֶׁהִיא הָרִאשׁוֹנָה:


תרומה ויחזור ויתרום – and he gives both of them to the Kohen, and the Kohen gives him the monetary value of the firs which islarger than the second, and second’s [value] is reduced according to what the heave-offering of the first costs, and since the Kohen makes a claim against his colleague, and we hold that in every place that “the claimant must produce evidence”/ "המוציא מחבירו עליו הראיה"(see Tractate Bava Metzia, Chapter 3, Mishnah 11), therefore, he does not acquire without money but the smaller/lesser object, and he must give him the monetary value of the larger/more important object which is the first.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 3:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/3:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/3:1:5)


**הָרִאשׁוֹנָה אֵינָהּ מְדַמַּעַת בִּפְנֵי עַצְמָהּ.** אִם נָפְלָה לְפָחוֹת מִמֵּאָה שֶׁל חֻלִּין אֵין נַעֲשֵׂית מְדֻמָּע, דְּשֶׁמָּא אֵינָהּ תְּרוּמָה אֶלָּא הַשְּׁנִיָּה הִיא תְרוּמָה, וְכֵן הַשְּׁנִיָּה אֵינָהּ מְדַמַּעַת בִּפְנֵי עַצְמָהּ, שֶׁמָּא הָרִאשׁוֹנָה תְּרוּמָה וְלֹא הַשְּׁנִיָּה:


הראשונה אינה מדמעת בפני עצמה – if less than one-hundred of non-sacred produce had fallen, something otherwise exempt, does not become subject to the law of Terumah, to mix non-sacred grain/wine/oil with Terumah in proportions to make the whole prohibited to non-priests, lest it is not Terumah but rather the second one is Terumah, and similarly, the second part does not become a mixture of sacred and non-sacred produce on its own, lest the firs part was Terumah and not the second.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 3:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/3:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/3:1:6)


**וְאֵין חַיָּבִים עָלֶיהָ חֹמֶשׁ.** זָר שֶׁאָכַל הָרִאשׁוֹנָה לְבַדָּהּ אוֹ הַשְּׁנִיָּה לְבַדָּהּ אֵינוֹ מְשַׁלֵּם חֹמֶשׁ כְּדִין שְׁאָר אוֹכְלֵי תְּרוּמָה בִּשְׁגָגָה:


ואין חייבים עליה חומש – a foreigner (i.e., non-Kohen) who ate the first [batch] (which may have doubtfully been Terumah since it may have fermented) alone or the second [batch] alone, he does not pay the one-fifth penalty like the law of others who consume heave-offering inadvertently.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 3:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/3:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/3:2:1)


**מְדַמְּעוֹת כַּקְּטַנָּה שֶׁבִּשְׁתֵּיהֶן.** כְּשִׁעוּר הַשְּׁנִיָּה שֶׁהִיא קְטַנָּה שֶׁבִּשְׁתֵּיהֶן, וְאִם יֵשׁ בַּחֻלִּין מֵאָה כְּנֶגֶד הַקְּטַנָּה אֵין נַעֲשִׂים מְדֻמָּע, אֶלָּא נוֹטֵל מִן הַחֻלִּין כְּשִׁעוּר אֵלּוּ הַשְּׁתֵּי תְרוּמוֹת שֶׁנָּפְלוּ בָהֶן וְנוֹתְנָן לַכֹּהֵן, וְהַשְּׁאָר חֻלִּין כְּמוֹ שֶׁהָיוּ:


מדמעות בקטנה שבשתיהן – like the measure of the second [batch] which is the smaller of the two of them, and if here is in the non-sacred produce one hundred [parts] corresponding to the smaller [batch], it does not impose the status of heave-offering , but one takes from the non-sacred produce like the measure of those two heave-offerings that fell in them and he gives them to the Kohen and the rest of it is non-sacred produce as they were.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 3:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/3:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/3:3:1)


**תְּרוּמַת שְׁנֵיהֶם תְּרוּמָה.** הַיְנוּ דִתְרוּמַת כָּל אֶחָד מֵהֶן חֶצְיָהּ תְּרוּמָה וְחֶצְיָהּ חֻלִּין, נִמְצָא כְּשֶׁתָּרְמוּ שְׁנֵיהֶם מֵחֲמִשִּׁים סְאִין זֶה סְאָה וְזֶה סְאָה, שֶׁלֹּא קָדְשָׁה תְּרוּמָה כִּי אִם חֲצִי סְאָה בְּשֶׁל זֶה וַחֲצִי סְאָה בְּשֶׁל זֶה, כָּל אֶחָד כְּפִי חֶלְקוֹ:


תרומת שניהם תרומה – that is the heave-offering of each of them is one-half heave-offering and one-half non-sacred produce, it is found that twhen both of them separated heave offering, from fifty Seah, that this one [set aside] one Seah and that individual one Seah, that the heave-offering was not sanctified other than one-half Seah belonging to this one and one-half Seah belonging to that one, each one of them according to his portion.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 3:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/3:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/3:3:2)


**אִם תָּרַם הָרִאשׁוֹן כַּשִּׁעוּר.** אֶחָד מֵחֲמִשִּׁים, כַּשִּׁעוּר שֶׁנָּתְנוּ חֲכָמִים, תְּרוּמָתוֹ תְרוּמָה. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי, דִּלְפֵרוּשֵׁי מִלְתַיְהוּ דַחֲכָמִים קָאָתֵי. וְאִית דִּמְפָרְשֵׁי אִם תָּרַם הָרִאשׁוֹן כְּשִׁעוּר מַה שֶּׁתָּרַם חֲבֵרוֹ אַחַר כָּךְ תְּרוּמָתוֹ תְרוּמָה, דַּחֲזִינַן דַּהֲוָה נִיחָא לֵיהּ לְהַאִיךְ בַּתְרָאָה בְּמַה שֶּׁתָּרַם הָרִאשׁוֹן, שֶׁהֲרֵי הָיְתָה דַעְתּוֹ מַסְכֶּמֶת עִמּוֹ בְּשִׁעוּר הַתְּרוּמָה. וּפֵרוּשׁ רִאשׁוֹן נִרְאָה בְּעֵינַי עִקָּר:


אם תרם הראשון כשעור – one from fifty – according to the measure that the Sages gave, his heave-offering is a heave-offering. And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yosi (see Tractate Terumot, Chapter 4, Mishnah 3) , for he comes to explain the words of the Sages. But there are those who explain that if the first [person] separated according to the measure that his colleague separated [for heave-offering] afterwards, his heave-offering is a heave-offering, as we see that it was easy for him for that latter one with what the first one set aside as a heave-offering , for it was his intention agrees with him, in the measure of the heave-offering. But, the first explanation appears in my eyes to be essential.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 3:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/3:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/3:4:1)


**בַּמֶּה דְבָרִים אֲמוּרִים.** הָא דְפָלֵיג רַבִּי עֲקִיבָא לְעֵיל וְאָמַר תְּרוּמַת שְׁנֵיהֶם תְּרוּמָה:


במה דברים אמורים – that which Rabbi disputes above and states that the heave-offering of both is Terumah/heave-offering.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 3:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/3:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/3:4:2)


**בְּשֶׁלֹּא דִבֵּר.** שֶׁלֹּא נָטַל רְשׁוּת מֵחֲבֵרוֹ לִתְרֹם אֶלָּא תָּרַם מִדַּעְתּוֹ:


בשלא דבר – that this one did not receive permission from his colleague to separate the heave-offering, but rather, he separated the heave-offering of his own accord.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 3:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/3:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/3:4:3)


**אֲבָל הִרְשָׁה.** שֶׁנָּתַן בַּעַל הַבַּיִת רְשׁוּת לִתְרֹם, תְּרוּמָתוֹ תְרוּמָה:


אבל הרשה – that the owner gave permission [to his colleague] to separate the heave-offering, his heave-offering is a heave-offering.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 3:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/3:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/3:4:4)


**בִּטֵּל.** אִם לְאַחַר שֶׁמִּנָּה שָׁלִיחַ לִתְרֹם בִּטֵּל שְׁלִיחוּתוֹ לְאַחַר שֶׁהָלַךְ הַשָּׁלִיחַ מִלְּפָנָיו:


בטל – if afer he had appointed an agent to separate the heave-offering, he cancelled his agency after the agent went from before him.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 3:4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/3:4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/3:4:5)


**חוּץ.** בְּעָלִים עַמֵּי הָאָרֶץ שֶׁהִשְׂכִּירוּ פּוֹעֲלִים חֲבֵרִים כְּדֵי לְהַפְרִישׁ תְּרוּמָה בְּטָהֳרָה, רַשָּׁאִים לִתְרֹם עַד שֶׁלֹּא יִגְּעוּ בוֹ הַבְּעָלִים עַמֵּי הָאָרֶץ וִיטַמְּאוּ אֶת הַתְּרוּמָה, מִפְּנֵי שֶׁהֵן מְטַמְּאִים אֶת הַגַּת, שֶׁהַבְּעָלִים עַמֵּי הָאָרֶץ מִיָּד כְּשֶׁהִתְחִילוּ הַדּוֹרְכִים לִדְרֹךְ וְהָלְכוּ בַגַּת שְׁתִי וָעֵרֶב אֵינָם נִזְהָרִים יוֹתֵר מִלִּגַּע בְּגִתָּן וּמְטַמְּאִים אוֹתָהּ, לְפִי שֶׁהֵם חוֹשְׁבִים שֶׁמִּיָּד כְּשֶׁהִתְחִילוּ לִדְרֹךְ הִפְרִישׁוּ הַחֲבֵרִים תְּרוּמָה גְדוֹלָה. הִלְכָּךְ מַפְרִישִׁים הַחֲבֵרִים הַדּוֹרְכִים מִן הַיַּיִן כְּדֵי שִׁעוּר תְּרוּמָה הַצְּרִיכָה לְכָל הַגַּת מִיָּד כְּשֶׁהָלְכוּ בָהּ שְׁתִי וָעֵרֶב, שֶׁאִם יָבֹאוּ בְּעָלִים וִיטַמְּאוּ הַגַּת קֹדֶם שֶׁיִּגְמְרוּ יֵשׁ לָהֶן יַיִן מְזֻמָּן לְהַפְרִישׁ עַל הַכֹּל בְּטָהֳרָה:


חוץ – the owners who are illiterate who hired workers who are members of the order for the observance of Levitical laws in daily intercourse in order to separate the heave-offering in ritual purity, are permitted to separate the heave offering until the illiterate owners arrived who would defile the heave-offering, because they ritually defile the vat for wine pressing, for the illiterate owners immediately when the when those who tread began to stamp and they went to the vat for wine pressing in the longitudinal and latitudinal directions are not more careful from coming into contact in their vas for wine-pressing and defile it, because they think that immediately when they begin to tread, their colleagues had separated out the Great Terumah/heave-offering (i.e., two percent for the Kohen). Therefore, they separate the members of the order for the observance of Levitical laws in daily intercourse that stamp from the wine in order [to reach] the measure of heave-offering that is necessary for the vat for wine-pressing immediately when they walked in it longitudinally and latitudinally. If the owners would come and defile the vat for wine pressing prior to their completing it, they have designated wine to separate everything in ritual purity.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 3:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/3:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/3:5:1)


**קָרָא שֵׁם.** וְצָרִיךְ לִתְרֹם וּלְעַשֵּׂר מִתּוֹכוֹ, וְאֵינוֹ יָכוֹל לְהַפְרִישׁ עָלָיו מִמָּקוֹם אַחֵר. וְאַף עַל גַּב דְּרַחְמָנָא קַרְיֵהּ לִתְרוּמָה רֵאשִׁית וּבָעִינַן שֶׁיִּהְיוּ שְׁיָרֶיהָ נִכָּרִים, כֵּיוָן דְּאָמַר בְּתוֹכוֹ, מַשְׁמַע בְּאֶמְצַע הַכְּרִי תְּהֵא הַתְּרוּמָה, וְהָא אִיכָּא סְבִיבָיו שֶׁהֵם שְׁיָרֶיהָ נִכָּרִים:


קרא שם – and he must separate the heave-offering and to tithe from within it., but he is not able to separate [tithes] from another place. And even though the All-Merciful called the Terumah/heave offering "ראשית"/”the first” and we require that its remnants are recognized, for since he said, “within it,” it implies, in the middle of the pile will be the heave-offering, but there is surrounding it which is its remnants that are recognized.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 3:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/3:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/3:5:2)


**עַד שֶׁיֹּאמַר בִּצְפוֹנוֹ אוֹ בִדְרוֹמוֹ.** אַף עַל גַּב דִּבְתוֹכוֹ מַשְׁמַע בְּאֶמְצָעִיתוֹ, לֹא הֲוֵי מָקוֹם מְסֻיָּם, וַהֲרֵי הוּא כְּאִלּוּ לֹא אָמַר כְּלוּם עַד שֶׁיֹּאמַר בִּצְפוֹנוֹ אוֹ בִדְרוֹמוֹ. וְכֵן הֲלָכָה:


עד שיאמר בצפונו או בדרומו – even though “within it” implies in its middle, it is not a specific place, and it is as if he did not say anything until he would say, in its northern portion or in its southern portion, and this is the Halakha.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 3:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/3:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/3:5:3)


**תְּרוּמַת הַכְּרִי מִמֶּנּוּ עָלָיו קָרָא שֵׁם.** דְּלֹא בָעִינַן שְׁיָרֶיהָ נִכָּרִים:


תרומת הכרי ממנו עליו קרא שם – and we don’t require its remnants being recognized.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 3:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/3:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/3:5:4)


**עִשּׂוּר מַעֲשֵׂר זֶה וְכוּ'.** רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר בֶּן יַעֲקֹב פָּלֵיג אַרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן דְּאָמַר תְּרוּמַת מַעֲשֵׂר זֶה בְּתוֹךְ הַכְּרִי קָרָא שֵׁם, וְקָאָמַר דִּתְרוּמַת מַעֲשֵׂר זֶה בְּתוֹכוֹ קֹדֶם שֶׁהֻפְרַשׁ הַמַּעֲשֵׂר לֹא קָרָא שֵׁם, אֲבָל לְאַחַר שֶׁהֻפְרַשׁ הַמַּעֲשֵׂר אִם אָמַר תְּרוּמַת מַעֲשֵׂר עָלָיו קָרָא שֵׁם. וְלֵית הִלְכְתָא כְּחַד מִנַּיְהוּ אֶלָּא כַּחֲכָמִים בִּלְבָד:


עשור מעשר זה וכו' – Rabbi Eliezer ben Yaakov disputes on Rabbi Shimon who stated that this Terumah of the tithe (which the Levite to a Kohen) is within the pile that he validly designated, but after the tithe is separated, if he stated that the Terumah of the tithe is for it, he has validly designated it. But the Halakha is not like any of them, other than the Sages alone.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 3:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/3:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/3:6:1)


**שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר מְלֵאָתְךָ וְדִמְעֲךָ לֹא תְאַחֵר.** הָכִי קָאָמַר אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהוּא עוֹבֵר בְּלֹא תַעֲשֶׂה שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר מְלֵאָתְךָ וְדִמְעֲךָ לֹא תְאַחֵר, אַף עַל פִּי כֵן מַה שֶּׁעָשָׂה עָשׂוּי. מְלֵאָתְךָ אֵלּוּ הַבִּכּוּרִים שֶׁמִּתְמַלְּאִים בַּבִּכּוּר, וְדִמְעֲךָ זוֹ תְּרוּמָה, וּמַעֲשֵׂר רִאשׁוֹן נַמִּי יֵשׁ בּוֹ תְּרוּמַת מַעֲשֵׂר. לֹא תְאַחֵר, לֹא תְשַׁנֶּה הַסֵּדֶר:


שנאמר מלאתך ודמעך לא תאחר – this is what he said: he though he is transgressing a negative commandment, as it states (Exodus 22:28): “You shall not put off the skimming of the first yield of your vats,” nevertheless, what he did is done, as "מלאתך" /”your fullness,” – these are the first fruits, that fill the early seed/first-ripening, and "דמעך"/”full bloom”/”best part” – these are the heave-offerings, and First Tithe has the Terumah of the tithe (i.e., given by the Levite to the Kohen), "לא תאחר"/”do not put off,” you should not change the order.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 3:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/3:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/3:7:1)


**זֶה קָרוּי תְּרוּמָה וְרֵאשִׁית.** בִּכּוּרִים קְרוּיִין תְּרוּמָה, דְּאָמַר מַר [פְּסָחִים לח:] וּתְרוּמַת יָדְךָ (דברים יב) אֵלּוּ בִּכּוּרִים דִּכְתִיב בְּהוּ (שם כו) וְלָקַח הַכֹּהֵן הַטֶּנֶא מִיָּדֶךָ. וּקְרוּיִין רֵאשִׁית, רֵאשִׁית בִּכּוּרֵי אַדְמָתְךָ (שמות כג). תְּרוּמָה קְרוּיָה תְּרוּמָה, אֶת מִשְׁמֶרֶת תְּרוּמֹתָי (במדבר יח), וּקְרוּיָה רֵאשִׁית דְּגָנְךָ (דברים יח):


זה קרוי תרומה וראשית – "ביכורים"/”first fruits” is called "תרומה"/”heave-offering” , as the Master said [Tractate Pesahim 38b}: “or of your contributions”/"תרומת ידך" (Deuteronomy 12:17) – these are the first fruits, as it is written concerning them (Deuteronomy 26:4): “The priest shall take the basket from your hand [and set it down in front of the altar of the LORD your God.” And they are called: "ראשית"/ “choice” (Exodus 23:19): “he choice first fruits of your soil [you shall bring to the house of the LORD your God.” "תרומה"/heave-offering is called “Terumah” (Numbers 18:9) “I give you charge of My gifts,” and it is called (Deuteronomy 18:4): “[You shall also give him] the first fruits of your new grain [and wine and oil, and the first shearing of your sheep].”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 3:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/3:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/3:7:2)


**בִּכּוּרִים שֶׁהֵן בִּכּוּרִים לַכֹּל.** לְשׁוֹן בִּכּוּרִים דָּרֵישׁ, לְכָךְ קָרִינְהוּ רַחֲמָנָא בִּכּוּרִים. אִי נַמִּי מִשּׁוּם דִּכְתִיבֵי בְּפָרָשַׁת כִּי תִשָּׂא עַד שֶׁלֹּא נִצְטַוּוּ עַל הַתְּרוּמוֹת:


כבורים שהן כבורים לכל – the language of "בכורים"/”first fruits”, is expounded, therefore, the All-Merciful One calls them "בכורים"/”first fruits”; alternatively, because it is written in the Torah portion of Kee Tissa (Exodus 34:26 – “The choice first fruits of your soil you shall bring to the house of the LORD your God”_ until they had not been commanded regarding the heave-offerings/Terumot.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 3:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/3:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/3:7:3)


**שֶׁיֶּשׁ בּוֹ רֵאשִׁית.** דִּבְמַעֲשֵׂר רִאשׁוֹן יֵשׁ בּוֹ תְּרוּמַת מַעֲשֵׂר, וְהַתְּרוּמָה קְרוּיָה רֵאשִׁית, לְפִיכָךְ דִּין הוּא שֶׁיַּקְדִּים רִאשׁוֹן לַשֵּׁנִי:


שיש בו ראשית – that in the First tithe, it has in it the Terumah/heave-offering of the tithe, and the Terumah is called: "ראשית"/”first” – therefore, it is law that the First [Tithe] should come before the Second [Tithe].



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 3:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/3:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/3:8:1)


**עַד שֶׁיִּהְיוּ פִיו וְלִבּוֹ שָׁוִין.** דִּכְתִיב (שם כג) מוֹצָא שְׂפָתֶיךָ תִּשְׁמֹר, וּכְתִיב (שמות לד) כָּל נְדִיב לֵב, הָא כֵּיצַד, בָּעִינַן גּוֹמֵר בְּלִבּוֹ וּמוֹצִיא בִשְׂפָתָיו:


עד שיהיו פיו ולבו שוין – as it is written (Deuteronomy 23:24): “You must fulfill what has crossed your lips [and perform what you have voluntarily vowed to the LORD your God, having made the promise with your own mouth],” and it is written (Exodus 35:22 – the printed text listed Chapter 34, an error): [“Men and women] whose hearts moved them, [all who would gladly make an elevation of gold to the LORD],” How now? We require that he resolved in his heart and enunciating [the same thought] with his lips.



### Mishnah 9

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 3:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/3:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/3:9:1)


**הַנָּכְרִי וְהַכּוּתִי תְּרוּמָתָן תְּרוּמָה.** אִם הִפְרִישׁוּ תְּרוּמָה מִן הַתְּבוּאָה שֶׁלָּהֶן, אֲבָל אִם תָּרַם הַנָּכְרִי מִשֶּׁל יִשְׂרָאֵל בִּשְׁלִיחוּתוֹ אֵין תְּרוּמָתוֹ תְרוּמָה, כִּדְדָרְשִׁינַן מִכֵּן תָּרִימוּ גַם אַתֶּם (במדבר יח) מָה אַתֶּם בְּנֵי בְרִית אַף שְׁלוּחֲכֶם בְּנֵי בְרִית:


הנכרי והכותי תרומתן תרומה – if they separated the heave-offering from heir grain, but if the heathen separated the heave-offering from that belonging to an Israelite through his agency, his heave-offering is not a heave offering, as we expound from (Numbers 18:28): “so shall you on your part set aside a gift [for the LORD from all the tithe s that you receive from the Israelites;” just as you are children of the covenant (i.e., Israelites), so your agents are also children of the covenant.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 3:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/3:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/3:9:2)


**וַחֲכָמִים אוֹמְרִים יֶשׁ לוֹ.** וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


וחכ"א יש לו – and the Halakha is according to the Sages.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 3:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/3:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/3:9:3)


**תְּרוּמַת הַנָּכְרִי מְדַמַּעַת.** אִם נָפְלָה לְפָחוֹת מִמֵּאָה נַעֲשָׂה הַכֹּל מְדֻמָּע וְאָסוּר לְזָרִים:


תרומת הנכרי מדמעת – if their fell at least from one-hundred, everything becomes prohibited to non-priests and is considered as Terumah.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 3:9:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/3:9:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/3:9:4)


**וְחַיָּבִין עָלֶיהָ חֹמֶשׁ.** זָר שֶׁאֲכָלָהּ בִּשְׁגָגָה מְשַׁלֵּם קֶרֶן וְחֹמֶשׁ:


וחייבין עליה חומש – a foreigner (i.e., non-Kohen) who consumed it inadvertently pays the principal plus an added fifth.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 3:9:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/3:9:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/3:9:5)


**וְרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן פּוֹטֵר.** מֵחֹמֶשׁ, אֲבָל מוֹדֶה הוּא דִּמְדַמַּעַת. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן:


ור"ש פוטר – from the added firth, but he admits that non-sacred grain, wine, oil mixed with Terumah in proportions sufficient to make the who prohibited to non-priests, but the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Shimon.



## Chapter 4


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 4:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:1:1)


**הַמַּפְרִישׁ מִקְצָת תְּרוּמָה וּמַעַשְׂרוֹת.** כְּגוֹן שֶׁהָיָה לוֹ מֵאָה סְאִין טֶבֶל וְהוֹצִיא מֵהֶן סְאָה תְּרוּמָה וּבְדַעְתּוֹ לְהַפְרִישׁ עֲדַיִן הַשְּׁאָר, חוֹזֵר וּמוֹצִיא מֵאוֹתוֹ הַכְּרִי עַצְמוֹ עַד שֶׁיַּשְׁלִים שְׁנֵי סְאִין כְּשִׁעוּר הַתְּרוּמָה, דְּהַטֶּבֶל הוּא שֶׁיַּעֲלֶה בְיָדוֹ וְלֹא מַה שֶּׁכְּבָר מְתֻקָּן:


המפריש מקצת תרומה ומעשרות – as, for example, that he had one-hundred Seah of eatables forbidden pending separation of sacred gifts, and he removed from them a Seah (i.e., note that he is required to give 2% to a Kohen, not 1%), but it was his intention to yet separate the rest, he goes back and removes from the same pile itself until he completes the two Seah, like the measure of the Terumah/heave-offering. For the eatables forbidden pending separation of sacred gifts is what will be offered in his hand and not what was already made legally fit for use by giving the priestly dues.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 4:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:1:2)


**אֲבָל לֹא לְמָקוֹם אַחֵר.** אִם יֵשׁ לוֹ מֵאָה סְאִין אֲחֵרִים שֶׁל טֶבֶל אֵינוֹ יָכוֹל לְהַפְרִישׁ עֲלֵיהֶן מֵאֵלּוּ שֶׁהוֹצִיא מִקְצַת תְּרוּמָתָן, דִּלְגַבֵּי אֲחֵרִים תָּלִינַן דִּלְמָא מִן הַמְתֻקָּן הוּא מוֹצִיא עֲלֵיהֶן וְלֹא מִן הַטֶּבֶל שֶׁבּוֹ, וְרַבִּי מֵאִיר שָׁרֵי אַף לְמָקוֹם אַחֵר. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי מֵאִיר:


אבל לא למקום אחר – if he has another one-hundred Seah of eatables forbidden pending separation of sacred gifts,, he is not able to separate upon them from those which he took part of their heave-offering, for regarding the others, we rely, perhaps, from that which was already made legally fit for use by giving the priestly dues, he removes from them, and not from the eatables forbidden pending separation of sacred gifs, but Rabbi Meir permits even from another place. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Meir.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 4:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:2:1)


**בַּמְּגוּרָה.** בָּאוֹצָר, כְּדִכְתִיב (חגי ב) הַעוֹד הַזֶּרַע בַּמְּגוּרָה:


במגורה – in a storehouse, as it is written (Haggai 2:19): “While the seed is still in the granary, [and the vine, fig tree, pomegranate and olive tree have not yet borne fruit].”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 4:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:2:2)


**וְנָתַן סְאָה לְבֶן לֵוִי.** מַתְנִיתִין אַיְרֵי בְּפוֹעֵל חָבֵר הַבָּא לִסְעֹד אֵצֶל בַּעַל הַבַּיִת וְאֵינוֹ מַאֲמִינוֹ עַל הַמַּעַשְׂרוֹת, וְרָאָה הַפּוֹעֵל אֶת בַּעַל הַבַּיִת נוֹתֵן מִמְּגוּרָתוֹ סְאָה לְבֶן לֵוִי וּסְאָה לְעָנִי. רַבִּי מֵאִיר אוֹמֵר דְּאוֹתוֹ פּוֹעֵל יָכוֹל לֶאֱכֹל מִן הַמְּגוּרָה שְׁמֹנֶה סְאִים, דְּמִסְּתָמָא בְּתוֹרַת מַעֲשֵׂר רִאשׁוֹן נָתַן לַלֵּוִי וּבְתוֹרַת מַעֲשַׂר עָנִי נָתַן לֶעָנִי, וַהֲרֵי יֵשׁ כָּאן שְׁמֹנֶה סְאִים מְתֻקָּנִים וּמְעַט יוֹתֵר. וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁלֹּא רָאָהוּ מַפְרִישׁ תְּרוּמָה גְדוֹלָה, לֹא נֶחְשְׁדוּ עַמֵּי הָאָרֶץ עַל הַתְּרוּמָה:


ונתן סאה לבן לוי – Our Mishnah is speaking about a worker who is a member of the order for the observance of Levitical laws in daily intercourse (i.e., Haver), who comes to dine with the owner of he house and does not believe him on the tithes, and the worker saw that owner of the house giving from his storehouse a Seah to the son of a Levite (i.e., First Tithe) and a Seah to a poor person (i.e., the Poor Tithe, in years 3 and 6 of the seven year cycle). Rabbi Meir states that this worker can eat from the storehouse eight Seah, for undefined, according to the law of First Tithe, he gave to the Levite, and according to the law of the Poor Tithe, he gave to the poor person, and behold there are eight Seah which were already made legally fit for use by giving the priestly dues and a little bit more. And even though he did not see him separate the Great Terumah (i.e., the two-percent for the Kohen), the illiterate were not suspected [of not fulfilling] the heave-offering.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 4:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:2:3)
