**וַחֲכָמִים אוֹמְרִים לֹא הִפְרִישׁ בַּעַל הַבַּיִת אֶלָּא לְפִי חֶשְׁבּוֹן.** סְעֻדָּה זוֹ שֶׁרוֹצֶה הַפּוֹעֵל לֶאֱכֹל אֶצְלוֹ, וְהַשְּׁאָר מַתָּנָה בְּעַלְמָא הוּא דְּיָהֵיב לַלֵּוִי וְלֶעָנִי, הִלְכָּךְ אֵין הַפּוֹעֵל יָכוֹל לֶאֱכֹל אֶצְלוֹ עַד שֶׁיִּכְלוּ שְׁמֹנֶה סְאִין כְּדִבְרֵי רַבִּי מֵאִיר, אֶלָּא אוֹתָהּ סְעֻדָּה שֶׁהָיָה לוֹ לֶאֱכֹל אֶצְלוֹ וְתוּ לֹא. פֵּרוּשׁ אַחֵר וְנָתַן סְאָה לְבֶן לֵוִי וּסְאָה לְעָנִי וְהָלְכוּ לָהֶם, וּכְשֶׁבָּא לְהַפְרִישׁ שְׁמֹנֶה סְאִין מִן הַמְּגוּרָה וְלֶאֱכֹל אוֹתָם עַל סְמַךְ הַשְּׁנֵי סְאִין שֶׁנָּתַן לְבֶן לֵוִי וְלְעָנִי וְאֵינוֹ יוֹדֵעַ אִם אוֹתָן שְׁנֵי סְאִין קַיָּמִים אִם לָאו, רַבִּי מֵאִיר אוֹמֵר מַפְרִישׁ שְׁמֹנֶה סְאִין וְאוֹכְלָן עַל סְמַךְ אוֹתָן שְׁנֵי סְאִין שֶׁנָּתַן לְבֶן לֵוִי וְלְעָנִי בְּחֶזְקַת שֶׁהֵן קַיָּמִים. וַחֲכָמִים אוֹמְרִים אֵינוֹ מַפְרִישׁ אֶלָּא לְפִי חֶשְׁבּוֹן הַקַּיָּמִים, אִם כָּל הַשְּׁנֵי סְאִין קַיָּמִים מַפְרִישׁ עֲלֵיהֶן שְׁמֹנֶה סְאִין וּמְעַט יוֹתֵר, וְאִם אֵין קַיָּמִים אֶלָּא חֶצְיָן אֵינוֹ מַפְרִישׁ עֲלֵיהֶם אֶלָּא אַרְבַּע סְאִין, לְפִי חֶשְׁבּוֹן מַה שֶּׁנִּמְצָא מֵהֶן קַיָּמִים כָּךְ מַפְרִישׁ עֲלֵיהֶם וְלֹא יוֹתֵר. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


וחכ"א לא הפריש בעל הבית אלא לפי חשבון (with a calculation [of the percentage of tithes which remain to be separa from the batch as a whole]) - this meal that the worker wants to eat with him., but the remainder is a mere gift that he gives to the Levite and the poor person, therefore, the worker does not have to eat with him until he has completed eight Seah according to the words of Rabbi Meir, but that meal which he was to eat with him and nothing more. Another explanation: “and he gave a Seah to the son of Levite and a Seah to a poor person, and they went on their way. But when he came to separate eight Seah from the storehouse and to eat them on the support of the two Seah that he gave to the son of the Levite and the poor person, he doesn’t know if those two Seah exist or not. Rabbi Meir says that he should separate eight Seah and eat them, in support of those two Seah that he gave to the son of the Levite and the poor person in the presupposition that they exist. But the Sages say that he does not separate other than according to a calculation [of the percentage of tithes which to be separated from the batch as a whole], that exist; if all of the two Seah exist, he separates upon them eight Seah and a bit more, and if only one-half of them exist, he does not separate on them other than four Seah, according to the calculation of what he finds of them existing, thus he separates upon them and not more. And the Halakha is according to the Sages.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 4:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:3:1)


**עַיִן יָפָה אֶחָד מֵאַרְבָּעִים.** מִי שֶׁהוּא נְדִיב לֵב וְתוֹרֵם בְּעַיִן יָפָה מַפְרִישׁ אֶחָד מֵאַרְבָּעִים:


עין יפה אחד מארבעים – He who is prompted by the heart and donates the heave-offering generously, separates one in forty.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 4:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:3:2)


**וְהַבֵּינוֹנִית אֶחָד מֵחֲמִשִּׁים.** לָאו דִּבְרֵי בֵית שַׁמַּאי נִינְהוּ אֶלָּא תַּנָּא קַמָּא קָאָמַר לַהּ. וּמֵעַיִן יָפָה דְּבֵית שַׁמַּאי אַתָּה לָמֵד לְדִבְרֵיהֶם עַיִן רָעָה וּבֵינוֹנִית אַף עַל גַּב דְּלֹא תָנֵי לַהּ בְּמַתְנִיתִין:


והבינונית אחד בחמשים – these are not the words of the School of Shammai, but rather, the first Tanna, who stated it. But from the generosity of the School of Shammai (who said one in thirty) you learn for their words what is selfishness/the evil eye and the middle/intermediate amount, even though they are not taught [explicitly] in our Mishnah.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 4:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:3:3)


**וְעָלָה בְיָדוֹ אֶחָד מִשִּׁשִּׁים.** כְּגוֹן שֶׁתָּרַם מֵאֹמֶד וְעָלָה בְיָדוֹ אֶחָד מִשִּׁשִּׁים וְהוּא עַיִן רָעָה:


ועלה בידו אחד מששים – as, for example, that he donated from an estimate/guess-work, and there came up into his hand one out of sixty which is selfishness/the evil eye.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 4:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:3:4)


**חָזַר וְהוֹסִיף חַיָּב בְּמַעַשְׂרוֹת.** דִּכְבָר נִפְטְרוּ וְאֵין שֵׁם תְּרוּמָה חָל עַל מַה שֶּׁמּוֹסִיף, הִלְכָּךְ הַתּוֹסֶפֶת הַזֹּאת הַוְיָא טֶבֶל גָּמוּר וְחַיֶּבֶת בְּמַעַשְׂרוֹת:


חזר והוסיף חייב במעשרות – for he has already exempted and the designation of heave-offering does not fall upon what he adds, therefore, this addition is completely eatables forbidden pending the separation of sacred gifts , and he is liable for tithes.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 4:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:3:5)


**מִשִּׁשִּׁים וְאֶחָד.** כְּגוֹן שֶׁהָיוּ בַכְּרִי שִׁשִּׁים וְאֶחָד סְאִין וְעָלָה בְיָדוֹ סְאָה, אוֹתָהּ סְאָה תְּרוּמָה:


מששים ואחד – as, for example, that there were sixty one Seah in the pile and he took up a Seah in his hand, that Seah is the heave-offering/Terumah.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 4:3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:3:6)


**וְיַחֲזֹר וְיִתְרֹם כְּמוֹ שֶׁהוּא לָמוּד.** [אִם הוּא רָגִיל] לִתְרֹם אֶחָד מִשִּׁשִּׁים יַפְרִישׁ עֲדַיִן אֶחָד מִשִּׁשִּׁים בַּסְּאָה, וְאִם הוּא רָגִיל לִתְרֹם אֶחָד מֵחֲמִשִּׁים נִמְצְאוּ דְנִשְׁאֲרוּ עֲדַיִן אַחַת עֶשְׂרֵה סְאִין שֶׁלֹּא נִתְרְמוּ:


ויחזור ויתרום כמו שהוא למוד – [if he was accustomed] to donate one from sixty, he should still separate one from sixty in a Seah, and if he was accustomed to donate one from fifty [Seah], and there were found that there still remained eleven Seah that had not been donated [for Terumah].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 4:3:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:3:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:3:7)


**בְּמִדָּה וּבְמִשְׁקָל.** כְּלוֹמַר אוֹתוֹ מוֹתָר שֶׁהוּא תוֹרֵם, מֻתָּר לִתְרֹם בְּמִדָּה וּבְמִשְׁקָל וּבְמִנְיָן. דְּלֹא אָמְרוּ לִתְרֹם מֵאֹמֶד (לעיל פ"א מ"ז), אֶלָּא בִּתְחִלַּת הַתְּרוּמָה:


במדה ובמשקל – meaning to say, that excess/surplus that he donates [as Terumah], it is permissible to donate/separate out Terumah by measure, or by weight or by number. For they didn’t say to donate/separate Terumah] from an estimate, other than at the beginning of the heave-offerinf.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 4:3:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:3:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:3:8)


**אַף שֶׁלֹּא מִן הַמֻּקָּף.** דְּסָבַר רַבִּי יְהוּדָה אֵין צָרִיךְ לִתְרֹם מִן הַמֻּקָּף אֶלָּא בִּתְחִלַּת תְּרוּמָה. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


אף שלא מן המוקף – for Rabbi Yehuda holds that one does not need to donate [Terumah] from that which is near and adjoining, other than at the beginning of Terumah. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehuda.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 4:3:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:3:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:3:9)


**מֻקָּף.** קָרוֹב וְסָמוּךְ וּבְאוֹתוֹ מָקוֹם עַצְמוֹ:


מוקף – near and adjoing, and in the same place iself.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 4:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:4:1)


**כְּדַעְתּוֹ שֶׁל בַּעַל הַבָּיִת.** לְפִי שֶׁיֵּשׁ תּוֹרֵם בְּעַיִן יָפָה, וְיֵשׁ בְּעַיִן רָעָה, וְיֵשׁ בְּבֵינוֹנִית:


כדעתו של בעל הבית – because [he is one] who donates generously, and there are those who are miserly/with an evil eye, and there are those who donate the intermediate amount.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 4:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:4:2)


**פִּחֵת עֲשָׂרָה.** וְתָרַם מֵאַרְבָּעִים:


פיחת עשרה – and he donated one in forty.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 4:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:4:3)


**אוֹ הוֹסִיף עֲשָׂרָה.** וְתָרַם מִשִּׁשִּׁים:


או הוסיף עשרה – and donated [Terumah] from [one in] sixty.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 4:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:4:4)


**תְּרוּמָתוֹ תְרוּמָה.** דְּכֵיוָן דִּבְאֶחָד מִשִּׁעוּרֵי חֲכָמִים תָּרַם, יָכוֹל לְמֵימַר לֵיהּ אֲנָא לְהָכִי אֲמַדְתִּיךָ:


תרומתו תרומה – for since that in one of he measures of he Sages he donated [Terumah], he can say to him: “For this I have estimated for you.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 4:4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:4:5)


**וְאִם נִתְכַּוֵּן לְהוֹסִיף.** שֶׁהָיָה יוֹדֵעַ דַּעְתּוֹ שֶׁל בַּעַל הַבַּיִת וּבְמִתְכַּוֵּן הוֹסִיף, אֲפִלּוּ אֶחָד, אֵין תְּרוּמָתוֹ תְרוּמָה:


ואם נתכוין להוסיף – that he knows the intention of the owner of the house, and he intended to add, even one, his heave-offering is not a heave-offering.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 4:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:5:1)


**אֶחָד מֵעֲשָׂרָה.** יָכוֹל לְהַרְבּוֹת וּלְהַפְרִישׁ תְּרוּמָה, הוֹאִיל וְאַשְׁכְּחָן שֵׁם תְּרוּמָה בְּמָקוֹם אַחֵר שֶׁהִיא אֶחָד מֵעֲשָׂרָה. וְאִם רִבָּה יוֹתֵר אֵין שֵׁם תְּרוּמָה עַל אוֹתוֹ רִבּוּי, וְהוּא חֻלִּין הַטְּבוּלִין לְמַעֲשֵׂר, וּמְדֻמָּע בַּתְּרוּמָה. וְזוֹ הִיא תַּקָּנָתוֹ, שֶׁיַּעֲשֶׂנּוּ תְּרוּמַת מַעֲשֵׂר לְמָקוֹם אַחֵר וְיִתְּנֵהוּ לַלֵּוִי שֶׁיַּעֲשֶׂנּוּ תְּרוּמַת מַעֲשֵׂר עַל מַעֲשֵׂר טֶבֶל שֶׁלּוֹ, וְהַלֵּוִי יִתֵּן לוֹ חֻלִּין כְּנֶגֶד אוֹתוֹ רִבּוּי. וְהָא דְקָאָמַר יַעֲשֶׂנּוּ תְּרוּמַת מַעֲשֵׂר לְמָקוֹם אַחֵר וְלֹא אָמַר יַעֲשֶׂנּוּ תְּרוּמָה לְמָקוֹם אַחֵר, לְפִי שֶׁאֵין תּוֹרְמִין שֶׁלֹּא מִן הַמֻּקָּף, אֲבָל תְּרוּמַת מַעֲשֵׂר מַפְרִישִׁין אוֹתָהּ שֶׁלֹּא מִן הַמֻּקָּף:


חד מעשרה – he is able to increase and to separate Terumah, for since that we find that the designation of Terumah in another place, which is one out of ten. But if it even greater [a percentage], the designation of Terumah is not on that excess, and it is unconsecrated produce that is subject to sacred gifts of tithes, and it is subject to the laws of Terumah, and this is its remedy, that he should make it Terumah of the tithe (i.e., that which the Levite gives to the Kohen) in another place, and give it to the Levite that he can make it the Terumah of the tithe of his eatables forbidden pending the separation of sacred gifts, and the Levite will give him unconsecrated produce corresponding to that excess. And that we say that he should make it Terumah of the tithe for another place and did not say that he should make it Terumah/heave offering for another place, because we don’t give Terumah/heave offering other than from that which is close b, vut the Terumah of the tithe, we separate it from something not close by.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 4:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:5:2)


**מֶחֱצָה חֻלִּין וּמֶחֱצָה תְּרוּמָה.** דְּסָבַר יָכוֹל אָדָם לַעֲשׂוֹת חֲצִי כִּרְיוֹ תְּרוּמָה, דִּכְתִיב (דברים יח) רֵאשִׁית דְּגָנְךָ, דַּיּוֹ לָרֵאשִׁית שֶׁיִּהְיֶה כַדָּגָן, [רֵאשִׁית הִיא תְּרוּמָה], וְדָגָן הוּא הַשִּׁירַיִם:


מחצה חולין ומחצה תרומה – that he (i.e., Rabbi Yishmael) held that a person can make half of his pile Terumah/heave-offering, as it is written (Deuteronomy 18:4): “You shall also give him the first fruits [of your new grain and wine and oil, and the first shearing of your sheep],” it is enough for the first that it be like grain [and the first is Terumah/heave-offering] and the grain is the left-overs.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 4:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:5:3)


**עַד שֶׁיְּשַׁיֵּר שָׁם חֻלִּין.** לֹא בָעִינַן רַק שֶׁיִּהְיוּ שְׁיָרֶיהָ נִכָּרִין. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי טַרְפוֹן וְרַבִּי עֲקִיבָא:


עד שישייר שם חולין – we do not require oly that its left-overs be recognized, and the Halakha is according to Rabbi Tarfon and Rabbi Akiva.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 4:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:6:1)


**הַכַּלְכָּלָה.** הַסַּל שֶׁמּוֹדְדִים בּוֹ לְהַפְרִישׁ הַמַּעַשְׂרוֹת וּתְרוּמַת מַעֲשֵׂר, שֶׁאֵין מַפְרִישִׁים אוֹתָן אֶלָּא בְּמִדָּה וּבְמִנְיָן וּבְמִשְׁקָל, כְּדִתְנַן (אבות פ"א מט"ז) וְאַל תַּרְבֶּה לְעַשֵּׂר אֹמָדוֹת. אֲבָל תְּרוּמָה גְדוֹלָה אֵין מַפְרִישִׁים אוֹתָהּ אֶלָּא מֵאֹמֶד כְּדִתְנַן לְעֵיל (פ"א מ"ז):


הכלכלה – the basket that they measure in it to separate the tithes and the Terumah of the tithe, for we don’t separate them other than by measurement, and by number and by weight, as it is taught in the Mishnah (see Tractate Terumot, Chapter 1, Mishnah 7), and don’t increase to tithe by estimations, but the Great Terumah – we do not separate it other than from estimation, as it is taught in the Mishnah above (Tractate Terumot 1;7).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 4:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:6:2)


**מְשַׁעֲרִין אֶת הַכַּלְכָּלָה.** לֵידַע מַה שֶׁיִּכָּנְסוּ בָהּ מִן הַפֵּרוֹת:


משערין את הכלכלה – [the calculate] to know what they will bring in from the produce.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 4:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:6:3)


**בַּבַּכּוּרוֹת.** בִּזְמַן שֶׁהַפֵּרוֹת בְּבִכּוּרֵיהֶן הֵן בְּמִלּוּאָן וְהַכַּלְכָּלָה מְכִילָה מֵהֶן פָּחוֹת מִמַּה שֶּׁהִיא מְכִילָה מֵהֶן בְּסוֹף הַקַּיִץ, שֶׁכְּבָר הִתְחִילוּ לְהִצְטַמֵּק וְהֵן צְנוּמוֹת דַּקּוֹת:


בבכורות – at the time when the produce are in their ripening stage and are in their fullness and the basket includes less of them from what it holds of them at the end of the summer, when they begin to reduce in size and they are shriveled [and] thin (see Genesis 41:23).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 4:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:6:4)


**וּבְאֶמְצַע הַקָּיִץ.** שֶׁהֵן מְבֻשָּׁלוֹת וְאֵינָם מְצֻמָּקוֹת, הַכַּלְכָּלָה מַחֲזֶקֶת מִן הַפֵּרוֹת יוֹתֵר מִמַּה שֶּׁמַּחֲזֶקֶת מֵהֶן כְּשֶׁהֵן מְבֻכָּרוֹת בִּמְלוֹאָן, וּפָחוֹת מִמַּה שֶּׁהִיא מַחֲזֶקֶת מֵהֶן בְּסוֹף הַקַּיִץ כְּשֶׁכְּבָר נִצְטַמְּקוּ. הִלְכָּךְ מְשַׁעֲרִין בִּשְׁלֹשָׁה זְמַנִּים הַלָּלוּ:


ובאמצע הקיץ – when they are ripened and are not shriveled, and the basket holds from the produce more than what it holds of them when they fully developed, but less than what hit holds of them at the end of the summer when they have become reduced in size. Therefore, we calculate at these three seasons/times.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 4:6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:6:5)


**וּבַסְּיָפוֹת.** פֵּרוֹת שֶׁבְּסוֹף הַקַּיִץ:


ובסייפות – produce that is at the end of the summer.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 4:6:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:6:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:6:6)


**הַמּוֹנֶה מְשֻׁבָּח.** מִן הַמּוֹצִיא בְּאֹמֶד:


המונה משובח – from the one who removes through estimation.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 4:6:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:6:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:6:7)


**וְהַמּוֹדֵד מְשֻׁבָּח וְכוּ'.** וְדַוְקָא בְּמַעַשְׂרוֹת וּבִתְרוּמַת מַעֲשֵׂר, אֲבָל לֹא בִתְרוּמָה גְדוֹלָה כְּדִפְרִישִׁית:


והמודד משובח וכו' – and especially with tithes and the Terumah of the tithe, but not with the Great Terumah (i.e., that two percent which is given to the Kohen by Israelites), as we have explained.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 4:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:7:1)


**תְּרוּמָה עוֹלָה בְּאֶחָד וּמֵאָה.** סְאָה שֶׁל תְּרוּמָה שֶׁנָּפְלָה לְמֵאָה סְאִין שֶׁל חֻלִּין, שֶׁהֵם בֵּין הַכֹּל אֶחָד וּמֵאָה, נוֹטֵל אַחַת מֵהֶן וְנוֹתְנָהּ לַכֹּהֵן, וְהַשְּׁאָר חֻלִּין כְּמוֹ שֶׁהָיוּ:


תרומה עולה באחד ומאה – a Seah of heave-offering/Terumah that fell into one-hundred Seah of unconsecrated produce, which are, amidst everything one-and one-hundred, he takes one [Seah] from them and gives it to the Kohen, and the rest is unconsecrated produce as they were.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 4:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:7:2)


**בְּמֵאָה וָעוֹד.** אִם נָפְלָה סְאָה שֶׁל תְּרוּמָה לְתוֹךְ תִּשְׁעָה וְתִשְׁעִים סְאִין שֶׁל חֻלִּין וּמַשֶּׁהוּ יוֹתֵר מִתִּשְׁעִים וְתִשְׁעָה, וְזֶה הַמַּשֶּׁהוּ אֵין לוֹ שִׁעוּר, הוֹאִיל וְיֵשׁ כָּאן מֵאָה וְעוֹד מַשֶּׁהוּ, נוֹטֵל סְאָה אַחַת וְנוֹתְנָהּ לַכֹּהֵן, וְהַשְּׁאָר חֻלִּין כְּשֶׁהָיוּ:


במאה ועוד – if a Seah of heave-offering/Terumah fell into ninety-nine Seah of unconsecrated produce and a bit more than ninety nine, and this a bit more, has no measurement, for since there is one-hundred and another something, he takes one Seah and gives it to the Kohen, and the rest is unconsecrated produce as they were.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 4:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:7:3)


**קַב לְמֵאָה סְאָה שְׁתוּת לַמְדַמֵּעַ.** אִם נָפְלָה סְאָה שֶׁל תְּרוּמָה בְּתִשְׁעִים וָתֵשַׁע שֶׁל חֻלִּין וְקַב יוֹתֵר, שֶׁהַקַּב הוּא שְׁתוּת לַסְּאָה שֶׁל תְּרוּמָה הַמְדַמַּעַת, שֶׁהַסְּאָה שִׁשָּׁה קַבִּים, אָז נוֹטֵל סְאָה אַחַת וְהַשְּׁאָר חֻלִּין כְּשֶׁהָיוּ. אֲבָל אִם הָיוּ הַחֻלִּין פָּחוֹת מִתִּשְׁעִים וְתִשְׁעָה וְקַב, הַכֹּל מְדֻמָּע, וְיִמָּכְרוּ כֻלָּם לַכֹּהֵן בִּדְמֵי תְרוּמָה חוּץ מִדְּמֵי סְאָה שֶׁל תְּרוּמָה שֶׁבָּהּ. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר שֶׁאֵין תְּרוּמָה עוֹלָה בְּפָחוֹת מֵאַחַת וּמֵאָה, דְסָתַם לָן תַּנָּא כְּוָתֵיהּ בְּכַמָּה דּוּכְתֵּי. וְהַכֹּל מוֹדִים שֶׁאֵין הַתְּרוּמָה עוֹלָה בְּמֵאָה בִּלְבָד, וְכָל שֶׁכֵּן בְּפָחוֹת מִמֵּאָה, דִּכְתִיב בִּתְרוּמַת מַעֲשֵׂר (במדבר יח) מִכָּל חֶלְבּוֹ אֶת מִקְדְּשׁוֹ מִמֶּנּוּ, דָּבָר שֶׁאַתָּה תּוֹרֵם מִמֶּנּוּ אִם נָפַל לְתוֹכוֹ מְקַדְּשׁוֹ, וְהַיְנוּ אֶחָד מִמֵּאָה, דְּמִמֵּאָה סְאִין מַפְרִישִׁין עֲשָׂרָה לְמַעֲשֵׂר רִאשׁוֹן, וּמֵאוֹתָן עֲשָׂרָה סְאָה לִתְרוּמַת מַעֲשֵׂר, וְאָמַר רַחֲמָנָא שֶׁאִם חָזְרָה אוֹתָהּ סְאָה לְתִשְׁעִים וְתִשְׁעָה מְקַדַּשְׁתָּן וְנַעֲשֶׂה הַכֹּל מְדֻמָּע:


קב למאה סאה שתות למדמע – if a Seah of Terumah/heave-offering fell into ninety-nine [parts] of unconsecrated produce and a Kab more, for the Kab is one-sixth of a Seah of Terumah in a proportion that makes the whole prohibited to non-priests, for a Seah is six Kabim, then he takes one Seah and the rest is unconsecrated produce as they were. But if there was unconsecrated produce less than ninety-nine [parts] and a Kab, the entire whole is forbidden to non-priests, and all of it should be sold to a Kohen at the value of Terumah outside of the value of a Seah of the Terumah that is in it. But the Halakha is according to Rabbi Eliezer that the Terumah is not offered with less than one hundred and one. For the Tanna/teacher of the Mishnah taught for us taught us anonymously according to him in several places. And everyone admits that the Terumah is not offered with one-hundred alone, and all the more so, with less than one-hundred [corresponding parts] as it is written (Numbers 18:29): “from every thing its best portion, the part thereof that is to be consecrated,” a thank that you donate from it [as Terumah], if it fell into it, you sanctify it, which is identical with one from one -hundred [parts], for from one-hundred Seah, you separate ten for First Tithe, and from those ten Seah for the Terumah of the tithe, and the All-Merciful stated that if that Seah returned to the ninety-nine, you sanctify them and everything becomes forbidden to non-priests, subject to the laws of Terumah.



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 4:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:8:1)


**תְּאֵנִים שְׁחוֹרוֹת מַעֲלוֹת אֶת הַלְּבָנוֹת.** תְּאֵנָה שֶׁל תְּרוּמָה, שְׁחוֹרָה אוֹ לְבָנָה, שֶׁנָּפְלָה לְמֵאָה תְּאֵנִים שֶׁל חֻלִּין, חֶצְיָן שְׁחוֹרוֹת וְחֶצְיָן לְבָנוֹת, כֻּלָּן מִצְטָרְפִין לְבַטְּלָהּ, שֶׁאִם הָיָה רוֹצֶה הָיָה דוֹרְסָן וּמְעָרְבָן הַכֹּל בְּיַחַד. וְאִם שְׁחוֹרָה נָפְלָה, מַעֲלֶה אַחַת מִן הַשְּׁחוֹרוֹת, וְאִם לְבָנָה, מַעֲלֶה אַחַת מִן הַלְּבָנוֹת, וְהַשְּׁאָר חֻלִּין כְּשֶׁהָיוּ:


תאנים שחורים מעלות ת הלבנות – a fig of heave-offering, black or white, that fell into one-hundred unconsecrated figs , half of them black and half of hem white, all of them combine to be voided, for if he had wanted, he would tread on them and mix them all together. But if a a black one fell, it neutralizes one from the black ones, and if a white one [fell], it neutralizes one of the white ones, and the remainder are unconsecrated produce as they were.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 4:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:8:2)


**עִגּוּלֵי דְּבֵלָה.** דְּבֵלוֹת תְּאֵנִים הַנִּדְרָסִים בְּעִגּוּל, גְּדוֹלִים מַעֲלִים אֶת הַקְּטַנִּים, אִם יֵשׁ שָׁם חֲמִשִּׁים עִגּוּלִים קְטַנִּים וְעֶשְׂרִים וַחֲמִשָּׁה עִגּוּלִים גְּדוֹלִים, וְאֶחָד מִן הַגְּדוֹלִים כִּשְׁנַיִם מִן הַקְּטַנִּים, וְנָפַל לְתוֹכָן עִגּוּל קָטָן שֶׁל תְּרוּמָה, מְסַיְּעוֹת כֻּלָּן לְבַטֵּל. וְכֵן עִגּוּלִים וּמַלְבְּנִים. עִגּוּלִים הֵם הַנִּדְרָסוֹת בְּעִגּוּל, מַלְבְּנִים, תְּאֵנִים הַנִּדְרָסוֹת בְּצוּרַת רִבּוּעַ כְּמִין דְּפוּס שֶׁעוֹשִׂים בּוֹ הַלְּבֵנִים מְרֻבָּעוֹת:


עגולי דבלה – cakes of of figs that are pressed/stamped with a round mold in which figs are pressed, the large ones neutralize the small ones,, if there are fifty small cakes found there and twenty-five large cakes, and one of he large ones is like two of the small ones, and there fell into them a small cake of heave-offering/Terumah, they assist all of them to be nullified. And similarly, round ones neutralize the figs pressed in quadrangular molds, these round ones are pressed in a round mold, the quadrangular ones are figs pressed in the square shape, like a frame/mold that they make the square stamped ones.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 4:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:8:3)


**וְרַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר אוֹסֵר.** אִם שְׁחוֹרָה נָפְלָה הַשְּׁחוֹרוֹת אֲסוּרוֹת, וְאִם לְבָנָה נָפְלָה הַלְּבָנוֹת אֲסוּרוֹת, וְכֵן בִּגְדוֹלִים וּקְטַנִּים, וְעִגּוּלִים וּמַלְבְּנִים, הַדּוֹמִין לְאוֹתָהּ שֶׁנָּפְלָה אֲסוּרִים:


רבי אליעזר אוסר – if a black one fell, the black ones are forbidden, and id if what one fell, the white ones are forbidden, and similarly, large and small ones, and round and square ones, that which is similar to what fell are forbidden.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 4:8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:8:4)


**בְּיָדוּעַ מַה נָּפְלָה.** שְׁחוֹרָה אוֹ לְבָנָה, אֵין מַעֲלוֹת, לְפִי שֶׁיָּכוֹל לֶאֱכֹל הָאֲחֵרוֹת, וְכֵיוָן דְּמֻתָּרוֹת הֵן אֵין מְסַיְּעוֹת לְבַטֵּל. אֲבָל אִם אֵין יָדוּעַ אִם שְׁחוֹרָה נָפְלָה אוֹ לְבָנָה נָפְלָה, כֵּיוָן דְּכֻלָּן בְּסָפֵק אִסּוּר, מַעֲלוֹת זוֹ אֶת זוֹ. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי עֲקִיבָא:


בידו מה נפלה – black or white, they do not neutralize, because one is able to eat the others, and since they are permitted, they do not help to nullify. But if it is not known if black ones fell or white ones fell, that all of them are doubtfully forbidden, they neutralize each other, and he Halakha is according to Rabbi Akiva.



### Mishnah 9

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 4:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:9:1)


**כֵּיצַד.** לְפֵרוּשֵׁי מִלְּתֵיהּ דְּרַבִּי עֲקִיבָא קָאָתֵי:


כיצד – it comes to explain the words of Rabbi Akiva.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 4:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:9:2)


**וּבְזוֹ רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר מַחְמִיר.** בְּהָא דְתָנִינַן לְעֵיל, אֲבָל בְּמַאי דְבָעֵינָא לְמֵימַר לְקַמָּן הֲוֵי אִפְּכָא:


ובזו ר' אליעזר מחמיר – of that which we taught above (in Mishnah 8), but in what that he need to say further on (the next Mishnah), it is the opposite.



### Mishnah 10

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 4:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:10:1)


**בְּדוֹרֵס לִיטְרָא קְצִיעוֹת.** לִיטְרָא שֶׁל תְּאֵנִים יְבֵשִׁים שֶׁל תְּרוּמָה:


בדורס ליטרא קציעות – a Litra/pound of dry figs of heave-offering.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 4:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:10:2)


**עַל פִּי הַכַּד.** שֶׁל חֻלִּין, וְהַרְבֵּה כַּדִּין יֵשׁ:


על פי הכד – of heave-offering [in the mouth of a jar] and there are many jars.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 4:10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:10:3)


**וְאֵין יָדוּעַ.** בְּאֵיזֶה כַּד דְּרָסָהּ, וּבְכָל כַּד יֵשׁ מֵאָה לִיטְרוֹת שֶׁל חֻלִּין, אֲפִלּוּ אֵין כָּאן מֵאָה כַּדִּין, שָׁרֵי רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר, וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁיָּדוּעַ הוּא שֶׁהַלִּיטְרָא שֶׁל תְּרוּמָה הִיא בְּפִי הַכַּד וְאֵינָהּ מְעֹרֶבֶת עִם הַחֻלִּין, רוֹאִין אוֹתָן כְּאִלּוּ נִפְרְדוּ מִמְּקוֹמָן וְנִתְעָרְבוּ הַחֻלִּין בַּתְּרוּמָה, וְעוֹלִין בְּאֶחָד וּמֵאָה. וְרַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ סָבַר כֵּיוָן דְּאֵינָהּ מְעֹרֶבֶת אֵין הַתַּחְתּוֹנוֹת מְסַיְּעוֹת לְבַטֵּל אֶת הָעֶלְיוֹנוֹת, אֶלָּא הַשּׁוּלַיִם מֻתָּרִים וְהַפּוּמִים אֲסוּרִים, עַד שֶׁיִּהְיוּ שָׁם מֵאָה כַדִּין וְיִתְבַּטֵּל פִּי חָבִית זֶה בְּמֵאָה פוּמִין, וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ:


ואין ידוע – in which jar he stuffed in [the figs], and in each jar there are one-hundred Litra/pounds of unconsecrated produce (i.e., figs), even if there are aren’t here one hundred according to the law, Rabbi Eliezer permits it. But even though that it is known that the Litra in the mouth of the jar is of heave-offering, and is not mixed with the unconsecrated produce (i.e., figs), we view them as if they were separated from their place and the unconsecrated produce (i.e., figs) combined with the heave offering and are neutralized in one and one-hundred. But Rabbi Yehoshua holds that since they are not mixed/combined, the bottom/lower ones do not assist to nullify the upper ones, but rather, the rims at the bottom of the vessel are permitted, whereas the ones at the mouth are forbidden, until there will be there one-hundred according to the law and it will nullify the mouth of this jug with one-hundred mouths, and the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yehoshua.



### Mishnah 11

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 4:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:11:1)


**מְגוּרָה.** אוֹצָר שֶׁאוֹגְרִים בּוֹ הַתְּבוּאָה:


מגורה – a storehouse where they store the grain.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 4:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:11:2)


**וְקִפְּאָהּ.** הֵסִיר מַה שֶּׁלְּמַעְלָה, תַּרְגּוּם וַיִּצֶּף הַבַּרְזֶל (מלכים ב ו) וְקָפָא פַרְזְלָא:


וקפאה – he removed what was above, the Aramaic translation of (II Kings 6:6): “and he made the ax head float”: the iron floated.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 4:11:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:11:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:11:3)


**אִם יֵשׁ בַּקִּפּוּי תַּעֲלֶה בְּאֶחָד וּמֵאָה.** מְפָרֵשׁ בַּיְרוּשַׁלְמִי דְּהָכִי קָאָמַר אִם יֵשׁ בַּקִּפּוּי עִם מַה שֶּׁתַּחְתָּיו אֶחָד וּמֵאָה תַּעֲלֶה, וְרַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר לְטַעֲמֵיהּ דְּאָמַר הַתַּחְתּוֹנוֹת מַעֲלוֹת אֶת הָעֶלְיוֹנוֹת, וְקָא מַשְׁמַע לָן דְּאַף עַל גַּב דְּקִפְּאָהּ וְגָלֵי דַּעְתֵּיהּ שֶׁלֹּא נִתְעָרְבָה עִם הַתַּחְתּוֹנוֹת, אַף עַל פִּי כֵן הַתַּחְתּוֹנוֹת מְסַיְּעוֹת לְבַטֵּל:


אם יש בקפוי תעלה באחד ומאה – The Jerusalem Talmud explains that this is what he said: if there is in the skimming with what is underneath it one-hundred and one [parts], it is neutralized, and Rabbi Eliezer, according to his reasoning, who said that he ones on the bottom neutralize the ones on the top, and it comes to tell us that even though he skimmed it, he revealed his intention that it had not become mixed with the bottom ones, nevertheless, the bottom ones assist to nullify.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 4:11:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:11:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:11:4)


**וְרַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ אוֹמֵר לֹא תַעֲלֶה.** רַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ לְטַעֲמֵיהּ דְּאָמַר לְעֵיל שֶׁאֵין הַתַּחְתּוֹנוֹת מְסַיְּעוֹת לְבַטֵּל:


ורבי יהושע אומר לא תעלה – Rabbi Yehoshua, according to his reasoning who said about that the bottom ones do not assist to nullify.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 4:11:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:11:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:11:5)


**סְאָה תְרוּמָה וְכוּ'.** סִיּוּמָא דְמִלְּתֵיהּ דְּרַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ הִיא, וְהָכִי קָאָמַר, כֵּיוָן דְּלֹא תַעֲלֶה כֵּיצַד יַעֲשֶׂה, יַקְפִּיאֶנָּה. יָסִיר מַה שֶּׁצָּף לְמַעְלָה וּמְעַט סָבִיב לָהּ עַד שֶׁיֵּדַע שֶׁהֵסִיר כֻּלָּהּ, וְהַשְּׁאָר הַכֹּל חֻלִּין:


סאה תרומה וכו' – it is the conclusion of the words of Rabbi Yehoshua, and this is what he said: since it is not neutralized, how does he do it? יקפיאנה – he should remove what floats above and a bit around it until he knows that he removed everything, and all of the rest is unconsecrated produce.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 4:11:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:11:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:11:6)


**אִם כֵּן לָמָּה אָמְרוּ.** מַתְנִיתִין פָּרֵיךְ עַל מִלְּתֵיהּ דְּרַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ. אִם כֵּן בְּאֵיזֶה מָקוֹם אָמְרוּ תְּרוּמָה צְרִיכָה אֶחָד וּמֵאָה, כֵּיוָן שֶׁיֵּשׁ תַּקָּנָה בְּקִפּוּי. וּמְשָׁנֵי בְּשֶׁאֵין יָדוּעַ אִם נִבְלְלָה הַסְּאָה בַּמְּגוּרָה אַחַר שֶׁנָּפְלָה עַל פִּי הַמְּגוּרָה, אִי נַמִּי מִתְּחִלָּה אֵין יָדוּעַ לְהֵיכָן נָפְלָה. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ:


אם כן למה אמרו – our Mishnah disputes the words of Rabbi Yehoshua. If so, in which place did they say that heave-offering requires one and one hundred [parts], since there is repair through skimming. And they respond that when it is not known if the Seah had become mixed up in the storehouse after it fell on the lip of the storehouse, alternatively, from the outset, it was not known where it fell, and the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yehoshua.



### Mishnah 12

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 4:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:12:1)


**שְׁתֵּי קֻפּוֹת.** בְּכָל אַחַת חֲמִשִּׁים סְאָה:


שתי קופות – in each one is fifty Seah [of unconsecrated produce].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 4:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:12:2)


**וְאֵין יָדוּעַ לְאֵיזוֹ מֵהֶן נָפְלָה.** וּשְׁתֵּיהֶן אֲסוּרוֹת מִסָּפֵק, מְסַיְּעוֹת זוֹ אֶת זוֹ לְבַטֵּל, וְאִם רָצָה לְהַעֲלוֹת סְאָה מִזּוֹ מַעֲלֶה, מִזּוֹ מַעֲלֶה, מֶחֱצָה מִזּוֹ וּמֶחֱצָה מִזּוֹ מַעֲלֶה. וּשְׁתֵּי קֻפּוֹת אֲפִלּוּ הֵן בִּשְׁנֵי בָתִּים וְאֵין יָדוּעַ לְאֵיזוֹ מֵהֶן נָפְלָה סְאָה שֶׁל תְּרוּמָה, מַעֲלוֹת זוֹ אֶת זוֹ, מִפְּנֵי שֶׁדַּרְכָּן לְהִתְפַּנּוֹת, וְהֵן מִטַּלְטְלוֹת מִמָּקוֹם לְמָקוֹם, וּפְעָמִים שֶׁמִּתְעָרְבוֹת זוֹ עִם זוֹ. אֲבָל שְׁתֵּי מְגוּרוֹת, שֶׁאֵינָן מִטַּלְטְלוֹת, בִּזְמַן שֶׁשְּׁתֵּיהֶן בְּבַיִת אֶחָד, מַעֲלוֹת זוֹ אֶת זוֹ, וְאִם הֵן בִּשְׁנֵי [בָתִּים] אֵין מַעֲלוֹת זוֹ אֶת זוֹ:


ואין ידוע לאיזו מהן נפלה – and both are forbidden from doubt, they assist each other tonullify, and if he wanted to neutralize a Seah from this one, he neutralizes, from that one, he neutralizes, half from this and half from that one, he neutralizes. And these two bins, even if they are in two homes, and it is not known from into which the Seah of heave-offering fell, they neutralize each other, because it is their manner to be vacated, and they are carried from place to place and sometimes are mixed with each other. But two storehouses are not carried; when they are in the same house, they neutralize each other, and if they are in two [houses], they don’t neutralize each other.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 4:12:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:12:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:12:3)


**רַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן אוֹמֵר אֲפִלּוּ הֵן בִּשְׁתֵּי עֲיָרוֹת.** וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן:


ר"ש אומר אפילו הן שבתי עיירות – but the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Shimon.



### Mishnah 13

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 4:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:13:1)


**שֶׁנָּפְלָה אַחַת מֵהֶן.** אֲגֻדָּה אַחַת כַּיּוֹצֵא בָהֶן, וְאֵינָהּ נִכֶּרֶת מִבֵּינֵיהֶן. וְאוֹתָהּ אֲגֻדָּה הָיְתָה חֶצְיָהּ חֻלִּין וְחֶצְיָהּ תְּרוּמָה:


שנפלה אחת מהן – one bunch in a similar manner, and it is not recognized from between them, and that bunch was half unconsecrated produce and half heave-offering.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 4:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/4:13:2)


**שֶׁהָיוּ שָׁם מֵאָה וּשְׁנֵי חֲצָיִים.** עִם הַחֵצִי שֶׁל תְּרוּמָה שֶׁבָּהּ. וְאַשְׁמְעִינַן רַבִּי יוֹסֵי שֶׁאֵין הָאֲגֻדָּה שֶׁחֶצְיָהּ תְּרוּמָה מְדַמַּעַת אֶלָּא לְפִי חֶשְׁבּוֹן תְּרוּמָה שֶׁבָּהּ, וְלֹא אָמְרִינַן שֶׁהָאֲגֻדָּה כֻלָּהּ תְּדַמַּע, כֵּיוָן שֶׁהִיא תְּרוּמָה מְעֹרֶבֶת בְּחֻלִּין:


שהיו שם מאה ושני חציים – with the half of heave-offering which is in it, and Rabbi Yosi teaches us that the the bunch which is half heave-offering becomes Terumah, but according to he percentage of Terumah that is in it, and we don’t say that the entire bunch becomes forbidden to non-priests, since it is Terumah that is mixed with unconsecrated produce.



## Chapter 5


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 5:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/5:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/5:1:1)


**סְאָה תְרוּמָה טְמֵאָה שֶׁנָּפְלָה לְפָחוֹת מִמֵּאָה חֻלִּין.** דִּתְרוּמָה טְמֵאָה נַמִּי עוֹלָה בְּאַחַת וּמֵאָה:


סאה תרומה טמאה שנפלה לפחות ממאה חולין – for impure heave-offering also is neutralized with one and one-hundred.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 5:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/5:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/5:1:2)


**יֵרָקֵבוּ.** דְּאִי אֶפְשָׁר לַכֹּהֵן לְאָכְלָהּ, מִשּׁוּם תְּרוּמָה טְמֵאָה. וְהָא דְלֹא תְּנַן יִדָּלְקוּ, מִשּׁוּם דְּחַיְשִׁינַן דִּלְמָא אַדְּמַדְלִיק לְהוּ אָתֵי בְּהוּ לִידֵי תַקָּלָה וְאָתֵי לְמֵיכָל מִנַּיְהוּ, מִשּׁוּם דְּדִמּוּעַ קִיל לְהוּ לְאִינְשֵׁי וְלֹא מִזְדַּהֲרֵי בֵיהּ כֻּלֵּי הַאי:


ירקבו – for it is impossible for a Kohen to consume it because it is impure heave-offering. And surely as it is not taught in the Mishnah "ידלקו" /”that it should be burned,” tbecause we are concerned that perhaps that if he kindles them, they would come to a stumbling block/snare and that they would come to eat from it, because it is considered a mixture of heave-offering and unconsecrated produce (which is forbidden to non-priests), people would treat it lightly and they would not be careful about it all that much.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 5:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/5:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/5:1:3)


**אִם טְהוֹרָה הָיְתָה אוֹתָהּ סְאָה.** וְגַם הַחֻלִּין טְהוֹרִים, יִמָּכְרוּ לַכֹּהֲנִים בִּדְמֵי תְרוּמָה, שֶׁדָּמֶיהָ פְּחוּתִים לְפִי שֶׁאֵין לָהּ אוֹכְלִים מְרֻבִּים, וְגַם אִם נִטְמֵאת לֹא חַזְיָא לַאֲכִילָה:


אם טהורה היתה אותה סאה – and also the unconsecrated produce should be sold to the Kohanim at the [low] cost of the Terumah/heave-offering, for its worth is less, because there aren’t many who consume it, if it is defiled, it is not fit for eating.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 5:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/5:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/5:1:4)


**חוּץ מִדְּמֵי אוֹתָהּ סְאָה.** שֶׁצָּרִיךְ לִתְּנָהּ לַכֹּהֵן:


חוץ מדמי אותה סאה – for it is necessary to give it to the Kohen.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 5:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/5:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/5:1:5)


**וְאִם לְמַעֲשֵׂר רִאשׁוֹן.** טָהוֹר שֶׁלֹּא נִטְּלָה מִמֶּנּוּ תְּרוּמַת מַעֲשֵׂר נָפְלָה, יְבַקֵּשׁ מִן הַלֵּוִי שֶׁיַּחֲלִיפֵם לוֹ בְּחֻלִּין, וְיַעֲשֵׂם הַלֵּוִי תְּרוּמַת מַעֲשֵׂר עַל טִבְלוֹ:


ואם למעשר ראשון – pure [First Tithe] whose tithe had not been taken from it, the Terumah of the tithe that fell, he should request from the Levite that he should exchange them with unconsecrated produce, and the Levite should make them the Terumah of the tithe on his eatables forbidden pending the separation of sacred gifts..


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 5:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/5:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/5:1:6)


**וְאִם לְמַעֲשֵׂר שֵׁנִי וְהֶקְדֵּשׁ טְהוֹרִים נָפְלָה.** יִפָּדוּ וְיֹאכַל דְּמֵי הַמַּעֲשֵׂר בִּירוּשָׁלַיִם, וְהַדִּמּוּעַ יִמְכֹּר לַכֹּהֲנִים בִּדְמֵי תְרוּמָה חוּץ מִדְּמֵי אוֹתָהּ סְאָה:


ואם למעשר שני והקדש טהורים נפלה – he should redeem them and consume the monetary worth of the tithe in Jerusalem, and that mixture of heave-offering and unconsecrated produce should be sold to the Kohanim at the monetary value of the heave-offering less the value of that Seah.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 5:1:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/5:1:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/5:1:7)


**וְאִם טְמֵאִים הָיוּ.** הַחֻלִּין וְהַמַּעֲשֵׂר רִאשׁוֹן שֶׁנָּפְלָה בָהֶן תְּרוּמָה טְהוֹרָה, דְּהַשְׁתָּא לֹא מָצֵי לִמְעַבְדִינְהוּ תְּרוּמַת מַעֲשֵׂר עַל מָקוֹם אַחֵר, מַפְרִישׁ עָלָיו תְּרוּמַת מַעֲשֵׂר וְיִמְכְּרֶנּוּ לַכֹּהֲנִים, וְיֹאכְלוּם הַכֹּהֲנִים נִקּוּדִים, פֵּרוּשׁ בְּיֹבֶשׁ, כְּמוֹ יָבֵשׁ הָיָה נְקוּדִים (יהושע ט):


ואם טמאים היו – the unconsecrated produce and the First Tithe into which pure heave-offering fell, for now, he is not able to produce Terumah of the tithe on another place, he separates upon it Terumah of the Tithe and sells them to Kohanim, and the Kohanim can eat them in small bits, in explanation, in a dry condition, like (Joshua 9:12 – not quoted correctly by Bartenura): “[This bread of ours….and see] how dry and crumbly it has become.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 5:1:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/5:1:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/5:1:8)


**אוֹ קְלָיוֹת.** כִּי הֵיכִי דְּלֹא תִתְכַּשַׁר הַתְּרוּמָה הַטְּהוֹרָה שֶׁיֵּשׁ בָּהּ לְקַבֵּל טֻמְאָה, דְּכָל אֹכֶל שֶׁלֹּא בָא עָלָיו מַשְׁקֶה מִשִּׁבְעָה מַשְׁקִים אֵינוֹ מֻכְשָׁר לְקַבֵּל טֻמְאָה:


או קליות – just as that the ritually pure heave-offering was not made fit to receive Levitical uncleanness , and all food-stuffs that do not have liquid come upon them from the seven liquids (see Tractate Makhshirin, Chapter 6, Mishnah 4: dew, water, wine, oil, blood, milk, the honey of bees), it is not fitted to receive Levitical uncleanness.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 5:1:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/5:1:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/5:1:9)


**אוֹ יִלּוֹשׁוּ בְמֵי פֵרוֹת.** כְּגוֹן מֵי תוּתִים וְרִמּוֹנִים, דְּלֹא מַכְשְׁרֵי:


או ילושו במי פירות – as, for example, mulberry juice and pomegranates that are not made fit [to receive Levitical uncleanness].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 5:1:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/5:1:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/5:1:10)


**אוֹ יְחַלְּקוּם לְעִסּוֹת.** שֶׁיִּתֵּן פָּחוֹת מִכַּבֵּיצָה בְּכָל עִסָּה וְעִסָּה, דְּאֹכֶל שֶׁהוּא פָּחוֹת מִכַּבֵּיצָה אֵינוֹ מְקַבֵּל טֻמְאַת אֳכָלִים, וְאֵינוֹ מִטַּמֵּא טֻמְאַת אֳכָלִים. וְאִי קַשְׁיָא מַאי שְׁנָא מִסְּאָה תְּרוּמָה טְמֵאָה שֶׁנָּפְלָה בְּחֻלִּין טְהוֹרִים דְּאָמְרִינַן יֵרָקְבוּ מִשּׁוּם דְּעוֹשֶׂה הַכֹּל תְּרוּמָה וְנֶחְשָׁב כִּתְרוּמָה טְמֵאָה וּטְהוֹרָה שֶׁנִּתְעָרְבוּ, וְאִם כֵּן תְּרוּמָה טְהוֹרָה וְחֻלִּין טְמֵאִים נַמִּי שֶׁנִּתְעָרְבוּ יֵחָשְׁבוּ כִּתְרוּמָה טְהוֹרָה וּטְמֵאָה שֶׁנִּתְעָרְבוּ וְיִהְיוּ אֲסוּרִים בַּאֲכִילָה. וְיֵשׁ לוֹמַר דְּלֹא דָמִי, דִּלְעֵיל הֲנָךְ חֻלִּין נַעֲשׂוּ כִּתְרוּמָה לֵאָסֵר לְזָרִים מִכֹּחַ תְּרוּמָה טְמֵאָה, הִלְכָּךְ לַכֹּהֲנִים נַמִּי אֲסִירֵי כְּדִין תְּרוּמָה טְמֵאָה, אֲבָל הֲנָךְ חֻלִּין דְּהָכָא אֵין נַעֲשִׂים תְּרוּמָה אֶלָּא מִכֹּחַ תְּרוּמָה טְהוֹרָה, הִלְכָּךְ שַׁרְיָן לַכֹּהֲנִים כִּתְרוּמָה טְהוֹרָה:


או יחלקום לעיסות – that he will give less than an egg’s bulk in each [little] lump of dough/started dough, and food-stuffs that are less than an egg’s size does not receive ritual impurities of eatables and it doesn’t defile ritual defilement of eatables, and if someone asks what is the difference from a Seah of ritually impure heave-offering that fell into ritually pure unconsecrated produce, that we state that it should rot, because it makes everything heave-offering and is considered like ritually impure and heave-offering that were mixed and would be forbidden to be eaten? But one can say that they are not similar, for above, this unconsecrated produce became like heave -offering, to be prohibited to foreigners (i.e., non-priests) on the strength of the ritually impure heave-offering, therefore, for Kohaim also, they are forbidden according to the law of ritually impure heave-offering/Terumah, but those unconsecrated produce of here, are not made into heave-offering, other than from the strength of pure heave-offering, therefore, they are permitted to Kohanim like ritually pure Terumah.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 5:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/5:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/5:2:1)


**סְאָה תְרוּמָה טְמֵאָה שֶׁנָּפְלָה לְמֵאָה חֻלִּין טְהוֹרִים.** וְהַשְׁתָּא הַתְּרוּמָה עוֹלָה בְּאֶחָד וּמֵאָה וְלֹא נַעֲשֶׂה הַכֹּל מְדֻמָּע, וְהַחֻלִּין טְהוֹרִים לֹא הֻכְשְׁרוּ לְקַבֵּל טֻמְאָה וְלֹא טִמְּאָתָן הַתְּרוּמָה טְמֵאָה:


סאה תרומה טמאה שנפלה למאה חולין טהורים – and now the heave-offering is neutralized in one-and one hundred and everything is not made into a combination of heave-offering and unconsecrated produce that is prohibited to all non-priests. But the pure unconsecrated produce is not made fit to receive ritual impurity, nor does the ritually impure unconsecrated produce defile them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 5:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/5:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/5:2:2)


**תֵּרוֹם וְתִשָּׂרֵף.** כְּשֵׁם שֶׁאִם הָיְתָה תְרוּמָה טְהוֹרָה תַּעֲלֶה וְתֵאָכֵל, כָּךְ הַטְּמֵאָה תֵּרוֹם וְתִשָּׂרֵף. וְהַאי תִשָּׂרֵף דּוּמְיָא דְתֵרָקֵב, שֶׁאָסוּר לֵהָנוֹת מִמֶּנָּה בְּשָׁעָה שֶׁשּׂוֹרְפָהּ, דְּאִי שַׁרְיָת לֵיהּ לֵהָנוֹת מִמֶּנָּה אָתֵי לְמֵיכְלָהּ, דְּסָבוּר שֶׁהִיא מֻתֶּרֶת עַל יְדֵי בִטּוּל:


תרום ותשרף – just as if the pure heave-offering was neutralized and eaten, so too, that which is ritually impure should be separated as heave-offering and burned. And that being burned is similar to that rotting, for it is prohibited to benefit from it at the time when you burn it, for if you permitted it be able to derive benefit from it, you might come to eat it, for he holds that it is permitted through renunciation.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 5:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/5:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/5:2:3)


**תַּעֲלֶה וְתֵאָכֵל נִקּוּדִים.** בְּיֹבֶשׁ, כֻּלֵּיהּ כִּדְפָרְשִׁינַן לְעֵיל. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


תעלה ותאכל נקודים – as dry stuff, all of this as we have explained above (in Mishnah 1 of this chapter), and the Halakha is according to the Sages.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 5:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/5:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/5:3:1)


**תַּעֲלֶה וְתֵאָכֵל.** בְּהָא מוֹדֶה רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר דְּאַף אוֹתָהּ סְאָה נֶאֱכֶלֶת, מִשּׁוּם דִּלְעֵיל תְּרוּמָה טְמֵאָה נָפְלָה, אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁנִּתְבַּטְּלָה בַּחֻלִּין הַטְּהוֹרִים, מִכָּל מָקוֹם כְּשֶׁמַּגְבִּיהָהּ שֵׁם תְּרוּמָה עָלֶיהָ וְחוֹזֶרֶת לִשְׁמָהּ הָרִאשׁוֹן. אֲבָל הָכָא דִּטְהוֹרָה נָפְלָה, אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהַחֻלִּין הָיוּ טְמֵאִים, כְּשֶׁמַּגְבִּיהָהּ חָזְרָה לְשֵׁם תְּרוּמָה טְהוֹרָה כְּמוֹ שֶׁהָיְתָה:


תעלה ותאכל – In this, Rabbi Eliezer agrees that even that Seah is eaten, because of that above of ritually impure heave-offering that fell, even though it was suspended in pure heave-offering, nevertheless, when he causes heave-offering to be collected and it returns to its first designation, but here, when the pure [heave-offering] fell, even though the heave offering was ritually impure, when he collects it, it returned to the designation fo ritually pure heave-offering as it was.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 5:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/5:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/5:4:1)


**בֵּית שַׁמַּאי אוֹסְרִים.** דְּסָבְרֵי תְּרוּמָה טְמֵאָה שֶׁנִּתְעָרְבָה בִּטְהוֹרָה אֵינָהּ עוֹלָה בְּאֶחָד וּמֵאָה, כְּדִמְפָרֵשׁ טַעֲמָא לְקַמָּן:


ב"ש אוסרים – who (i.e., the School of Shammai) holds that impure heave-offering that was mixed with pure [heave-offering] is not neutralized in one and one-hundred, as we explain the reason further on.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 5:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/5:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/5:4:2)


**לְאַחַר שֶׁהוֹדוּ.** בֵּית שַׁמַּאי לְדִבְרֵי בֵית הִלֵּל. מִשּׁוּם דְּהִקְשׁוּ לָהֶם בֵּית הִלֵּל לְבֵית שַׁמַּאי, וּמַה תְּרוּמָה טְהוֹרָה שֶׁהִיא בְּמִיתָה אֵצֶל הַזָּרִים עוֹלָה, טְמֵאָה שֶׁהִיא בַּעֲשֵׂה אֵצֶל כֹּהֵן לֹא כָּל שֶׁכֵּן, וְחָזְרוּ בֵית שַׁמַּאי לְהוֹרוֹת כְּדִבְרֵי בֵית הִלֵּל:


לאחר שהודו – the School of Shammai to the words of the School of Hillel. Because the School of Hillel raised a difficulty to the School of Shammai: If evfen iturally pure heave-offering [which is punishable] by death [if eaten] by foreigners (i.e., non-Kohanim) is neutralized, impure [heave-offering which is a positive-commandment regarding a Kohen, is it not obvious? And the School of Shammai retracted to teach according to the words of the School of Hillel.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 5:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/5:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/5:4:3)


**תֵּרוֹם וְתִשָּׂרֵף.** וְהַשְּׁאָר מֻתָּר:


תרום ותשרף – and the rest is permitted.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 5:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/5:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/5:4:4)


**אָבְדָה בְמִעוּטָהּ.** כֵּיוָן דְּהַכֹּל תְּרוּמָה וְאֵין כָּאן גֶּזֶל הַשֵּׁבֶט, אֲפִלּוּ לִתְרֹם אֵין צָרִיךְ. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


אבדה במעוטה – since it is all heave-offering, and there isn’t an injustice to the tribe [of Levi] even to elevate it [as a heave-offering] is unnecessary. And the Halakha is according to the Sages.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 5:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/5:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/5:5:1)


**כִּתְרוּמָה וַדַּאי.** רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר לְטַעֲמֵיהּ דְּאָמַר שֶׁאֲנִי אוֹמֵר סְאָה שֶׁנָּפְלָה הִיא סְאָה שֶׁעָלְתָה:


כתרומה ודאי – Rabbi Eliezer, according to his reasoning who said that I state that a Seah that fell is a Seah that was neutralized.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 5:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/5:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/5:5:2)


**אֵינָהּ מְדַמַּעַת אֶלָּא לְפִי חֶשְׁבּוֹן.** וְהַסְּאָה שֶׁהִגְבִּיהַּ אֵין בָּהּ אֶלָּא חֵלֶק אֶחָד מִמֵּאָה תְּרוּמָה, וְהַשְּׁאָר חֻלִּין:


אינה מדמע אלא לפי חשבון – and the Seah that he lifted up, only has one part from one-hundred of heave-offering, and the rest is unconsecrated produce.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 5:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/5:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/5:6:1)


**אֵין הַמְּדֻמָּע מְדַמֵּעַ אֶלָּא לְפִי חֶשְׁבּוֹן.** לְפִי מַה שֶּׁיֵּשׁ תְּרוּמָה בִּסְאָה זוֹ שֶׁל דִּמּוּעַ בָּעִינַן מֵאָה מִן הַחֻלִּין, וְלֹא בָעִינַן מֵאָה סְאִין כְּנֶגֶד כָּל אוֹתָהּ סְאָה שֶׁל דִּמּוּעַ, שֶׁאֵינָהּ נֶחֱשֶׁבֶת כֻּלָּהּ תְּרוּמָה לֶאֱסֹר חֻלִּין שְׁנִיִּים:


אין המדומע מדמע אלא לפי חשבון – because what that is Terumah/heave-offering in this Seah of mixed heave offering and unconsecrated produce we require one-hundred of the unconsecrated produce, but we don’t require one-hundred Seah corresponding all of that Seah of mixed heave-offering and unconsecrated produce , which is not considered completely Terumah/heave-offering to prohibit secondary unconsecrated produce.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 5:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/5:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/5:6:2)


**וְאֵין הַמְחֻמָּץ מַחְמִיץ אֶלָּא לְפִי חֶשְׁבּוֹן.** עִסָּה שֶׁל חֻלִּין שֶׁנִּתְחַמְּצָה בִּשְׂאֹר שֶׁל תְּרוּמָה הֲרֵי כֻלָּהּ אֲסוּרָה לְזָרִים, וְאִם נָפַל מֵאוֹתָהּ עִסָּה לְתוֹךְ עִסָּה אַחֶרֶת שֶׁל חֻלִּין וְחִמְצָתָהּ אֵינָהּ אוֹסֶרֶת אֶלָּא לְפִי חֶשְׁבּוֹן שְׂאֹר שֶׁל תְּרוּמָה שֶׁמְּעֹרָב בָּהּ, וְלֹא מִתְּסְרָה אַחֲרוֹנָה אֶלָּא אִם כֵּן נָפַל מִן הָרִאשׁוֹנָה שִׁעוּר גָּדוֹל כָּל כָּךְ שֶׁיֵּשׁ בַּשְּׂאֹר שֶׁל תְּרוּמָה הַמְעֹרָב בָּהּ כְּדֵי לְחַמֵּץ הָאַחֲרוֹנָה בְּלֹא צֵרוּף הַחֻלִּין הַמְדֻמָּעִין:


ואין המחומץ מחמץ אלא לפי חשבון – started dough of unconsecrated produce that had leavened in leaven of heave-offering, it is all forbidden to foreigners (i.e., non-Kohanim) and if there fell from that started dough into another piece of stared dough of heave-offering and became leavened, it is not forbidden other than according to the calculation [of the quantity of true heave-offering contained in the mixture] of the measure of leave of Terumah that is mixed in it, and does not forbid another piece unless there fell from the first a measure so large that there is in the leaven of Terumah that is combined in it in order to leave the later piece without combination of the unconsecrated produce that is combined with heave-offering.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 5:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/5:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/5:6:3)


**וְאֵין מַיִם שְׁאוּבִים פּוֹסְלִים אֶת הַמִּקְוֶה אֶלָּא לְפִי חֶשְׁבּוֹן.** מִקְוֶה שֶׁיֵּשׁ בּוֹ עֶשְׂרִים וְאֶחָד סְאִים מֵי גְשָׁמִים, מְמַלֵּא בַּכָּתֵף תְּשַׁע עֶשְׂרֵה סְאִים וּפוֹתְקָן לַמִּקְוֶה דֶּרֶךְ הַמְשָׁכָה וְהֵן טְהוֹרִין. וְאַף עַל גַּב דִּשְׁלֹשָׁה לֻגִּין מַיִם שְׁאוּבִים פּוֹסְלִים אֶת הַמִּקְוֶה, הַשְּׁאִיבָה כְּשֵׁרָה כְּשֶׁהִיא דֶּרֶךְ הַמְשָׁכָה וְהָיָה שָׁם רֹב שִׁעוּר הַמִּקְוֶה שֶׁהֵם עֶשְׂרִים וְאֶחָד סְאִים מֵי גְשָׁמִים. וְהַיְנוּ לְפִי חֶשְׁבּוֹן, דְּאֵין הַשְּׁאוּבִים פּוֹסְלִין הַמִּקְוֶה כְּשֶׁהֵן דֶּרֶךְ הַמְשָׁכָה אֶלָּא אִם כֵּן הֵם עֶשְׂרִים [סְאִים] מַיִם שְׁאוּבִים, שֶׁאֵין שָׁם רֹב שִׁעוּר מִקְוֶה מִמֵּי גְשָׁמִים. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


ואין מים שאובים פוסלים את המקוה אלא לפי חשבון – A Mikveh/ritual bath that has in it twenty-one Seah of rain water, we will fill/draw on its shoulder nineteen [additional] Seah and let it run through a conducting channel/gutter into the Mikveh (see Tractate Temurah 12b) and they are pure, and even though three Seah of drawn waters disqualify a Mikveh, the drawn waters are kosher when it is through a conducting channel it was there that most of the measure of the Mikveh, which is twenty-one Seah of rain water, according to the calculation, for the drawn waters do not disqualify the Mikveh when they are going through a conducting channel unless there are twenty [Seah] of drawn water, for isn’t there the majority of the measure of the Mikveh from rain waters. And the Halakha is according to the Sages.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 5:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/5:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/5:7:1)


**הִגְבִּיהָהּ.** כְּדִין תְּרוּמָה שֶׁעוֹלָה בְּאֶחָד וּמֵאָה:


הגביהה – according to the law of heave-offering that is neutralized in one-and one-hundred.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 5:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/5:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/5:7:2)


**הֲרֵי זוֹ מֻתֶּרֶת.** וְצָרִיךְ לְהָרִים:


הרי זו מותרת – and one needs to lift it up [out of the mixture].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 5:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/5:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/5:7:3)


**עַד שֶׁתִּרְבֶּה תְרוּמָה עַל הַחֻלִּין.** דְּהַיְנוּ עַד שֶׁיִּפְּלוּ שָׁם חֲמִשִּׁים וְאֶחָד שֶׁל תְּרוּמָה, אֲבָל מֶחֱצָה עַל מֶחֱצָה שָׁרֵי:


עד שתרבה תרומה על החולין – that is, until there would fall there fifty-one [parts] of heave-offering/Terumah, but half by half is permitted.



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 5:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/5:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/5:8:1)


**עַד שֶׁנָּפְלָה אַחֶרֶת.** סְאָה אַחֶרֶת שֶׁל תְּרוּמָה:


עד שנפלה אחרת – another Seah of heave-offering/Terumah.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 5:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/5:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/5:8:2)


**הֲרֵי זוֹ אֲסוּרָה.** דִּכְמוֹ שֶׁנָּפְלוּ בְּבַת אַחַת דָּמִי:


הרי זו אסורה – that it is like they fell at one time.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 5:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/5:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/5:8:3)


**וְרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן מַתִּיר.** כְּשֶׁלֹּא נוֹדַע לוֹ נְפִילָה רִאשׁוֹנָה עַד שֶׁנָּפְלָה שְׁנִיָּה מוֹדֶה רַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן דִּכְמַאן דְּנָפְלוּ בְּבַת אַחַת דָּמִי. כִּי פָּלֵיג הֵיכָא דְנוֹדַע לוֹ בֵּינְתַּיִם וְלֹא הִסְפִּיק לְהָרִים עַד שֶׁנָּפְלָה אַחֶרֶת, רַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן סָבַר כֵּיוָן דְּעוֹמֵד לְהָרִים כְּמוּרָם דָּמִי, וְאַזְדָּא לְטַעֲמֵיהּ דְּאִית לֵיהּ בְּעָלְמָא (פסחים יג: בבא קמא עו:) כָּל הָעוֹמֵד לִזָּרֵק כְּזָרוּק דָּמִי, וַחֲכָמִים אוֹמְרִים הַשְׁתָּא מִיהַת לֹא הוּרְמָה. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


ור' שמעון מתיר – and it was known known to about first falling until it fell a second time, Rabbi Shimon admits that it is like the case when they fell at the same time. But he disputes where it was known to him in the meantime and he did not have the opportunity to lift it up until another fell, Rabbi Shimon holds that since he was about to lift it up is like something that has been lifted up, and he goes according to his reasoning , that he holds in general (Tractate Pesahim 13b; Tractate Bava Kamma 76b): He who is about to be sprinkled is considered as if it was sprinkled, but the Sages state that now, hoever, it was not lifted, and the Halakha is according to the Sages.



### Mishnah 9

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 5:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/5:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/5:9:1)


**וּטְחָנָן.** כֻּלָּן בְּבַת אַחַת:


וטחנן – all of it [was grounded] at one time.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 5:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/5:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/5:9:2)


**וּפָחָתוּ.** לֹא אָמְרִינַן חֻלִּין פָּחָתוּ וְלֹא הַתְּרוּמָה וְאֵין כָּאן מֵאָה וְאֶחָד וְאָסוּר:


ופתחו – and we don’t say that unconsecrated produced lessened but not the heave-offering, and there isn’t one-and one-hundred [parts neutralizing it] and it is forbidden.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 5:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/5:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/5:9:3)


**וְאִם יָדוּעַ שֶׁהַחִטִּים שֶׁל חֻלִּין יָפוֹת מִשֶּׁל תְּרוּמָה מֻתָּר.** דְּשֶׁל חֻלִּין הוֹתִירוּ וְשֶׁל תְּרוּמָה לֹא הוֹתִירוּ, וְיֵשׁ כָּאן אֶחָד וּמֵאָה. וְאַף עַל גַּב דְּמֵעִקָּרָא נֶאֶסְרוּ, חָזְרוּ וְהֻתְּרוּ בַּטְּחִינָה, וַאֲפִלּוּ לְכַתְּחִלָּה יָכוֹל לִטְחֹן כְּדֵי לְהַתִּירָן:


ואם ידוע שהחטים של חוליין יפות משל תרומה מותר -, and those of the unconsecrated produce increased [in amount] but those of heave-offering did not increase, and there is here one-and one-hundred, but even though that initially they were forbidden, they returned and became increased through grinding, and even ab initio, he is able to grind it in order to permit them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 5:9:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/5:9:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/5:9:4)


**וְאִם מֵזִיד אָסוּר.** קְנָסוּהוּ רַבָּנָן לְפִי שֶׁבִּטֵּל אִסּוּר לְכַתְּחִלָּה, וְהוּא הַדִּין לְכָל אִסּוּרִים שֶׁבַּתּוֹרָה שֶׁבִּטְּלָן בְּמֵזִיד, שֶׁהֵן אֲסוּרִים:


ואם מזיד אסור – the Rabbis fined him because he nullified a prohibition ab initio, and the same law applies to all of the prohibitions in the Torah that were nullified willfully that are prohibited.



## Chapter 6


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 6:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/6:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/6:1:1)


**הָאוֹכֵל תְּרוּמָה שׁוֹגֵג מְשַׁלֵּם קֶרֶן וְחֹמֶשׁ.** כְּדִכְתִיב (ויקרא כב) כִּי יֹאכַל קֹדֶשׁ בִּשְׁגָגָה וְיָסַף חֲמִשִּׁיתוֹ עָלָיו. וְהַחֹמֶשׁ הוּא רְבִיעִית מַה שֶּׁאָכַל, כְּגוֹן אִם אָכַל תְּרוּמָה שֶׁשָּׁוָה דִּינָר מְשַׁלֵּם דִּינָר וּרְבִיעַ, שֶׁהֵם בֵּין הַכֹּל חֲמִשָּׁה רְבִיעִיּוֹת דִּינָר, נִמְצָא הַקֶּרֶן עִם חֻמְשׁוֹ חֲמִשָּׁה. וְכָל חֳמָשִׁים הָאֲמוּרִים בַּתּוֹרָה כָּךְ הֵם:


האוכל תרומה שוגג משלם קרן וחומש – as it is written (Leviticus 22:14): “but if a man eats a sacred donation unwittingly, he shall pay the priest for the sacred donation, adding one-fifth of its value.” And the one-fifth is one quarter of what he ate, as for example, if he ate heave-offering that was worth a Denar, he pays a Deinar and one-quarter, which are amidst all, five-fourths Denar, it is found that the principle with its fifth is five, and all the added-fifths mentioned in the Torah are such.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 6:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/6:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/6:1:2)


**אֶחָד הַשּׁוֹתֶה.** יַיִן:


אחד השותה – [who drinks] wine.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 6:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/6:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/6:1:3)


**וְאֶחָד הַסָּךְ.** שֶׁמֶן. דִּשְׁתִיָּה בִּכְלַל אֲכִילָה דִּכְתִיב (דברים יד) בַּבָּקָר וּבַצֹּאן וּבַיַּיִן וּבַשֵּׁכָר, וּכְתִיב בַּתְרֵיהּ וְאָכַלְתָּ שָׁם. וְסִיכָה כִּשְׁתִיָּה דִּכְתִיב (תהלים קט) וַתָּבֹא כַמַּיִם בְּקִרְבּוֹ וְכַשֶּׁמֶן בְּעַצְמוֹתָיו:


ואחד הסך – [who anoints] with oil. For drinking is included within eating, as it is written (Deuteronomy 14:26): “[and spend the money on anything you want -] cattle, sheep, wine, or any other intoxicant,” and it is written afterwards (ibid.,) “and you shall feast there.” And anointing is like drinking, as it is written (Psalms 109:18): “May it enter his body like water, his bones like oil.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 6:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/6:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/6:1:4)


**וְחֹמֶשׁ חֻמְשָׁהּ.** שֶׁאִם אָכַל תְּרוּמָה וְשִׁלֵּם קֶרֶן וְחֹמֶשׁ, וְחָזַר וְאָכַל תַּשְׁלוּמֵי הַחֹמֶשׁ, מוֹסִיף חֹמֶשׁ עַל אוֹתוֹ חֹמֶשׁ:


וחומש חומשה – for if he ate heave-offering and paid the principal and added-fifth, and went back and ate the payment of the fifth, he adds a fifth on that same fifth.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 6:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/6:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/6:1:5)


**אֵינוֹ מְשַׁלֵּם תְּרוּמָה.** שֶׁהַתְּרוּמָה לַכֹּהֵן, וְתַשְׁלוּמֵי מַה שֶּׁאָכַל הֵם חוֹב עָלָיו, וְאֵינוֹ יָכוֹל לִפְרֹעַ חוֹבוֹ מִמָּקוֹם אַחֵר:


אינו משלם תרומה – for the heave-offering is for the Kohen, and the payments of what he ate are an obligation upon hi, and he is not able to pays his obligation from another place.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 6:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/6:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/6:1:6)


**אֶלָּא חֻלִּין מְתֻקָּנִים.** שֶׁהִפְרִישׁוּ מֵהֶן תְּרוּמוֹת וּמַעַשְׂרוֹת, דְּרַחֲמָנָא אָמַר (ויקרא כב) וְנָתַן לַכֹּהֵן אֶת הַקֹּדֶשׁ, דָּבָר הָרָאוּי לִהְיוֹת קֹדֶשׁ. וְחֹמֶשׁ נַמִּי צָרִיךְ לִהְיוֹת מֵחֻלִּין מְתֻקָּנִים, דִּכְתִיב (שם) וְיָסַף חֲמִשִּׁיתוֹ עָלָיו, מְלַמֵּד שֶׁחֻמְּשׁוֹ כְּמוֹתוֹ:


אלא חולין מתקונים – tha they separated from them heave-offerings and tithes, for the All-Merciful stated (Leviticus 22:14): “He shall pay the priest for the sacred donation,” that things which is appropriate to me holy. And the added fifth also needs to come from designated unconsecrated produce , as it is written (Leviticus 22:14): “adding one-fifth of its value,” teaching that its added fifth is like it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 6:1:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/6:1:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/6:1:7)


**וְהֵן נַעֲשִׂין תְּרוּמָה.** אוֹתָן חֻלִּין מְתֻקָּנִים:


והן נעשין תרומה – those designated unconsecrated produce.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 6:1:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/6:1:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/6:1:8)


**וְהַתַּשְׁלוּמִין תְּרוּמָה.** שֶׁאִם חָזַר וְאָכַל בְּשׁוֹגֵג אוֹתָן חֻלִּין שֶׁנִּשְׁתַּלְּמוּ, מַה שֶּׁחוֹזֵר וּמְשַׁלֵּם תַּחְתֵּיהֶן גַּם הֵם נַעֲשִׂין תְּרוּמָה:


והתשלומין תרומה – if he went back and inadvertently ate that unconsecrated produce that had been paid, what he goes back and pays in their place, they also become heave-offering.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 6:1:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/6:1:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/6:1:9)


**אֵינוֹ מוֹחֵל.** דְּכֵיוָן דִּגְזֵרַת הַכָּתוּב הִיא דְּחַיָּב לְשַׁלֵּם דָּבָר הָרָאוּי לִהְיוֹת קֹדֶשׁ וְאֵינוֹ יָכוֹל לִפְטֹר עַצְמוֹ בְּדָמִים, אֵין הַדָּבָר תָּלוּי בַּבְּעָלִים:


אינו מוחל – for since the decree of the Biblical verse is that he is liable to pay the appropriate thing to become holy and is not able to exempt himself with money, the matter is not dependent upon the owners.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 6:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/6:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/6:2:1)


**בַּת יִשְׂרָאֵל.** שֶׁהִפְרִישָׁה תְּרוּמָה וְלֹא נָתְנָה אוֹתָהּ לַכֹּהֵן וַאֲכָלָתָהּ שׁוֹגֵג:


בת ישראל – [the daughter of an Israelite] who separated heave-offering, but did not give it to the Kohen and inadvertently ate it [herself].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 6:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/6:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/6:2:2)


**וְאַחַר כָּךְ נִשֵּׂאת לַכֹּהֵן.** וַהֲרֵי הִיא רְאוּיָה לֶאֱכֹל בִּתְרוּמָה, דְּאֵשֶׁת כֹּהֵן אוֹכֶלֶת בִּתְרוּמָה:


ואח"כ נשאת לכהן – and behold she is worthy to consume heave-offering/Terumah for the wife of a Kohen eats heave-offering/Terumah.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 6:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/6:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/6:2:3)


**מְשַׁלֶּמֶת קֶרֶן וְחֹמֶשׁ לְעַצְמָהּ.** שֶׁהֲרֵי אִם הָיְתָה אוֹתָהּ תְּרוּמָה שֶׁאָכְלָה עַכְשָׁיו בְּעַיִן לֹא הָיְתָה חַיֶּבֶת לִתְּנָהּ לַכֹּהֵן, דְּהִיא נַעֲשֵׂית כֹּהֶנֶת בְּנִשּׂוּאֶיהָ, הִלְכָּךְ עַתָּה נַמִּי הַתַּשְׁלוּמִים הֵן שֶׁלָּהּ:


משלמת קרן וחומש לעצמה – behold if that selfsame heave-offering that she ate now in its natural form, she would not be liable to give to a Kohen for she has become a Kohenet through her marriage. Therefore, now, the payments are hers.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 6:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/6:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/6:2:4)


**מְשַׁלֶּמֶת קֶרֶן לַבְּעָלִים.** כְּדִין גּוֹזֵל חֲבֵרוֹ, אֲבָל הַחֹמֶשׁ תְּעַכֵּב לְעַצְמָהּ:


משלמת קרן לבעלים – according to the law of steal from one’s colleague, but the added fifth is detained for herself.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 6:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/6:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/6:3:1)


**הוּא מְשַׁלֵּם אֶת הַקֶּרֶן.** דְּמֵי תְרוּמָה, כְּדִין גּוֹזֵל, שֶׁהֲרֵי הוּא נַעֲשָׂה גַּזְלָן עָלֶיהָ:


הוא משלם את הקרן – he value of the heave-offering, according to the law of theft for he has become a thief regarding it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 6:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/6:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/6:3:2)


**וְהֵן מְשַׁלְּמִין אֶת הַחֹמֶשׁ.** עֲוֹן אֲכִילָתָן בְּשׁוֹגֵג, שֶׁאֵין מְשַׁלֵּם חֹמֶשׁ אֶלָּא אוֹכֵל וְשׁוֹתֶה וְסָךְ עַצְמוֹ, אֲבָל הַמַּזִּיק אֶת הַתְּרוּמָה אֵינוֹ מְשַׁלֵּם חֹמֶשׁ, דִּכְתִיב (ויקרא כב) וְאִישׁ כִּי יֹאכַל קֹדֶשׁ, פְּרָט לְמַזִּיק:


והן משלמין את החומש – the transgression of their eating inadvertently, but they do not pay the added firth, but rather eat and drink and anoint himself , but the person who damages the heave-offering does not pay the added fifth, as it is written (Leviticus 22:14): “But if a man eats of a sacred donation unwittingly, [he shall pay the priest for the sacred donation, adding one-fifth of its value], excluding the one who causes damage.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 6:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/6:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/6:3:3)


**הֵן מְשַׁלְּמִין קֶרֶן וְחֹמֶשׁ.** חֻלִּין מְתֻקָּנִים, וְיֵעָשׂוּ תְרוּמָה, וְהוּא מְשַׁלֵּם לָהֶם דְּמֵי חֻלִּין שֶׁהָיָה צָרִיךְ לְהַאֲכִילָן. וְאִיכָּא בֵּינַיְהוּ דִּלְרַבִּי מֵאִיר אֵין הַמַּאֲכִיל מְשַׁלֵּם אֶלָּא דְּמֵי תְרוּמָה, וּלְרַבָּנָן מְשַׁלֵּם דְּמֵי חֻלִּין:


הן משלמין קרן וחומש – properly designated unconsecrated produce, and they should make it Terumah, and he pays them the value of the unconsecrated produce that he would have had to feed them. But there is a difference between them, for the person who feeds does not pay anything ought the value of the heave-offering, but according to the Rabbis, he pays the value of the unconsecrated produce.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 6:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/6:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/6:4:1)


**דְּמֵי תְרוּמָה.** שֶׁאֵין דָּמֶיהָ יְקָרִים כְּחֻלִּין:


דמי תרומה – for its value is not expensive like unconsecrated produce.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 6:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/6:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/6:4:2)


**קֶרֶן וְחֹמֶשׁ מִן הַחֻלִּין.** וְהֵן נַעֲשִׂין תְּרוּמָה, כְּדִין כָּל אוֹכֵל תְּרוּמָה בִּשְׁגָגָה:


וחומש מן החולין – and they are made heave-offering, like the law of all who eat heave-offering inadvertently.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 6:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/6:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/6:4:3)


**וְקֶרֶן דְּמֵי תְרוּמָה.** מִשּׁוּם כֶּפֶל לַגַּנָּב:


וקרן דמי תרומה – because of the double [fine] to the thief.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 6:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/6:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/6:4:4)


**תְּרוּמַת הֶקְדֵּשׁ.** שֶׁהִקְדִּישׁ הַכֹּהֵן תְּרוּמָה לְבֶדֶק הַבַּיִת:


תרומת הקדש – that the Kohen dedicated for Temple repair.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 6:4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/6:4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/6:4:5)


**שְׁנֵי חֳמָשִׁים.** חַד מִשּׁוּם אוֹכֵל תְּרוּמָה, וְחַד מִשּׁוּם נֶהֱנֶה מִן הַהֶקְדֵּשׁ. אֲבָל כֶּפֶל בְּהֶקְדֵּשׁ לֵיכָּא, דִּכְתִיב (שמות כב) יְשַׁלֵּם שְׁנַיִם לְרֵעֵהוּ, וְלֹא לַהֶקְדֵּשׁ:


שני חומשים – one because he at heave-offering, and one because he benefited from that which was dedicated to the Temple, but there is no double payment for dedication to the Temple, as it is written (Exodus 22:8): “he whom God declares guilty shall pay double to the other,” but not to the Temple.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 6:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/6:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/6:5:1)


**מִן הַלֶּקֶט מִן הַשִּׁכְחָה וּמִן הַפֵּאָה.** מִשּׁוּם דְּאֵין בָּהֶם זִיקַת תְּרוּמָה וּמַעַשְׂרוֹת, הִלְכָּךְ לֹא הֲוֵי בִּכְלַל דָּבָר הָרָאוּי לִהְיוֹת קֹדֶשׁ. וְלֹא דָמִי לְחֻלִּין מְתֻקָּנִים, דְּהַנְהוּ הָיוּ רְאוּיִן לִהְיוֹת קֹדֶשׁ קֹדֶם שֶׁנִּתְקְנוּ, אֲבָל לֶקֶט שִׁכְחָה וּפֵאָה מֵעוֹלָם לֹא נִרְאוּ לִהְיוֹת קֹדֶשׁ:


מן הלקט ומן השכחה ומן הפאה – because they do not have in them the obligation [to pay] heave-offering/Terumah and tithes, therefore, they are not within the general thing that is appropriate to become holy, and they are not similar to designated unconsecrated produce for these were appropriate to being holy prior to being designated/fixed, but gleanings and that which was forgotten and the corner [of the field] did not appear to be holy.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 6:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/6:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/6:5:2)


**וּמִן הַהֶפְקֵר.** לְאַחַר שֶׁזָּכָה בוֹ:


ומן ההפקר – for after he took possession of it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 6:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/6:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/6:5:3)


**וְלֹא מִמַּעֲשֵׂר רִאשׁוֹן שֶׁנִּטְּלָה תְרוּמָתוֹ.** וְאַף עַל גַּב דְּהַשְׁתָּא הֲוֵי כְּחֻלִּין הַמְתֻקָּנִים, כֵּיוָן דְּקֹדֶם שֶׁנִּטְּלָה תְרוּמָתוֹ לֹא חָזֵי, הַשְׁתָּא נַמִּי לֹא חָזֵי:


ולא ממעשר ראשון שנטלה תרומתו – for even though that now, they are like designated/fixed unconsecrated produce, since it precedes taking its heave-offering it was not appropriate, it is also not not appropriate.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 6:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/6:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/6:5:4)


**שֶׁאֵין הֶקְדֵּשׁ פּוֹדֶה הֶקְדֵּשׁ.** וְאֵלּוּ חֲשׁוּבִים כְּהֶקְדֵּשׁ קֹדֶם שֶׁנִּפְדּוּ, וְאֵין אָדָם יָכוֹל לִפְטֹר עַצְמוֹ בְּמָמוֹן גָּבוֹהַּ. וְסָבַר רַבִּי מֵאִיר מַעֲשֵׂר שֵׁנִי מָמוֹן גָּבוֹהַּ הוּא, וְכֵיוָן דְּקֹדֶם שֶׁנִּפְדּוּ לֹא חָזוּ תּוּ לֹא חָזוּ לְאַחַר שֶׁנִּפְדּוּ:


שאין הקדש פודה הקדש – and these are important like that something dedicated to the Temple prior to their being redeemed, and a person cannot exempt himself with the money belonging to “On High.” And Rabbi Meir holds that Second Tithe is the money of “On High,” and since it is prior to their being redeemed they were not appropriate, further, it is not appropriate after they are redeemed.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 6:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/6:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/6:5:5)


**וַחֲכָמִים מַתִּירִין בָּאֵלּוּ.** בְּמַעֲשֵׂר שֵׁנִי וְהֶקְדֵּשׁ שֶׁנִּפְדּוּ. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


וחכמים מתירין באלו – with Second Tithe and that dedicated to the Temple which was redeemed. And the Halakha is according to the Sages.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 6:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/6:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/6:6:1)


**שֶׁיְּשַׁלֵּם מִן הַיָּפֶה עַל הָרָע.** כְּגוֹן שֶׁאָכַל גְּרוֹגָרוֹת, וּלְפִי מִדָּה שֶׁאָכַל גְּרוֹגָרוֹת יְשַׁלֵּם תְּמָרִים, וְלֹא לְפִי דָמִים, דְּכֵיוָן דִּבְזוּזָא אָכַל וּבְזוּזָא מְשַׁלֵּם, מַאי נַפְקָא מִנָּהּ שֶׁיְּשַׁלֵּם מִן הַיָּפֶה עַל הָרָע. וְאִיכָּא לְמַאן דְּאָמַר דַּאֲפִלּוּ לְפִי דָמִים מָצִינַן לְמֵימַר דְּאִי אָכַל מִידֵי דְּלֹא קָפַץ עָלֵיהּ זָבֵינָא מְשַׁלֵּם מִידֵי דְקָפַץ עָלֵיהּ זָבֵינָא:


שישלם מן היפה על הרע – as for example, that he ate dried figs, and according to the measure that he consumed dried figs, he should pay dates, and not according to the value, for since that he consumed a Zuz’s worth, that he pays back a Zuz – what difference does it make to him if he should pay from greater choice for less choice. And here are those who state that even according to the value, we are able to say that if he ate something that purchasers don’t jump to buy, he should pay with something that purchasers jump to buy. and are not worthy for eating, but another species he is not able [to pay]; he must wait until the aftermath of the Sabbatical year and pay from cucumbers from the aftermath of the Seventh Year, but from the Seventh Year [itself] are forbidden to pay his liability, for that is similar to doing business [which is prohibited on the Sabbatical year].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 6:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/6:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/6:6:2)


**לְפִיכָךְ.** שֶׁאֵין יָכוֹל לְשַׁלֵּם מִמִּין אַחֵר, אִם אָכַל קִשּׁוּאִים שֶׁל תְּרוּמָה שֶׁל עֶרֶב שְׁבִיעִית וְאֵין קִשּׁוּאִים שֶׁל אוֹתָהּ שָׁנָה מְצוּיִים עוֹד, שֶׁכְּבָר נִתְקַשּׁוּ וְלֹא חָזוּ לַאֲכִילָה, וּמִין אַחֵר אֵינוֹ יָכוֹל לְשַׁלֵּם, צָרִיךְ לְהַמְתִּין לְמוֹצָאֵי שְׁבִיעִית וִישַׁלֵּם מִקִּשּׁוּאִין שֶׁל מוֹצָאֵי שְׁבִיעִית, דְּמִשֶּׁל שְׁבִיעִית אָסוּר לִפְרֹעַ חוֹבוֹ, דְּדָמֵי לִסְחוֹרָה:


לפיכך – he cannot pay with another species; if he ate cucumbers of terumah from the eve of the Sabbatical year and cucumbers from that year are no longer available because they have hardened and are not fit for eating, and he cannot pay with another species, he needs to wait until after the Sabbatical year and pay with cucumbers from after the Sabbatical year, because it is forbidden to pay one's debt with produce from the Sabbatical year, as it resembles doing business [with Sabbatical produce].



## Chapter 7


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 7:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/7:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/7:1:1)


**הָאוֹכֵל תְּרוּמָה מֵזִיד.** בְּלֹא הַתְרָאָה, דְּאִלּוּ אִתְרוּ בֵיהּ לוֹקֶה וְאֵינוֹ מְשַׁלֵּם. וְאַף עַל גַּב דִּבְלֹא הַתְרָאָה חַיָּב מִיתָה בִּידֵי שָׁמַיִם, אֵינָהּ פּוֹטֶרֶת מִן הַתַּשְׁלוּמִין:


האוכל תרומה מזיד – without warning, for whereas if they warned him, he is flogged and does not pay. But even thought that without warning, he is liable for death at the hands of heaven, he is not exempted from payment/indemnity.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 7:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/7:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/7:1:2)


**וְאֵינוֹ מְשַׁלֵּם אֶת הַחֹמֶשׁ.** דְּלֹא חִיְּבָה תּוֹרָה חֹמֶשׁ אֶלָּא לָאוֹכֵל תְּרוּמָה בִּשְׁגָגָה:
