ואינו משלם את החומש – for the Torah did not obligate the added fifth, other than for one who consumes heave-offering inadvertently.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 7:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/7:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/7:1:3)


**הַתַּשְׁלוּמִין חֻלִּין.** דְּתַשְׁלוּמִין דְּשׁוֹגֵג בִּלְבָד קָרִינְהוּ רַחֲמָנָא קֹדֶשׁ, כְּדִכְתִיב וְנָתַן לַכֹּהֵן אֶת הַקֹּדֶשׁ, אֲבָל תַּשְׁלוּמִין דְּמֵזִיד חֻלִּין הֵן, וְהוֹאִיל וְהֵן חֻלִּין אִם רָצָה הַכֹּהֵן לִמְחֹל מוֹחֵל, מַה שֶּׁאֵין כֵּן בְּתַשְׁלוּמִין שֶׁל שׁוֹגֵג שֶׁאִם רָצָה לִמְחֹל אֵינוֹ מוֹחֵל, מִפְּנֵי שֶׁהֵן קֹדֶשׁ:


התשלומין חולין – for payment of someone who acted inadvertently alone, the All-Merciful calls him “holy,” as it is written (Leviticus 22:14): “[but if a man eats of a sacred donation unwittingly,] he shall pay the priest for the sacred donation, [adding one-fifth of its value],” but payment of someone who acts willfully is unconsecrated produce, for since they are unconsecrated, if the Kohen wants to refuse, he can refuse, what is not the case with payment of a person who acts inadvertently, that if he wants to refuse it, he cannot refuse it because they are holy.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 7:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/7:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/7:2:1)


**בַּת כֹּהֵן שֶׁנִּשֵּׂאת לְיִשְׂרָאֵל.** וַאֲסוּרָה לֶאֱכֹל בִּתְרוּמָה:


בת כהן שנשאת לישראל – and she is forbidden to consume heave-offering.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 7:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/7:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/7:2:2)


**מְשַׁלֶּמֶת אֶת הַקֶּרֶן.** כְּדִין גּוֹזֵל אֶת חֲבֵרוֹ, אֲבָל לֹא חֹמֶשׁ, דְּכֹהֶנֶת הִיא, וּכְתִיב (ויקרא כב) וְכָל זָר לֹא יֹאכַל קֹדֶשׁ, יָצְאָה זֹאת שֶׁאֵינָהּ זָרָה וְאֶפְשָׁר לָהּ שֶׁתָּשׁוּב לְבֵית אָבִיהָ כִּנְעוּרֶיהָ וּתְהֵא מֻתֶּרֶת לֶאֱכֹל בִּתְרוּמָה:


משלמת את הקרן – according to the law of someone who steals from his neighbor, but not the added fifth, for she is the daughter of a Kohen, as it is written (Leviticus 22:10): “No lay person shall eat of the sacred donations,” excluding this one who is not a foreigner (for after all, she is the daughter of a Kohen), and it is possible for her that she will return to the house of her father like in her youth (i.e., if she is divorced by her husband, for example) and she would be permitted to eat heave-offering.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 7:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/7:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/7:2:3)


**וּמִיתָתָהּ בִּשְׂרֵפָה.** אִם זָנְתָה תַּחַת בַּעְלָהּ יִשְׂרָאֵל, כְּדִכְתִיב (ויקרא כא) וּבַת אִישׁ כֹּהֵן כִּי תֵחֵל לִזְנוֹת וְכוּ', כָּל שֶׁהִיא בַּת כֹּהֵן, לֹא שְׁנָא אֵשֶׁת כֹּהֵן וְלֹא שְׁנָא אֵשֶׁת יִשְׂרָאֵל, בָּאֵשׁ תִּשָּׂרֵף:


ומיתתה בשריפה – if she ran about as a prostitute underneath her Israelite husband, as it is written (Leviticus 21:9): “When the daughter of a priest defiles herself through harlotry, [it is her father whom she defiles; she shall be put to the fire],” as long as she is the daughter of a Kohen, it doesn’t matter whether the wife of a Kohen nor the wife of an Israelite, she is [put to death] through burning by fire.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 7:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/7:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/7:2:4)


**נִשֵּׂאת לְאֶחָד מִכָּל הַפְּסוּלִים.** כְּגוֹן חָלָל נָתִין וּמַמְזֵר, מְשַׁלֶּמֶת קֶרֶן וְחֹמֶשׁ, שֶׁהֲרֵי נִתְחַלְּלָה בְּבִיאָתוֹ וְאֵינָהּ רְאוּיָה עוֹד לֶאֱכֹל בִּתְרוּמָה וַהֲרֵי הִיא כְזָרָה:


נשאת לאחד מכל הפסולים – as, for example, one unfit for the priesthood on account of his father’s illegitimate connection/חלל, a descendant of the Gibeonites/נתין and an illegitimate child/ממזר, pays the principle and an added fifth, for she became degraded/desecrated through his sexual union [with her] and she is not worthy any longer to consume heave-offering, and behold, she is like a foreign woman.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 7:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/7:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/7:2:5)


**וּמִיתָתָהּ בְּחֶנֶק.** דִּכְתִיב וּבַת אִישׁ כֹּהֵן כִּי תֵחֵל לִזְנוֹת, מִי שֶׁרְאוּיָה לַחֲזֹר לְבֵית אָבִיהָ אִלְמָלֵא זְנוּת זֶה הִיא בִשְׂרֵפָה, יָצְתָה נִבְעֶלֶת לְפָסוּל לָהּ שֶׁאֵינָהּ רְאוּיָה לַחֲזֹר לְבֵית אָבִיהָ, שֶׁזּוֹ אֵינָהּ בִּשְׂרֵפָה:


ומיתתה בחנק – as it is written (Leviicus 21:9): “When the daughter of a priest defiles herself though harlotry.” Whomever is worthy of returning to her father’s house, if it were not for this prostitution, she [would be punished] by fire/burning, excluding if she had sexual intercourse with someone who is unfit for her where she is not worthy to return to her father’s house, for this is not [punishable] through burning.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 7:2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/7:2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/7:2:6)


**וַחֲכָמִים אוֹמְרִים וְכוּ'.** דְּלֹא הַוְיָא זָרָה עַד שֶׁתְּהֵא זָרָה מִתְּחִלָּתָהּ וְעַד סוֹפָהּ, וְזוֹ הוֹאִיל וּכְבָר אָכְלָה בִּתְרוּמָה אֵינָהּ מְשַׁלֶּמֶת אֶת הַחֹמֶשׁ:


וחכמים אומרים כוכ' – for she is not a “foreign woman” until she is a foreigner from her beginning until her hend, and this since she has already eaten heave-offering, she does not pay the added firth.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 7:2:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/7:2:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/7:2:7)


**וּמִיתָתָהּ בִּשְׂרֵפָה.** וּבַת אִישׁ כֹּהֵן כְּתִיב, מִכָּל מָקוֹם. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


ומיתתה בשריפה – “when the daughter of a priest” (Leviticus 21:9) is written, anyway. And he Halakha is according to the Sages.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 7:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/7:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/7:3:1)


**הַמַּאֲכִיל אֶת בָּנָיו קְטַנִּים.** דְּלָאו בְּנֵי חִיּוּבָא נִינְהוּ, וַעֲבָדָיו אֲפִלּוּ גְדוֹלִים:


המאכיל את בניו הקטנים – for these [minor-age children] are not liable, and his slaves, even if they are adults.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 7:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/7:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/7:3:2)


**וּתְרוּמַת חוּץ לָאָרֶץ.** אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהִיא מְדַמַּעַת, אֵין מוֹסִיפִין חֹמֶשׁ עָלֶיהָ:


ותרומה חוץ לארץ – even though it is subject to the laws of heave-offering/Terumah, we don’t add the added-fifth [payment] to it.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 7:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/7:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/7:5:1)


**לְתוֹךְ שֶׁל תְּרוּמָה נָפְלָה.** וְשֶׁל חֻלִּין מֻתֶּרֶת. וְדַוְקָא בִּשְׁתֵּי קֻפּוֹת אַחַת שֶׁל חֻלִּין וְאַחַת שֶׁל תְּרוּמָה, תָּלִינַן תְּרוּמָה לְתוֹךְ תְּרוּמָה נָפְלָה, שֶׁיֵּשׁ הֶתֵּר לִשְׁתֵּי הַקֻּפּוֹת. אֲבָל אַחַת חֻלִּין וְאַחַת טֶבֶל אוֹ מַעֲשֵׂר רִאשׁוֹן וְכַיּוֹצֵא בָזֶה, לֹא תָלִינַן שֶׁנָּפְלָה הַתְּרוּמָה לַטֶּבֶל כְּדֵי לְהַתִּיר הַחֻלִּין, מֵאַחַר שֶׁהַטֶּבֶל נִדְמַע וְנֶאֱסַר, דְּמַאי חָזֵית לֶאֱסֹר קֻפָּה זוֹ יוֹתֵר מִזּוֹ. וְכֵן טְמֵאָה וּטְהוֹרָה תָּלִינַן בַּטְּמֵאָה, אוֹ מְדֻמַּעַת וְשֶׁאֵינָהּ מְדֻמַּעַת תָּלִינַן בַּמְּדֻמַּעַת. כְּלָלָא דְמִלְּתָא, כָּל הֵיכָא דְּאַחַת אֵינָהּ מִתְקַלְקֶלֶת, תָּלִינַן בָּהּ:


לתך על תרומה נפלה – and that of heave-offering is permitted, and specifically, with two bins, one of unconsecrated produce and one of heave-offering. We suspend heave-offering into heave-offering that fell that has a permission for two bins. But if one of them is unconsecrated produce and one of them are eatables forbidden pending the separation of sacred gifts/טבל, or First Tithe and similar kinds of things, we do not suspend that the heave-offering fell into eatables forbidden pending the separation of sacred gifts in order to permit the unconsecrated produce, for after that the eatables forbidden pending the separation of sacred gifts became totally prohibited to non-priests, and for why do you see it appropriate to prohibit this bin more than that one? And similarly, with ritually impure or ritually pure, we suspend with ritually impure or with something completely forbidden to non-Kohanim and that which is not completely forbidden to non-Kohanim we suspend with that which is completely forbidden to non-Kohanim? The general principle of the matter is that wherever that one is not spoiled/ruined, we suspend it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 7:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/7:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/7:5:2)


**אָכַל אַחַת מֵהֶן פָּטוּר.** דְּשֶׁמָּא שֶׁל חֻלִּין אָכַל:


אבל אחת מהן פטור – for perhaps he ate from unconsecrated produce.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 7:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/7:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/7:5:3)


**וְחַיֶּבֶת בְּחַלָּה.** דְּסָפֵק חֻלִּין הִיא:


וחייבת בחלה – for it is doubtful unconsecrated produce.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 7:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/7:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/7:5:4)


**וְרַבִּי יוֹסֵי פּוֹטֵר.** דְּכֵיוָן דְּלֹא יְדִיעַ הֲוָה לֵיהּ כִּמְדֻמָּע דְּפָטוּר מִן הַחַלָּה. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי בְּכָל הַנֵּי תְּלָתָא בָּבֵי דְמַתְנִיתִין:


ורבי יוסי פוטר – for since he did not know it would be like something forbidden to non-Kohanim (i.e., the that which is Terumah mixed with unconsecrated produce) , which is exempt from Hallah. And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yosi in all three segments of our Mishnah.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 7:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/7:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/7:5:5)


**אָכַל.** אָדָם אַחֵר אֶת הַשְּׁנִיָּה, פָּטוּר:


אכל – another person ate from the second [bin], he is exempt.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 7:5:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/7:5:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/7:5:6)


**כַּקְּטַנָּה שֶׁבִּשְׁנֵיהֶן.** לְהָקֵל:


כקטנה שבשניהם – to be lenient.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 7:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/7:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/7:6:1)


**אֵינָהּ מְדַמַּעַת.** דְּשֶׁמָּא הַיְנוּ אוֹתָהּ שֶׁל חֻלִּין:


אינה מדמעת – for perhaps it is identical with that of unconsecrated produce.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 7:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/7:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/7:7:1)


**זָרַע אַחַת מֵהֶן פָּטוּר.** שֶׁהַזּוֹרֵעַ אֶת הַתְּרוּמָה בְּשׁוֹגֵג צָרִיךְ לְהַפֵּךְ אֶת הַקַּרְקַע, וְאִם לֹא הִפֵּךְ, הַגִּדּוּלִים תְּרוּמָה, וְהָכָא פָּטוּר, דִּכְחֻלִּין הֵם:


זרע אחת מהן פטור – he who sows heave-offering/Terumah inadvertently, must turn over the ground, and if he did not overturn it, the large ones are Terumah/heave-offering, but here he is exempt because they are like unconsecrated produce.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 7:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/7:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/7:7:2)


**בְּדָבָר שֶׁזַּרְעוֹ כָלֶה.** כְּגוֹן חִטִּים וּשְׂעוֹרִים, מֻתָּר לְזָרִים, דְּאֵין זוֹ תְּרוּמָה וַדַּאי, וְחָשְׁבִינַן לַהּ כְּגִדּוּלֵי [מְדֻמָּע] דִּתְנַן לְקַמָּן בְּפֶרֶק ט'‏ שֶׁהֵן חֻלִּין:


בדבר שזרעו כלה (the seed does not disintegrate) – as, for example, wheat and barley; it is permitted to foreigners (i.e., non-Kohanim) for this is not definitely heave offering, but we consider it like the growth [of produce entirely forbidden to foreigners] as is taught in the Mishnah in chapter nine (see Tractate Terumot, Chapter 9, Mishnayot 5-6) that they are unconsecrated produce.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 7:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/7:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/7:7:3)


**בְּדָבָר שֶׁאֵין זַרְעוֹ כָלֶה.** כְּגוֹן שׁוּם וּבְצָלִים, אָסוּר, וְאַף עַל גַּב דְּחָשְׁבִינַן לֵיהּ כִּמְדֻמָּע וְלֹא כִתְרוּמָה, בְּדָבָר שֶׁאֵין זַרְעוֹ כָלֶה יֵשׁ לְהַחְמִיר טְפֵי:


כדבר שאין זרעו כלה – as, for example, garlic and onions which are forbidden, even though we consider it as something completely forbidden to non-priests, and not like Terumah, for in a case where the seed does not disintegrate, we must be more stringent.



## Chapter 8


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 8:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/8:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/8:1:1)


**הָאִשָּׁה.** בַּת יִשְׂרָאֵל הַנְּשׂוּאָה לְכֹהֵן:


האשה – the daughter of an Israelite married to a Kohen.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 8:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/8:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/8:1:2)


**מֵת רַבָּךְ.** וִירָשׁוֹ בֶן בִּתּוֹ מִיִּשְׂרָאֵל, אוֹ בִתּוֹ הַנְּשׂוּאָה לְיִשְׂרָאֵל:


מת רבך – and he was inherited by the son of his daughter from an Israelite or his daughter was married to an Israelite.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 8:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/8:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/8:1:3)


**שֶׁהוּא בֶן גְּרוּשָׁה אוֹ בֶן חֲלוּצָה.** וּפָסוּל מִלֶּאֱכֹל בִּתְרוּמָה:


שהוא בן גרושה או בן חלוצה – and he is unfit/disqualified from eating Terumah/heave-offering.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 8:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/8:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/8:1:4)


**רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר מְחַיֵּב קֶרֶן וְחֹמֶשׁ.** בְּכֻלְּהוּ:


רבי אליעזר מחייב קרן וחומש – in all of them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 8:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/8:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/8:1:5)


**וְרַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ פּוֹטֵר.** בַּגְּמָרָא מוֹקֵי לַהּ דַּוְקָא בְּאוֹכֵל תְּרוּמַת חָמֵץ בְּעֶרֶב פֶּסַח, מִפְּנֵי שֶׁזְּמַנּוֹ בָּהוּל, שֶׁהַשָּׁעָה דְחוּקָה וְעוֹמֵד לְבַעֵר הֶחָמֵץ שֶׁל תְּרוּמָה. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ:


ורבי יהושע פוטר – In the Gemara (Tractate Pesahim 72b; Tractate Yevamot 34a), it maintains it especially with eating leavened heave-offering on the Eve of Passover, because his time (for doing the thing) is pressed (and cannot be postponed) and the hour is pressed as he stands to remove the leavening from the Terumah/heave-offering. And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yehoshua.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 8:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/8:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/8:1:6)


**וְרַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ מַכְשִׁיר.** דִּכְתִיב (דברים לג) בָּרֵךְ ה'‏ חֵילוֹ וּפֹעַל יָדָיו תִּרְצֶה, אֲפִלּוּ חָלָל שֶׁבּוֹ פֹּעַל יָדָיו תִּרְצֶה:


ורבי יהושע מכשיר – as it is written (Deuteronomy 33:11): “Bless, O Lord, his substance, and favor his undertakings,” even one unfit for the priesthood on account of his father’s illegitimate connection, that he favor his undertakings.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 8:1:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/8:1:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/8:1:7)


**נוֹדַע שֶׁהוּא בַעַל מוּם עֲבוֹדָתוֹ פְּסוּלָה.** אַף לְרַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ, דִּכְתִיב (במדבר כח) אֶת בְּרִיתִי שָׁלוֹם, כְּשֶׁהוּא שָׁלֵם וְלֹא כְּשֶׁהוּא חָסֵר. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ:


נודע שהוא בעל מום עבודתו פסולה – even according to Rabbi Yehoshua, as it is written (Numbers 25:12 – and not chapter 28, as indicated in the printed text): “My pact of friendship/peace,” when he is complete and when he is lacking/defective. And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yehoshua.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 8:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/8:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/8:2:1)


**וְכֻלָּם.** עֶבֶד וְאִשָּׁה שֶׁאָכְלוּ מִתְּחִלָּה בְּהֶתֵּר, בְּהָא קָאָמַר רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר דְּיִבְלְעוּ, אֲבָל בֶּן גְּרוּשָׁה אוֹ בֶן חֲלוּצָה דְּמֵעוֹלָם לֹא אָכְלוּ בְּהֶתֵּר מוֹדֶה רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר דְּיִפְלֹט, מִידֵי דַהֲוָה אַטָּמֵא הָיִיתָ אוֹ טְמֵאָה הָיְתָה הַתְּרוּמָה קֹדֶם שֶׁנְּתָנָהּ לְתוֹךְ פִּיו, דְּמוֹדֶה רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר דְּיִפְלֹט:


וכולם – a slave and a woman who ate from the beginning with permission, it is in this that Rabbi Eliezer states that they should swallow it. But the son of a woman divorcee or the son of a woman who performed the ceremony of removing her deceased husband’s brother’s shoe, that forever did not eat with permission, Rabbi Eliezer admits that he should spit it out, something that he was disqualified, or the heave-offering was disqualified prior to his placing it in his mouth, as Rabbi Eliezer admits that he should spit it out.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 8:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/8:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/8:2:2)


**אוֹ שֶׁטָּעַם טַעַם פִּשְׁפֵּשׁ.** שֶׁרֶץ, כְּשֶׁמּוֹלְלִין אוֹתוֹ רֵיחוֹ מָאוּס בְּיוֹתֵר, וְהוּא מָצוּי בַּכְּתָלִים וּבַמִּטּוֹת, וְקוֹרִין לוֹ בַּעֲרָבִי בק"י וּבְלַעַ"ז צימיצ"א. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ:


אם שטעם טעם פשפש – an unclean reptile, for when they crush it, his breath is most repulsive, and it is found in the walls and in the beds and we call it B’KEE in Arabic and in the foreign language, TZIMITZA, and the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yehoshua.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 8:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/8:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/8:3:1)


**הָיָה אוֹכֵל בְּאֶשְׁכּוֹל.** תָּלַשׁ אֶשְׁכּוֹל מִגֶּפֶן שֶׁבַּגִּנָּה וְהָיָה אוֹכֵל וְהוֹלֵךְ עַד שֶׁנִּכְנַס לֶחָצֵר, וְחָצֵר קוֹבַעַת לְמַעֲשֵׂר, וַאֲפִלּוּ אֲכִילַת עֲרַאי אֲסוּרָה עַד שֶׁיִּתְרֹם וִיעַשֵּׂר:


היה אוכל באשכול – he detached a cluster from the vine that is in the garden and he would eat while walking until he entered the courtyard, for the courtyard establishes [its liability] for tithing, and even an incidental meal is forbidden until he separates heave-offering and tithes.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 8:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/8:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/8:3:2)


**רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר אוֹמֵר יִגְמֹר.** לֹא שֶׁיֹּאכַל בֶּחָצֵר. אֶלָּא יֵצֵא חוּץ לֶחָצֵר וְיִגְמֹר לֶאֱכֹל הָאֶשְׁכּוֹל בַּגִּנָּה:


ר' אליעזר אומר יגמור – not that he should eat in the courtyard, but rather, he should leave outside the courtyard and complete eating the cluster in the garden.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 8:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/8:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/8:3:3)


**לֹא יִגְמֹר.** וַאֲפִלּוּ בַּגִּנָּה, עַד שֶׁיְּעַשֵּׂר:


לא יגמור – and even in the garden, until he tithes.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 8:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/8:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/8:3:4)


**חֲשֵׁכָה לֵילֵי שַׁבָּת.** וְהָיָה אוֹכֵל אֲכִילַת עֲרַאי, וְשַׁבָּת קוֹבַעַת לְמַעֲשֵׂר וַאֲפִלּוּ אֲכִילַת עֲרַאי אֲסוּרָה:


חשיכה לילי שבת – and he was eating an incidental meal, for Shabbat establishes [liability for] tithing and even an incidental meal is prohibited.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 8:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/8:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/8:3:5)


**יִגְמֹר.** לְמוֹצָאֵי שַׁבָּת, אֲבָל בַּשַּׁבָּת עַצְמוֹ מוֹדֶה דְּאָסוּר:


יגמור – on Saturday night, but on Shabbat itself, he admits that it is prohibited.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 8:3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/8:3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/8:3:6)


**לֹא יִגְמֹר.** אֲפִלּוּ לְמוֹצָאֵי שַׁבָּת, עַד שֶׁיְּעַשֵּׂר. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ:


לא יגמור – even on Saturday night, until he tithes, and the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yehoshua.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 8:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/8:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/8:4:1)


**יִשָּׁפֵךְ.** וְאֵין כָּאן מִשּׁוּם אִבּוּד תְּרוּמָה, מִפְּנֵי שֶׁאָסוּר לִשְׁתּוֹתוֹ, שֶׁמָּא שָׁתָה מִמֶּנּוּ נָחָשׁ:


ישפך – and there isn’t here because of the destruction of heave-offering because it is forbidden to drink of it, lest a snake drank from it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 8:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/8:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/8:4:2)


**וּשְׁאָר כָּל הַמַּשְׁקִים מֻתָּרִים.** שֶׁאֵין נָחָשׁ שׁוֹתֶה מֵהֶן. אֶלָּא שֶׁהַדְּבַשׁ וְהַצִּיר וְהַמּוּרְיָס וְשׁוּמִים כְּתוּשִׁים, כָּל אֵלּוּ הֻזְכְּרוּ בַגְּמָרָא לֶאֱסֹר, שֶׁהַנָּחָשׁ שׁוֹתֶה מֵהֶן:


ושארכל המשקים מותרים – because a snake does not drink from them, but that the honey and brine and pickle (containing fish-hash and sometimes wine) and pounded garlic, all of these are mentioned in the Gemara (Tractate Hullin 49b) for prohibition, for the snake drinks from them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 8:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/8:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/8:4:3)


**כַּמָּה יִשְׁהוּ.** כַּמָּה יַעַמְדוּ מְגֻלִּין:


כמה ישתה – how much should stand uncovered.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 8:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/8:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/8:4:4)


**הָרַחַשׁ.** הַנָּחָשׁ, וּלְפִי שֶׁהוֹלֵךְ עַל גְּחוֹנוֹ קָרוּי רַחַשׁ, שֶׁאֵינוֹ נִרְאֶה כִּמְהַלֵּךְ אֶלָּא כְּרוֹחֵשׁ וּמִתְנַעְנֵעַ:


הרחש – the snake, and because it walks on its belly, it is called רחש/a creeping thing/reptile, for it does not appear like walking but rather, like moving/vibrating and shaking.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 8:4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/8:4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/8:4:5)


**מִמָּקוֹם קָרוֹב.** פֵּרְשׁוּ בוֹ (חולין י:) כְּדֵי שֶׁיֵּצֵא מִתַּחַת אֹזֶן הַכְּלִי וְיִשְׁתֶּה וְיַחֲזֹר לְחוֹרוֹ:


ממקום קרוב – the explained it (Tractate Hullin 10b), in order that it can go out from underneath the ear of he utensil, and it will drink and return to its hole.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 8:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/8:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/8:5:1)


**שִׁעוּר הַמַּיִם.** לִהְיוֹת אֲסוּרִים מִשּׁוּם גִּלּוּי:


שיעור המים – that it would be forbidden because of being uncovered.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 8:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/8:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/8:5:2)


**כְּדֵי שֶׁתֹּאבַד בָּהֶן מָרָה.** אִם הָיוּ מְרֻבִּים כָּל כָּךְ עַד שֶׁאֶרֶס הַנָּחָשׁ יִפָּסֵד וְיֹאבַד בָּהֶן אֵין בָּהֶן מִשּׁוּם גִּלּוּי. וְיוֹדְעִין הָיוּ כַּמָּה אֶרֶס הַנָּחָשׁ מַטִּיל בְּפַעַם אַחַת:


כדי שתאבד בהן מרה – if here was such a great deal that the poison of the snake would deteriorate [its venom] and would be lost in them, there would be nothing of being uncovered, and they would know how much poison a snake it puts in at one time.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 8:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/8:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/8:5:3)


**מָרָה.** הָאֶרֶס שֶׁהַנָּחָשׁ מַטִּיל קָרוּי מָרָה:


מרה – the poison that the snake puts in is called מרה/bitterness.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 8:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/8:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/8:5:4)


**בְּכֵלִים כָּל שֶׁהֵן.** אֲפִלּוּ מְרֻבִּין כָּל שֶׁהֵן יֵשׁ בָּהֶן מִשּׁוּם גִּלּוּי כְּשֶׁהַמַּיִם בַּכֵּלִים:


בכלים כל שהן – even if they are many, whatever the amount as they are, there is in them on account of be uncovered, when the water is in the utensils.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 8:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/8:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/8:5:5)


**וּבַקַּרְקָעוֹת עַד אַרְבָּעִים סְאָה.** יֵשׁ בָּהֶם מִשּׁוּם גִּלּוּי, טְפֵי מֵהָכִי אֵין בָּהֶן מִשּׁוּם גִּלּוּי, שֶׁהָאֶרֶס מִתְבַּטֵּל בָּהֶם. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי. וּמַעְיָן הַמּוֹשֵׁךְ אֲפִלּוּ כָּל שֶׁהוּא אֵין בּוֹ מִשּׁוּם גִּלּוּי. וּמַיִם מְגֻלִּים כְּשֵׁם שֶׁאָסוּר לִשְׁתּוֹתָן כָּךְ אָסוּר לְהַשְׁקוֹתָן לִבְהֶמְתּוֹ, וְלִרְחֹץ בָּהֶן פָּנָיו יָדָיו וְרַגְלָיו, וּלְגַבֵּל בָּהֶן אֶת הַטִּיט, וּלְהִשְׁתַּמֵּשׁ בָּהֶן בְּשׁוּם תַּשְׁמִישׁ:


ובקרקעות עד מ' סאה – there is in them because of being uncovered, more than this, there is none because of uncovering, for the poison is abolished in them. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yosi. But a spring that draws out even a bit, there is nothing in it of uncovering. And uncovered kwaters when they are prohibited to drink them, thusly there are forbidden to give to his cattle to drink and to wash in them his face, hands and feet, and to knead the plaster, and to use them in any manner of usage.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 8:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/8:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/8:6:1)


**נִקּוּרֵי תְאֵנִים.** תְּאֵנָה שֶׁנִּרְאֶה בָהּ נִקּוּר, אָסוּר, שֶׁמָּא נִקֵּר בָּהּ נָחָשׁ וְהִטִּיל בָּהּ אֶרֶס:


נקורי תאנים – a fig in which appears in it a biting, it is prohibited, perhaps the snake bit it and put in it poison.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 8:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/8:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/8:6:2)


**אֲפִלּוּ הֵן כִּכָּר.** כְּלוֹמַר שֶׁהֵן גְּדוֹלִים הַרְבֵּה וְהַנִּקּוּר מִצַּד אֶחָד, לֹא יֹאמַר הוּא אָכַל מִצַּד אֶחָד, אֲנִי מִצַּד אַחֵר:


אפילו הן ככר – meaning to say that they are very large and the bite is from one side, he should not say that he eats from one side and I [eat] from the other side.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 8:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/8:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/8:6:3)


**כָּל שֶׁיֶּשׁ בּוֹ לֵחָה.** הָאֶרֶס מִתְעָרֵב עִם הַלֵּחָה וּמִתְחַלְחֵל בְּכֻלּוֹ. אֲבָל בְּדָבָר יָבֵשׁ חוֹתֵךְ אֶת הַנִּקּוּר וְאוֹכֵל אֶת הַשְּׁאָר:


כל שיש בו ליחה – the poison combines with the moisture, and permeates through everything, ut in a dry thing, he cuts the bitten part and eats the rest.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 8:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/8:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/8:6:4)


**וּנְשׁוּכַת הַנָּחָשׁ.** בְּהֵמָה שֶׁנְּשָׁכָהּ נָחָשׁ וּשְׁחָטָהּ, אֲסוּרָה, שֶׁהָאֶרֶס מִתְחַלְחֵל בְּכָל גּוּפָהּ:


ונשוכת נחש – a beast/cattle that was bitten by a snake and he slaughtered it (i.e., the cattle), it is forbidden, because the poison permeates in the entire body.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 8:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/8:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/8:7:1)


**מְשַׁמֶּרֶת שֶׁל יַיִן.** אַף עַל גַּב שֶׁהַכְּלִי הַתַּחְתּוֹן מְכֻסֶּה בַּמְשַׁמֶּרֶת, יֵשׁ בּוֹ מִשּׁוּם גִּלּוּי, שֶׁהָאֶרֶס עוֹבֵר דֶּרֶךְ הַמְשַׁמֶּרֶת וְהוֹלֵךְ לַכְּלִי הַתַּחְתּוֹן:


משמרת של יין – even though the lower uensil is covered with a strainer, there is in it because of uncovering, for the poison passes along the path of the strainer and goes into the lower utensil.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 8:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/8:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/8:7:2)


**רַבִּי נְחֶמְיָה מַתִּיר.** לְפִי שֶׁהָאֶרֶס צָף וְאֵינוֹ עוֹבֵר. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי נְחֶמְיָה:


רבי נחמיה מתיר – because the poison floats and does not pass, but the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Nehemiah.



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 8:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/8:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/8:8:1)


**סְפֵק טֻמְאָה.** כְּגוֹן שְׁתֵּי חָבִיּוֹת בִּרְשׁוּת הַיָּחִיד וְנָגַע שֶׁרֶץ בְּאַחַת מֵהֶן וְלֹא יְדִיעַ לְהֵי מִנַּיְהוּ, וּשְׁתֵּיהֶן תְּלוּיוֹת:


ספק טומאה – as, for example, two jugs in the private domain, and a reptile touched one of them, and it is not known to which of them, and both of them are suspended.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 8:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/8:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/8:8:2)


**בִּמְקוֹם הַתֻּרְפָּה.** מָקוֹם שֶׁל הֶפְקֵר וְעוֹמֶדֶת לִפָּסֵד שָׁם:


במקום התורפה – a place that is ownerless and it stands to suffer loss there.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 8:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/8:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/8:8:3)


**יַנִּיחֶנָּה בְּמָקוֹם הַמֻּצְנָע.** דַּעֲדַיִן מֻזְהָר עַל שְׁמִירָתָהּ, דִּכְתִיב (במדבר יח) אֶת מִשְׁמֶרֶת תְּרוּמֹתָי, בִּשְׁתֵּי תְרוּמוֹת הַכָּתוּב מְדַבֵּר, אַחַת תְּרוּמָה טְהוֹרָה וְאַחַת תְּרוּמָה תְלוּיָה, דְּתַרְוַיְהוּ בָּעוּ שִׁמּוּר:


יניחנה במקום המוצנע – for he is still careful on its being guarded, as it is written (Numbers 18:8): “[I hereby give you] charge of all My gifts, [all the sacred donations of the Israelites],” with two heave-offerings the Biblical verse speaks – one is pure heave-offering and the other is suspended Terumah and both of them require guarding.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 8:8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/8:8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/8:8:4)


**רַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ אוֹמֵר וְכוּ'.** דְּסָבַר תְּרוּמֹתָי כְּתִיב בְּלֹא וָי"ו, וְיֵשׁ אֵם לַמָּסֹרֶת:


ר' יהושע אומר וכו' – for he holds that the word "תרומתי" – is written without the [letter] “Vav”, and there is the traditional Scriptural text (i.e., letters without vowels) is authoritative in Biblical interpretation.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 8:8:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/8:8:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/8:8:5)


**אַל יְחַדֵּשׁ בָּהּ דָּבָר.** שֶׁאֵין צָרִיךְ לְשָׁמְרָהּ, וְאַף אָסוּר לִגְרֹם לָהּ טֻמְאָה. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבָּן גַּמְלִיאֵל:


אל יחדש בה דבר – and there is no need to guard it, and it is even prohibited to cause the riual defilement, and he Halakha is according to Rabban Gamaliel.



### Mishnah 9

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 8:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/8:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/8:9:1)


**בַּגַּת הָעֶלְיוֹנָה.** מָקוֹם שֶׁעוֹצְרִים שָׁם:


בגת העליונה – place where they store it away.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 8:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/8:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/8:9:2)


**וְהַתַּחְתּוֹנָה.** בּוֹר עַל פְּנֵי הַגַּת שֶׁהַיַּיִן יוֹרֵד לְתוֹכוֹ:


והתחתונה – a pit on the inside of the vat where the wine goes into it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 8:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/8:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/8:9:3)


**טְמֵאָה.** חֻלִּין טְמֵאִים יֵשׁ בָּהּ, וּרְאוּיִין לוֹ בִּימֵי טֻמְאָתוֹ, אוֹ לְמִי שֶׁאֵינוֹ אוֹכֵל חֻלִּין בְּטָהֳרָה, וְאִם תִּפֹּל לְתוֹכוֹ הַתְּרוּמָה הֲוָה לֵיהּ מְדֻמָּע טָמֵא, וְאַף לַכֹּהֵן לֹא חַזְיָא:


טמאה – it has ritually impure unconsecrated produce, and it is appropriate for it during the days of its ritual defilement, or for someone who does not consume unconsecrated produce in ritual purity, and if the heave-offering/Terumah falls into it, he should have something impure that is forbidden to all non-priests, but even to Kohanim, it is not appropriate.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 8:9:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/8:9:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/8:9:4)


**שֶׁאִם יְכוֹלִים לְהַצִּיל מִמֶּנָּה רְבִיעִית.** אִם יָכוֹל לְחַזֵּר אַחַר כֵּלִים וּלְהַצִּיל מִמֶּנָּה רְבִיעִית בְּטָהֳרָה קֹדֶם שֶׁתֵּרֵד כֻּלָּהּ וְתִטַּמֵּא:


ואם יכולים להציל ממנה רביעית – if he able to search around behind the utensils and to save from it a fourth [of a LOG; a LOG= six eggs) in ritual purity prior to its all descending and becoming defiled.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 8:9:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/8:9:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/8:9:5)


**יַצִּיל.** יְחַזֵּר אַחַר כֵּלִים וְיַצִּיל, וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁבְּתוֹךְ כָּךְ שֶׁיְּחַזֵּר אַחַר כֵּלִים תֵּרֵד מִן הַתְּרוּמָה לַחֻלִּין שֶׁבַּתַּחְתּוֹנָה וְיִפָּסְדוּ הַחֻלִּין, לֹא יְטַמֵּא הַתְּרוּמָה בְּיָדַיִם לְהַצִּיל הַחֻלִּין, כֵּיוָן שֶׁיָּכוֹל לְהַצִּיל מִמֶּנָּה רְבִיעִית הַלֹּג בְּטָהֳרָה, שֶׁהוּא דָּבָר חָשׁוּב:


יציל – he should go around behind he utensils and save it, and even though it is within , he should go round behind the utensils that it would descend from the heave-offering to the unconsecraed produce that is in the lower [vats] and he will lose the heave-offering, he should no defile the heave offering with hands to save the heave-offering, since he is able to save from it one-quarter of a LOG in ritual purity which is an important thing.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 8:9:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/8:9:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/8:9:6)


**וְאִם לָאו.** שֶׁלֹּא יִמְצָא כְּלִי טָהוֹר, וְסוֹף סוֹף כֻּלָּהּ לְטֻמְאָה אַזְלָא, בְּהָא פְּלִיגֵי, דְּרַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ סָבַר יְטַמְּאֶנָּה בַּיָּד, לְהַצִּיל אֶת הַחֻלִּין, כִּדְקָתָנֵי סֵיפָא וְעַל זוֹ וְעַל זוֹ אָמַר רַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ וְכוּ'. וְרַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר סָבַר אַף עַל גַּב דְּסוֹף סוֹף כֻּלָּהּ לְטֻמְאָה אַזְלָא, לֹא יְטַמְּאֶנָּה בַּיָּד לְהַצִּיל הַחֻלִּין. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר:


ואם לאו – that he should not find a totally pure utensil, and eventually, all of it goes o riual impurity, and in this they dispute, for Rabbi Yehoshua holds that he should defile it by hand, to save the heave-offering as it is taught at the end [of this chapter, Mishnah 11) “but as regards both of these cases (Mishnayot 9-10), Rabbi Yehoshua stated, etc. But Rabbi Eliezer holds that even though that ultimately, it would all go to become unclean, he should not make it unclean by hand to save the Hullin, but the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Eliezer.



### Mishnah 10

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 8:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/8:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/8:10:1)


**וְכֵן חָבִית שֶׁל שֶׁמֶן.** טְהוֹרָה שֶׁנִּשְׁפְּכָה וְכֻלָּהּ הוֹלֶכֶת לְאִבּוּד, פְּלִיגֵי כְּדִלְעֵיל. וּלְהָכִי קָתָנֵי גַבֵּי שֶׁמֶן נִשְׁפְּכָה וְלֹא נָקַט כְּמוֹ גַבֵּי יַיִן, מִשּׁוּם דִּבְחָבִית שֶׁל שֶׁמֶן [שֶׁנִּשְׁבְּרָה] בַּגַּת הָעֶלְיוֹנָה, וּבַתַּחְתּוֹנָה חֻלִּין טְמֵאִים, מוֹדֶה רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר וְרַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ שֶׁאִם יָכוֹל לְהַצִּיל מִמֶּנָּה רְבִיעִית בְּטָהֳרָה יַצִּיל וְאִם לָאו תֵּרֵד וְאַל יְטַמְּאֶנָּה בַּיָּד, מִשּׁוּם דְּשֶׁמֶן רָאוּי לְהַדְלִיק וְלֹא חָשׁוּ לְהֶפְסֵד מֻעָט. וּבְיַיִן נַמִּי לֹא נֶחְלְקוּ אֶלָּא דַוְקָא בִּדְאִיכָּא בַּתַּחְתּוֹנָה פָּחוֹת מִמֵּאָה חֻלִּין, שֶׁנַּעֲשִׂין הַחֻלִּין כֻּלָּן מְדֻמָּע וְאִיכָּא הֶפְסֵד, אֲבָל אִי אִיכָּא מֵאָה חֻלִּין כֻּלְּהוּ מוֹדוּ דְּאַל יְטַמְּאֶנָּה בַּיָּד, מִשּׁוּם דְּלֵיכָּא הֶפְסֵד:


וכן חבית של שמן – [a jug of] pure [oil] that spilled, and everything goes to loss, they dispute as what is above (in Mishnah 9), and thus they teach regarding oil that spilled, and they did not take it like regarding wine, because a barrel of oil [that broke] in the upper vat, but in the bottom is unconsecrated ritually impure produce, Rabbi Eliezer and Rabbi Yehoshua agree that if he is able to save from it one-fourth [of a LOG] in ritual purity, he should save it, and if not, let it descend, and he should not defile it by hand, because the oil is appropriate to kindle and they did not trouble to lose a little bit. And with wine, also, they did not dispute, other than specifically when the lower [vat] has less than one-hundred [parts] of unconsecrated produce, for the entire unconsecrated produce becomes forbidden even for non-priests, and there is loss, but if there is one-hundred [parts] of unconsecrated produce, everyone admits, do not defile it by hand because there is no loss.



### Mishnah 11

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 8:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/8:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/8:11:1)


**עַל זוֹ וְעַל זוֹ.** עַל זוֹ שֶׁנּוֹלַד לָהּ סְפֵק טֻמְאָה, וְעַל זוֹ שֶׁהוֹלֶכֶת לְאִבּוּד, אָמַר רַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ שֶׁאֵינוֹ מֻזְהָר עַל טֻמְאָתָן, וְאוֹתָהּ שֶׁמֻּזְהָר עָלֶיהָ בַּל תְּטַמֵּא, כֵּיצַד, הָיָה עוֹבֵר וְכוּ', דְּקָאָמַר רַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ יַנִּיחַ לְפָנָיו אַחַת עַל הַסֶּלַע כְּדֵי שֶׁלֹּא יִגְרֹם טֻמְאָה לָאֲחֵרוֹת, וְאַף אוֹתָהּ אַחַת יַנִּיחַ עַל הַסֶּלַע וְלֹא יִתְּנֶנָּה בְיָדוֹ, שֶׁלֹּא יְטַמְּאֶנָּה בַּיָּדַיִם. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ:


ועל זו ועל זו – on that which a doubt of ritual impurity resulted, and on this that is getting lost, Rabbi Yehoshua stated he is not warned on their ritual defilement, and that which is is warned about, lest it become defiled, how so? He was passing [from place to place with loaves of heave-offering in his hand when a heathen approaches him who says to him – give him one of them so that he can defile it and if not he would defile all of them], Rabbi Yehoshua stated, he should leave one of them on the rock in order that he doesn’t cause ritual defilement to the others. Ans even that one, he should leave on the rock and not give it to him in his hand, so that he would not defile the hands. And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yehoshua.



### Mishnah 12

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 8:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/8:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/8:12:1)


**תְּנוּ לָנוּ אַחַת מִכֶּם.** שֶׁרוֹצִים לָבֹא עָלֶיהָ בְּאֹנֶס:


תנו לנו אחת מכם – they want to come upon her with force.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 8:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/8:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/8:12:2)


**יְטַמְּאוּ כֻּלָּן.** דְּאֵין דּוֹחִים נֶפֶשׁ מִפְּנֵי נֶפֶשׁ. וּבְהָא מוֹדוּ רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר וְרַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ:


יטמאו כולן – they do not set aside the regard due to one human life for the sake of saving another human life, and in this, Rabbi Eliezer and Rabbi Yehoshua agree.



## Chapter 9


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 9:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/9:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/9:1:1)


**הַזּוֹרֵעַ תְּרוּמָה שׁוֹגֵג יוֹפַךְ.** חוֹרֵשׁ וּמְהַפֵּךְ כְּדֵי שֶׁלֹּא יִגְדַּל, וְאֵין בְּכָךְ כְּלוּם:


הזורע תרומה שוגג יופך - he ploughs and turns it over, in order that will not grow, and there is nothing in this.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 9:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/9:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/9:1:2)


**וּמֵזִיד יְקַיֵּם.** כֵּיוָן שֶׁנִּקְרָא שֵׁם תְּרוּמָה עַל הַשָּׂדֶה הָיָה נִרְאֶה כִּמְאַבֵּד אֶת הַתְּרוּמָה:


ומזיד יקיים – since it was called by the designation of Terumah/heave-offering on the field, it would appear like losing the heave-offering.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 9:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/9:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/9:1:3)


**וְאִם הֵבִיאָה שְׁלִישׁ אֲפִלּוּ שׁוֹגֵג יְקַיֵּם.** מִשּׁוּם דְּאָסוּר לְאַבֵּד אֶת הַתְּרוּמָה:


ואם הביאה שליש אפילו שוגג יקיים – because it is prohibited to destroy the Terumah/heave-offering.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 9:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/9:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/9:1:4)


**וּבְפִשְׁתָּן מֵזִיד יוֹפַךְ.** וַאֲפִלּוּ הֵבִיאָה שְׁלִישׁ, כְּדֵי שֶׁלֹּא יֵהָנֶה בַּקִּסְמִין וְיַחֲשֹׁב שֶׁהַזֶּרַע בִּלְבָד הוּא דַּהֲוֵי תְרוּמָה וְאָסוּר, וְעִקַּר פִּשְׁתָּן מִשּׁוּם קִסְמִין הוּא וְלֹא מִשּׁוּם זֶרַע:


ובפשתן מזיד יופך – and even if it brought forth one-third, in order that the chips will not benefit, and he will think that the seed alone is what is Terumah/heave-offering and is prohibited. But the essence of the flax is because of the chips and not because of the seed.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 9:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/9:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/9:2:1)


**בִּדְמֵי תְרוּמָה.** דְּגִדּוּלֵי תְרוּמָה אֲסוּרִים לְזָרִים:


בדמי תרומה – that the growth of heave-offering is forbidden to foreigners (i.e., non-Kohanim).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 9:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/9:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/9:2:2)


**לֹא יְלַקְּטוּ אֶלָּא עֲנִיֵּי כֹהֲנִים.** דְּחַיְשִׁינַן אַדִּמְלַקְּטֵי לֵיהּ שָׁדוּ לְפוּמַיְהוּ:


לא ילקטו אלא עניי כהנים – for we suspect that when they glean it they will cast it into their mouths.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 9:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/9:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/9:2:3)


**אִם כֵּן לֹא יְלַקְּטוּ אֶלָּא טְהוֹרִים.** דְּהָא כֹּהֵן טָמֵא אָסוּר בִּתְרוּמָה. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי עֲקִיבָא:


אם כן לא ילקטו אלא טהורים – because a ritually impure Kohen is prohibited with [consuming] Terumah/heave offering. And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Akiva.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 9:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/9:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/9:3:1)


**וְחַיֶּבֶת בְּמַעַשְׂרוֹת.** מַעֲשֵׂר רִאשׁוֹן וְשֵׁנִי. וְהוּא הַדִּין בִּתְרוּמָה:


וחייבת במעשרות – First Tithe and Second [Tithe], and the same law applies with heave-offering/Terumah.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 9:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/9:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/9:3:2)


**וּבְמַעֲשַׂר עָנִי.** אִם הִיא שָׁנָה שְׁלִישִׁית אוֹ שִׁשִּׁית שֶׁל שְׁמִטָּה שֶׁמַּעֲשֵׂר עָנִי נוֹהֵג בָּהֶן:


ובמעשר עני – if it is the third year or the sixth year of the Shmittah (the seven year agricultural cycle), when the Poor Man’s Tithe is practiced on them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 9:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/9:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/9:3:3)


**הַחוֹבֵט.** גִּדּוּלֵי תְרוּמָה הַלָּלוּ בְּמַקְלוֹת, הֲרֵי זֶה מְשֻׁבָּח, כְּדֵי שֶׁלֹּא יִצְטָרֵךְ לַחֲסֹם אֶת הַבְּהֵמָה שֶׁדָּשׁ בָּהּ, שֶׁאָסוּר לְהַנִּיחָהּ לֶאֱכֹל בִּתְרוּמָה אִם אֵינָהּ פָּרָתוֹ שֶׁל כֹּהֵן:


החובט – growth of these heave-offerings with a staffs, behold this is praiseworthy , in order that he won’t need to muzzle the cattle that threshes it, for it is prohibited to allow her to eat with heave-offering if she is not the cow of a Kohen.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 9:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/9:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/9:3:4)


**כְּפִיפוֹת.** סַלִּין:


כפיפות – baskets.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 9:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/9:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/9:3:5)


**מֵאוֹתוֹ הַמִּין.** שֶׁל חֻלִּין, אִם חִטִּים חִטִּים וְאִם שְׂעוֹרִים שְׂעוֹרִים:


מאותו המין – of unconsecrated produce, if it is wheat, wheat, and if it barley, barley.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 9:3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/9:3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/9:3:6)


**נִמְצָא לֹא זוֹמֵם.** כְּלוֹמַר אֵינוֹ חוֹסֵם וְנוֹתֵן זֶמֶם לְתוֹךְ פִּיהָ, שֶׁהֲרֵי הִיא אוֹכֶלֶת מֵאוֹתוֹ הַמִּין:


נמצא לא זומם – meaning to say, he does not muzzle (by complying with the Torah law in Deuteronomy 25:4) and place a muzzle in its mouth, for behold it eats from the same species.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 9:3:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/9:3:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/9:3:7)


**וְלֹא מַאֲכִילָהּ תְּרוּמָה.** דְּמִשֶּׁל חֻלִּין שֶׁבַּכְּפִיפָה הַתְּלוּיָה בְּצַוָּארָהּ הִיא אוֹכֶלֶת:


ולא מאכילה תרומה – in the basket, it is of unconsecrated produce that is supending by its neck that she consumes.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 9:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/9:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/9:4:1)


**גִּדּוּלֵי תְרוּמָה תְּרוּמָה.** מִדְּרַבָּנָן, עָבְדוּ רַבָּנָן תַּקַּנְתָּא מִשּׁוּם תְּרוּמָה טְמֵאָה בְּיַד כֹּהֵן, כִּי הֵיכִי דְּלֹא לִישְׁהֵי לַהּ לְגַבֵּיהּ עַד זְמַן הַזֶּרַע כְּדֵי לְזָרְעָהּ וְאָתֵי בָהּ לִידֵי תַקָּלָה, וּמִשּׁוּם הָכִי גָּזְרוּ שֶׁיִּהְיוּ גִדּוּלֵי תְרוּמָה כִּתְרוּמָה:


גדולי תרומה תרומה – from the Rabbis the Rabbis made an ordinance because of ritually impure heave-offering in the hand of a Kohen, just as that it should not be delayed/tarried with him until the time of sowing in order to sow it, and it comes to a stumbling block/snare, and because of this, they decreed that growth of heave-offering is like heave offering.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 9:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/9:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/9:4:2)


**וְגִדּוּלֵי גִדּוּלִין חֻלִּין.** אִם חָזַר וְזָרַע אוֹתָן גִּדּוּלִין, גִּדּוּלֵיהֶן חֻלִּין:


וגדולי גדולין חולין – he did not go back and sow that growth, its growth would be unconsecrated produce.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 9:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/9:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/9:4:3)


**הַטֶּבֶל.** גִּדּוּלָיו חֻלִּין, מִשּׁוּם דְּרֻבּוֹ חֻלִּין:


הטבל – its growth is unconsecrated produce because the majority of it is unconsecrated produce.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 9:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/9:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/9:4:4)


**וּמַעֲשֵׂר רִאשׁוֹן.** נַמִּי מִשּׁוּם דְּרֻבּוֹ חֻלִּין:


ומעשר ראשון – also because its majority is unconsecrated produce.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 9:4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/9:4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/9:4:5)


**וּסְפִיחֵי שְׁבִיעִית.** לְפִי שֶׁאֵינָן מְצוּיִין וְאֵינָן נוֹהֲגִים בְּכָל שָׁנָה:


וספיחי שביעית – because they are not found and are not practiced in every year.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 9:4:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/9:4:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/9:4:6)


**וּתְרוּמַת חוּצָה לָאָרֶץ.** נַמִּי לְפִי שֶׁאֵינָהּ מְצוּיָה:


ותרומת חוץ לארץ – also because it is not found.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 9:4:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/9:4:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/9:4:7)


**וְהַמְדֻמָּע.** שֶׁרֻבּוֹ חֻלִּין:


המדומע – that its majority is unconsecrated produce.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 9:4:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/9:4:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/9:4:8)


**וּבִכּוּרִים.** שֶׁאֵינָן מְצוּיִין, [שֶׁאֵינָן] נוֹהֲגִים אֶלָּא בְּשִׁבְעַת הַמִּינִים:


ובכורים – that are not found [that are] practiced other than with the seven species (i.e., wheat, barley, pomegranate, [grape] vines, figs, olives and date honey – see Deuteronomy 8:8).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 9:4:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/9:4:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/9:4:9)


**גִּדּוּלֵי הֶקְדֵּשׁ וּמַעֲשֵׂר שֵׁנִי חֻלִּין.** שֶׁאִם זָרַע מֵהֶן סְאָה אַחַת וְהוֹסִיף כַּמָּה סְאָה, חֻלִּין, אַף בְּדָבָר שֶׁאֵין זַרְעוֹ כָלֶה:


גדולי הקדש ומעשר שני חולין – that if he sowed from them one Seah and added several Seah, it is unconsecrated produce. Even with something where its seed does not disintegrate..


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 9:4:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/9:4:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/9:4:10)


**וּפוֹדֶה אוֹתָם בִּזְמַן זַרְעָם.** פּוֹדֶה כָּל הָאוֹצָר בִּדְמֵי אוֹתָהּ סְאָה:


ופודה אותם בזמן זרעם – he redeems all the storehouse with the value of that Seah.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 9:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/9:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/9:5:1)


**מֵאָה לִגְנָה שֶׁל תְּרוּמָה.** שָׂדֶה שֶׁנִּזְרְעָה עֲרוּגוֹת עֲרוּגוֹת, וְיֵשׁ בָּהּ מֵאָה עֲרוּגוֹת שֶׁל תְּרוּמָה וְאַחַת שֶׁל חֻלִּין, וְלֹא יְדִיעַ הֵי דְחֻלִּין:


מאה לגנה של תרומה – a field that was sown with garden beds and there are one hundred garden beds of heave offering and one of unconsecrated produce and it is not known which of these is of unconsecrated produce.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 9:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/9:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/9:5:2)


**כֻּלָּן מֻתָּרִים.** קֻלָּא הוּא שֶׁהֵקֵלּוּ בְּגִדּוּלֵי תְרוּמָה, דַּעֲרוּגָה אַחַת שֶׁל חֻלִּין מַתֶּרֶת עֲרוּגוֹת רַבּוֹת בְּדָבָר שֶׁזַּרְעוֹ כָלֶה:


כולן מותרים – it is a leniency that they (i.e., the Rabbis) made with regard to the growth of heave-offering that one garden bed of unconsecrated produce is permits many garden beds with something where its seed has disintegrated.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 9:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/9:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/9:5:3)


**אֲבָל בְּדָבָר שֶׁאֵין זַרְעוֹ כָלֶה.** אַחַת שֶׁל תְּרוּמָה אוֹסֶרֶת מֵאָה שֶׁל חֻלִּין, לְפִי שֶׁאֵין הַקַּרְקַע עוֹלָה בְּאַחַת וּמֵאָה:


אבל בדבר שאין זרעו כלה – one [garden-bed] of heave-offering prohibits one-hundred of unconsecrated produce, because the land does not neutralize with one and one-hundred.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 9:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/9:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/9:6:1)


**הַטֶּבֶל גִּדּוּלָיו מֻתָּרִים.** בַּאֲכִילַת עֲרַאי, (כשאכל) [כִּשְׁאָר] טֶבֶל שֶׁלֹּא נִגְמְרָה מְלַאכְתּוֹ:


הטבל גדוליו מותרים – with an incidental meal, when he ate [he needs to say, like the rest] of the eatables forbidden to be consumed pending the separation of sacred gifts, that their work was not completed.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 9:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/9:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/9:6:2)


**בְּדָבָר שֶׁזַּרְעוֹ כָלֶה.** הַשְׁתָּא מְפָרֵשׁ תַּנָא לְמִלְּתֵיהּ דִּתְנַן לְעֵיל הַטֶּבֶל גִּדּוּלָיו חֻלִּין לֹא הַוְיָא אֶלָּא בְּדָבָר שֶׁזַּרְעוֹ כָלֶה. אֲבָל בְּדָבָר שֶׁאֵין זַרְעוֹ כָלֶה. אֲפִלּוּ גִּדּוּלֵי גִדּוּלִין אֲסוּרִים בַּאֲכִילַת עֲרַאי:


בדבר שזרעו כלה – now the Tanna/teacher explains this matter that is taught in the Mishnah above (Mishnah 8), that eatables forbidden to be consumed pending the separation of sacred gifts, its growths are unconsecrated produce and they would not be so other than with something where its seed disintegrated.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 9:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/9:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/9:6:3)


**הַשּׁוּם כַּשְּׂעוֹרָה.** כְּלוֹמַר הַשּׁוּם חָשׁוּב זַרְעוֹ כָלֶה כְּמוֹ הַשְּׂעוֹרָה. וְנָקַט שְׂעוֹרָה לְפִי שֶׁאֵין בַּתְּבוּאוֹת דָּבָר שֶׁיִּהְיֶה זַרְעוֹ כָלֶה וְנֶאֱבָד מְהֵרָה כְּמוֹ הַשְּׂעוֹרָה. פֵּרוּשׁ אַחֵר הַשּׁוּם שֶׁהוּא גָדוֹל כִּשְׂעוֹרָה אֵין זַרְעוֹ כָלֶה, אֲבָל פָּחוֹת מִכִּשְׂעוֹרָה זַרְעוֹ כָלֶה. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


אבל בדבר שאין זרעו כלה – meaning to say the garlic is considered that its seed disintegrates like the barley, and it (i.e., the Mishnah) took “barley” because there is nothing in the grains where its seed disintegrates and is lost quickly like barley. Another explanation: the garlic which is large like barley, its seed does not disintegrate, but less than the equivalent of a barley seed, its seed disintegrates. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehuda.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 9:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/9:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/9:7:1)


**הַמְנַכֵּשׁ.** תּוֹלֵשׁ עֲשָׂבִים רָעִים הַגְּדֵלִים בְּתוֹךְ הַתְּבוּאָה וְהַיְרָקוֹת:


המנכש – tears out bad grasses that grow amindst the grain and the vegetables.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 9:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/9:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/9:7:2)


**בַּחֲסִיּוֹת.** כְּגוֹן הַשּׁוּם וְהַלּוּף וְהַבְּצָלִים וְהַכַּרְתִּי, כָּל אֵלּוּ נִקְרָאִים חֲסִיּוֹת:


בחסיות – such as garlic and LOF (plants similar to colccasia, with edible leaves and root, and hearing beans – classified with onions and garlic) and onions and leek-green plants, all of these are called חסיות/leeks.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 9:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/9:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/9:7:3)


**אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁפֵּרוֹתָיו.** שֶׁל עוֹבֵד כּוֹכָבִים טֶבֶל, דְּסָבַר הַאי תַּנָּא אֵין קִנְיָן לְעוֹבֵד כּוֹכָבִים בְּאֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל לְהַפְקִיעַ מִן הַמַּעֲשֵׂר, וְגִדּוּלֵי טֶבֶל טֶבֶל בְּדָבָר שֶׁאֵין זַרְעוֹ כָלֶה, אֲפִלּוּ הָכִי אוֹכֵל מֵהֶן עֲרַאי:


אע"פ שפרירותיו – of the idolater are eatables forbidden to be eaten pending the separation of sacred gifts, for this Tanna holds that there is no acquisition for an idolater in the Land of Israel to release him from the obligation for tithing, and the growth of eatables forbidden to be eaten pending the separation of sacred gifts is eatables forbidden to be consumed pending the separation of sacred gifts like something where the seed does not disintegrate, nevertheless, he eats from them an incidental meal.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 9:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/9:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/9:7:4)


**שְׁתִילֵי תְרוּמָה.** כְּגוֹן שְׁתִילֵי כְרוּב וּתְרָדִין: **שֶׁנִּטְמְאוּ וּשְׁתָלָן טָהֲרוּ מִלְּטַמֵּא.** דְּכֵיוָן שֶׁנִּתְחַבְּרוּ לַקַּרְקַע נִתְבַּטְּלוּ מִתּוֹרַת אֹכֶל. וְאִם תֹּאמַר כֵּיוָן דְּעִקַּר גְּזֵרָה גִּדּוּלֵי תְרוּמָה כִּתְרוּמָה אֵינָהּ אֶלָּא מִשּׁוּם תְּרוּמָה טְמֵאָה בְּיַד כֹּהֵן, דִּלְמָא מַשְׁהֵי לַהּ גַּבֵּיהּ וְאָתֵי לִידֵי תַקָּלָה, אַכַּתִּי כֵּיוָן דְּטָהֲרוּ מַשְׁהֵי לַהּ גַּבֵּיהּ. וְיֵשׁ לוֹמַר דִּתְרוּמָה נִמְכֶּרֶת בְּזוֹל הַרְבֵּה לְפִי שֶׁאֵינָהּ רְאוּיָה אֶלָּא לְכֹהֲנִים טְהוֹרִים וְאִם נִטְמֵאת טְעוּנָה שְׂרֵפָה, הִלְכָּךְ אֲפִלּוּ אִם הָיוּ נוֹתְנִים לַכֹּהֵן תְּרוּמָה כְּדֵי לְזָרְעָהּ לֹא הָיָה (מבטל) [מַפְסִיד] שָׂדֵהוּ, אֲבָל אִם הָיוּ הַגִּדּוּלִין חֻלִּין הָיָה מַשְׁהֶה תְּרוּמָה טְמֵאָה כְּדֵי לְזָרְעָהּ:


שתילי תרומה – as, for example, saplings of cabbage and a species of beets. שנטמאו ושתלו טהרו מלטמא – and since they were attached/fastened to the ground, they became neutralized from he law of food. And if you should say that since the essence of the decree that the growth of heave-offering is like heave-offering, it is not other than because ritually impure heave-offering is in the hand of the Kohen, perhaps he will tarry near it and will become a stumbling block regarding him. Yet, since he purified it, he tarries with it. And one can say that heave-offering that is sold at a greatly cheapened price because it is not appropriate other than for ritually pure Kohanim and if it is defiled, it requires burning. Therefore, even if they would give heave-offering to a Kohen in order to sow it, he would [you must say “lose”] from abolishing his field. But if they were the growths of unconsecrated produce, he would detain the ritually impure heave-offering in order to sow it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 9:7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/9:7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/9:7:5)


**וַאֲסוּרִים לֶאֱכֹל.** מַעֲלָה בְּעַלְמָא הִיא:


ואסורים לאכול – it is a mere preference.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 9:7:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/9:7:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/9:7:6)


**עַד שֶׁיָּגוֹם הָאֹכֶל.** יַחְתּוֹךְ כָּל הָרָאוּי לַאֲכִילָה, וּמַה שֶּׁיִּגְדַּל אַחַר כָּךְ יְהֵא מֻתָּר:


עד שיגום האוכל – cut all that is appropriate for eating, and what will grow afterwards will be permitted.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 9:7:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/9:7:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/9:7:7)


**עַד שֶׁיָּגוֹם וְיִשְׁנֶה.** שֶׁיַּחְתּוֹךְ מַה שֶּׁגָּדַל גַּם בַּפַּעַם הַשְּׁנִיָּה, וּמַה שֶּׁיִּגְדַּל מִשֵּׁנִי וְאֵילָךְ יִהְיֶה מֻתָּר. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


עד שיגום וישנה – he will cutg what that grew also a second time, and and what that would grow from the second time onwards will be permitted. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehuda.



## Chapter 10


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 10:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/10:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/10:1:1)


**בָּצָל שֶׁנְּתָנוֹ לְתוֹךְ עֲדָשִׁים.** [שֶׁל חֻלִּין], וְהוּא הַדִּין בָּצָל שֶׁל תְּרוּמָה שֶׁנְּתָנוֹ לְתוֹךְ עֲדָשִׁים. [טָעוּת הַמַּעְתִּיק יֵשׁ כָּאן וְכָךְ צָרִיךְ לוֹמַר, בָּצָל שֶׁנְּתָנוֹ לְתוֹךְ עֲדָשִׁים. בָּצָל שֶׁל תְּרוּמָה שֶׁנְּתָנוֹ לְתוֹךְ עֲדָשִׁים שֶׁל חֻלִּין], וְהוּא הַדִּין לְבָצָל שֶׁל חֻלִּין שֶׁנְּתָנוֹ לְתוֹךְ עֲדָשִׁים שֶׁל תְּרוּמָה. וּמַיְרֵי כְּגוֹן שֶׁנְּתָנוֹ בָּעֲדָשִׁים אַחַר בִּשּׁוּלוֹ וְיָצְאוּ מִמֶּנּוּ מֵימָיו, שֶׁאָז אֵין הַבָּצָל כְּשֶׁהוּא שָׁלֵם פּוֹלֵט בָּעֲדָשִׁים וְלֹא בוֹלֵעַ מֵהֶם. אֲבָל אִם נִתְבַּשֵּׁל הַבָּצָל בָּעֲדָשִׁים פְּשִׁיטָא דְּיָהֵיב וּבָלַע טַעֲמָא אַף כְּשֶׁהוּא שָׁלֵם:


בטל שנתנו לתוך עדשים – [of unconsecrated produce] and the same law applies [regarding] an onion of heave-offering that was placed within lentils [there is an error of the copier here, and as one needs to say: ‘onion that was placed in the midst of lentils,” with an onion of heave-offering that was placed within lentils [[of unconsecrated produce]], and the same law applies to onion of unconsecrated produce that was placed into lentils of unconsecrated produce, and we are speaking as for example, that he placed it in the lentils after it became ripe and its water had gone out from it, for then, the onion when it is whole discharges on the lentils but does not absorb from them. But if the onion had been ripened with the lentils, it is obvious, that it gives and absorbs taste, even when it is whole.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 10:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/10:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/10:1:2)


**בְּנוֹתֵן טַעַם.** דְּטָעֵים לְהוּ כֹּהֵן, שֶׁמֻּתָּר בְּחֻלִּין וּבִתְרוּמָה. אִי אִית בְּהוּ טַעֲמָא דִתְרוּמָה הַכֹּל אָסוּר לְזָרִים, וְאִי לֹא הַכֹּל חֻלִּין כְּשֶׁהָיָה:


בנותן טעם – that the Kohen tastes them, that it is permitted in unconsecrated produce and in Terumah/heave offering, if it has the taste of heave-offering, everything is prohibited to foreigners (i.e., non-Kohanim), and if not, it is all unconsecrated produce as it was.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 10:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/10:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/10:1:3)


**וּשְׁאָר כָּל תַּבְשִׁיל.** כְּגוֹן שׁוּם וְקַפְלוֹט, וְכַיּוֹצֵא בָהֶן:


ושאר כל תבשיל – as, for example, garlic and purret (i.e., leek with a head), in a similar manner.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 10:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/10:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/10:1:4)


**מַתִּיר בְּצַחֲנָה.** אִם נָתַן בָּצָל שֶׁל תְּרוּמָה בְּצַחֲנָה, שֶׁהֵם דָּגִים קְטַנִּים הַכְּבוּשׁוֹת בְּצִיר, נוֹטֵל אֶת הַבָּצָל וְהַצַּחֲנָה מֻתָּר, וּבִלְבַד שֶׁיִּהְיֶה הַבָּצָל שָׁלֵם, שֶׁלֹּא נִמּוֹחַ וְלֹא נִתְעָרֵב עִם הַצַּחֲנָה. שֶׁאֵין הַבָּצָל נִתָּן לְתוֹכוֹ אֶלָּא לְהַעֲבִיר זֻהֲמַת הַדָּגִים וְלֹא כְדֵי לִתֵּן בּוֹ טַעַם. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


מתיר בצחנה – if he placed onion of heave offering in a small fish preserved in brine, which are small fish that are pickled in brine, he takes the onion and the small fish preserved in brine it is permitted, as long as the onion will be whole and it is not dissolved and is not combined with the small fish preserved in brine, for the onion that is placed in side it rather than to transfer the froth of the fish and not in order to give it a taste. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehuda.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 10:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/10:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/10:2:1)


**תַּפּוּחַ.** שֶׁל תְּרוּמָה שֶׁרִסְּקוֹ וּנְתָנוֹ כְּשֶׁהוּא מְרֻסָּק וְכָתוּשׁ לְתוֹךְ הָעִסָּה וְחִמְּצָהּ, הוֹאִיל וְחֻלִּין מֵחֲמַת הַתְּרוּמָה נִתְחַמְּצָה, הָעִסָּה אֲסוּרָה לְזָרִים:


תפוח – of heave-offering was chopped and placed when it is chopped and pounded into the started dough and it becomes leavened for since unconsecrated produce because of the heave-offering, that the dough was leavened is forbidden to foreigners (i.e., non-Kohanim).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 10:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/10:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/10:2:2)


**אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהִבְאִישׁוּ.** מֵחֲמַת הַשְּׂעוֹרִים שֶׁל תְּרוּמָה, מֵימָיו מֻתָּרִים:


אע"פ שהבאישו – because of the barley of heave-offering, its waters are permitted.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 10:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/10:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/10:3:1)


**הָרוֹדֶה.** הוֹצָאַת הַפַּת מִן הַתַּנּוּר נִקְרֵאת רְדִיָּה:


הרודה – removal of the bread/pita from the oven is called רדייה /detaching/taking bread out of the oven.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 10:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/10:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/10:3:2)


**וּנְתָנָהּ עַל פִּי חָבִית שֶׁל יַיִן שֶׁל תְּרוּמָה.** דְּהַשְׁתָּא הַפַּת קוֹלֶטֶת וְשׁוֹאֶבֶת מֵרֵיחַ הַיַּיִן:


ונתנה ע"פ חבית של יין של תרומה – that now, the bead/pita absorbs and imbibes from the scent/smell of the wine.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 10:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/10:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/10:3:3)


**רַבִּי מֵאִיר אוֹסֵר.** דְּסָבַר רֵיחָא מִלְּתָא הִיא:


ר"מ אוסר – that he holds that the scent/smell is a thing/affair.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 10:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/10:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/10:3:4)


**וְרַבִּי יְהוּדָה מַתִּיר.** דְּסָבַר רֵיחָא לָאו מִלְּתָא הִיא:


ורבי יהודה מתיר – for he holds that the scent/smell is not a thing/matter.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 10:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/10:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/10:3:5)


**מִפְּנֵי שֶׁהַשְּׂעוֹרִים שׁוֹאֲבוֹת.** מוֹשְׁכוֹת מִלַּחְלוּחִית יַיִן שֶׁל תְּרוּמָה. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי:


מפני שהשעורים שובות – draw from the moistness of the wine of heave-offering, and the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yosi.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 10:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/10:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/10:4:1)


**אֶלָּא רֵיחַ כַּמּוֹן.** דְּדַוְקָא רֵיחַ שֶׁל (פטום) [כַּמּוֹן] נִכְנָס לְתוֹךְ הַפַּת, וְרֵיחָא לָאו מִלְּתָא הִיא, בֵּין בִּתְרוּמָה בֵּין בְּכָל אִסּוּרִין שֶׁבַּתּוֹרָה. וְאִי מִשּׁוּם דְּהֻסַּק הַתַּנּוּר בִּתְרוּמָה, אֵין אִסּוּרָהּ אִסּוּר הֲנָאָה:


אלא ריח כמון – that exactly the smell of (spices) [cumin] enters into the pita/bread, and the scent/smell is not a matter, whether with heave-offering/Terumah or with all of the prohibitions tht are in the Torah. And if it is because the oven was heated with heae-offering, its prohibition is ot a prohibition of deriving benefit.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 10:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/10:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/10:5:1)


**תִּלְתָּן.** בַּעֲרָבִי חולב"א, וּבְלַעַ"ז פינגריג"ו:


תלתן – In Arabic, CHOLBA, and in the foreign language, FENGREEG”U


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 10:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/10:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/10:5:2)


**אֲבָל לֹא בָעֵץ.** אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁטַּעַם עֵצוֹ וּפִרְיוֹ שָׁוֶה אֵין הָעֵץ מִצְטָרֵף עִם הַפְּרִי לֶאֱסֹר יַיִן שֶׁל חֻלִּין, לְפִי שֶׁאֵין הָעֵץ קָדוֹשׁ בִּתְרוּמָה. אֲבָל שְׁבִיעִית וְכִלְאַיִם וְהֶקְדֵּשׁ אַף הָעֵץ אָסוּר. וְהָעֵץ שֶׁל תִּלְתָּן בַּיַּיִן נוֹתֵן טַעַם לְשֶׁבַח הוּא:


אבל לא בעץ – even though that the taste of its stalk/tree and its fruit are equivalent, the tree does not combine with the fruit to prohibit the wine of unconsecrated produce, because the tree/stalk is not holy in heave-offering. But, [regarding] the Seventh Year and diverse seeds and that which is dedicated to the Temple, even the tree/stalk is prohibitd, for the stalk of fenugreek in wine provides a flavor to improve it.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 10:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/10:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/10:6:1)


**יִדָּלֵקוּ.** שֶׁאַף הָעֵץ נֶאֱסַר, וְכִלְאֵי הַכֶּרֶם טְעוּנִים שְׂרֵפָה, דִּכְתִיב (דברים כב) פֶּן תִּקְדַּשׁ הַמְּלֵאָה, פֶּן תּוּקַד אֵשׁ:


ידלקו – for even the tree/stalk is forbidden, and the mixed seats/diverse kinds of the vineyard require burning as it is written (Deuteronomy 22:9): “else the crop-from the seed [you have sown]- and the yield of the vineyard,” lest it become burned with fire (i.e., lest it become sacred -forbidden property – lest a fire must be lighted – (for burning it – Jerusalem Talmud, Pesahim 28c).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 10:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/10:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/10:6:2)


**וּמְחַשֵּׁב כַּמָּה זֶרַע יֶשׁ בָּהֶן.** דִּתְרוּמָה נִטֶּלֶת בְּמַחֲשָׁבָה וּבְאֹמֶד הַדַּעַת:


ומחשב כמה זרע יש בהן – for heave-offering/Terumah is taken with thought/care, and with estimating the quantity.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 10:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/10:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/10:6:3)


**וְאֵין צָרִיךְ לְהַפְרִישׁ אֶת הָעֵץ.** וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁטַּעַם עֵצוֹ וּפִרְיוֹ שָׁוִין:


וא"צ להפריש את העץ – and even though the taste of the stalk/tree and its fruit are equivalent


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 10:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/10:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/10:6:4)


**וְאִם הִפְרִישׁ.** קֹדֶם כְּתִישָׁה, אֵינוֹ יָכוֹל לִכְתֹּשׁ וּלְהַפְרִישׁ מִן הַזֶּרַע וְיִשָּׁאֵר הָעֵץ לְעַצְמוֹ, דְּכֵיוָן שֶׁקָּרָא עָלָיו שֵׁם תְּרוּמָה צָרִיךְ לִתְּנוֹ לַכֹּהֵן:


ואם הפריש – prior to crushing, he is not able to crush and to separate them from the seed, and that the stalk/tree would remain to himself, for since he designated upon it the title of “Terumah/heave-offering,” he must give it to the Kohen.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 10:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/10:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/10:7:1)


**שֶׁכְּבָשָׁן עִם זֵיתֵי תְרוּמָה.** דֶּרֶךְ זֵיתִים לְכָבְשָׁן בְּמַיִם וּבְמֶלַח, וּכְשֶׁזֵּיתֵי חֻלִּין פְּצוּעִין, דְּהַיְנוּ כְּתוּשִׁים אוֹ מְרֻסָּקִים, בּוֹלְעִים מִזֵּיתֵי תְרוּמָה, אַף אִם זֵיתֵי תְרוּמָה הֵם שְׁלֵמִים, וְאַף אִם כְּבָשָׁן בְּמַיִם שֶׁנִּכְבְּשׁוּ בָּהֶן זֵיתֵי תְרוּמָה, אָסוּר:


שכבשן עם זיתי תרומה – it is the manner for olives to pickle them in water and in salt, and when the unconsecrated olives are crushed, that is, squeezed open, or chopped, they absorb from the unconsecrated olives, even if the heave-offering of olives are pure, for even if he pickled them in water, olives of heave-offering/Terumah are forbidden.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 10:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/10:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/10:7:2)


**אֲבָל שְׁלֵמֵי חֻלִּין.** כְּשֶׁזֵּיתֵי חֻלִּין שְׁלֵמִין, אַף אִם כְּבָשָׁן עִם זֵיתֵי [תְרוּמָה] פְּצוּעִין, אֵין בּוֹלְעִין מֵהֶן:


אבל שלימי חולין – when unconsecrated olives ae full, even if they are pickled with olives [of heave-offering] that are crushed, hey do not absorb from them.



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 10:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/10:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/10:8:1)


**גָּרָב.** חָבִית:


גרב – arched, pouched vessel/wine jug.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 10:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/10:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/10:8:2)


**כָּל גָּרָב שֶׁהוּא מַחֲזִיק סָאתַיִם.** אִם נִכְבַּשׁ דָּג טָמֵא עִם הַרְבֵּה דָגִים טְהוֹרִים בְּתוֹךְ חָבִית, וְהֶחָבִית מַחֲזִיק סָאתַיִם, וְיֵשׁ מִצִּיר דָּג טָמֵא מִשְׁקַל עֶשֶׂר זוּזִים שֶׁל יְהוּדָה, שֶׁהֵם חָמֵשׁ סְלָעִים שֶׁל גָּלִיל, וְכָל הַשְּׁאָר צִיר דָּג טָהוֹר, שֶׁנִּמְצָא צִיר שֶׁל דָּג טָמֵא אֶחָד מִתְּשַׁע מֵאוֹת וְשִׁשִּׁים שֶׁל דָּג טָהוֹר, אָסוּר. וְאִם הָיָה הַצִּיר שֶׁל דָּג טָמֵא פָּחוֹת מִכֵּן, מֻתָּר:


כל גרב שהוא מחזיק סאתים – if impure fish was pickled with many pure fish inside the jug/vessel, and the vessel holds two Seah, and there is from the brine of the impure fish at a weigh of ten Zuzim of Judea, which are five Selaim of the Galilee, and all the rest is the brine of pure fish, that is found the brine of impure fish one in nine hundred and sixty [parts] of pure fish, it is prohibited. But, if the brine of the impure fish was less than this, it is permitted.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 10:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/10:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/10:8:3)


**רַבִּי יְהוּדָה אוֹמֵר רְבִיעִית לְסָאתַיִם.** כְּשֶׁיִּהְיֶה רְבִיעִית לֹג שֶׁל צִיר טָמֵא בְּתוֹךְ סָאתַיִם שֶׁל טָהוֹר אָסוּר הַכֹּל, פָּחוֹת מִכֵּן מֻתָּר. וְשִׁעוּר זֶה הוּא קָרוֹב לְמָאתַיִם. וְאַף עַל גַּב דְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה סָבַר מִין בְּמִינוֹ לֹא בָּטִיל, שָׁאנֵי צִיר, דְּזֵעָה בְּעַלְמָא הוּא וְאֵין אִסּוּרוֹ אֶלָּא מִדְּרַבָּנָן, לְכָךְ אָמַר רַבִּי יְהוּדָה דְּבָטֵל בְּקָרוֹב לְמָאתַיִם:


רבי יהודה אומר רביעית לסאתים – when there would be one-quarter of a LOG of impure brine within the two Seah of pure brine, everything is forbidden; less than this, it is permitted. And this measure is close to two-hundred, and even though that Rabbi Yehuda holds that it is must be something of the same species but it doesn’t voided/neutralized, for brine is different, for it is mere moisture, and its prohibition is none other than Rabbinic, therefore, Rabi Yehuda states that it is annulled in near to two-hundred.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 10:8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/10:8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/10:8:4)


**וְרַבִּי יוֹסֵי אוֹמֵר.** דְּבָטֵל בְּשִׁשָּׁה עָשָׂר. וְהִלְכְתָא דְצִיר בָּטֵל בְּיוֹתֵר מֵאֶלֶף, אִם יֵשׁ יוֹתֵר מֵאֶלֶף מִדָּג טָהוֹר וְאֶחָד מִדָּג טָמֵא הַכֹּל מֻתָּר, וְאִם לָאו הַכֹּל אָסוּר:


ורבי יוסי אומר – that it is annulled in sixteen. And the law is that brine is annulled in more than one-thousand, and if there is more than one-thousand from a pure fish and one from an impure fish, it is all permissible,and if not, everything is forbidden.



### Mishnah 9

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 10:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/10:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/10:9:1)


**לֹא פָסְלוּ אֶת צִירָן.** דְּהֵקֵלּוּ בְּצִיר שֶׁל חֲגָבִים טְמֵאִים שֶׁאֵינוֹ אוֹסֵר, מִפְּנֵי שֶׁאֵין לָהֶם דָּם וְאֵינוֹ אֶלָּא לַחְלוּחִית בְּעַלְמָא:


לא פסלו את צירן – that they were lenient with the brine of impure locusts that it does not prohibit, because they lack blood and it not anything other than mere moistness.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 10:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/10:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/10:9:2)


**שֶׁהוּא טָהוֹר.** לֹא מִבַּעְיָא דְּאֵינוֹ אוֹסֵר אֶת תַּעֲרֻבְתּוֹ, אֶלָּא הוּא עַצְמוֹ טָהוֹר. וַהֲלָכָה כְּעֵדוּתוֹ שֶׁל רַבִּי צָדוֹק:


שהוא טהור – needless to say that it does not prohibit the mixture, but it itself is ritually pure. And the But the Halakha is according to the testimony of Rabbi Tzadok.



### Mishnah 10

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 10:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/10:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/10:10:1)


**כָּל הַנִּכְבָּשִׁים זֶה עִם זֶה.** יַרְקוֹת חֻלִּין שֶׁנִּכְבְּשׁוּ עִם יְרָקוֹת שֶׁל תְּרוּמָה, אֵין הַתְּרוּמָה אוֹסֶרֶת אֶת הַחֻלִּין:


כל הנכבשים זה עם זה – unconsecrated vegetables that were pickled with vegetables of heave-offering, the heave-offering does not prohibit the unconsecrated produce.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 10:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/10:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/10:10:2)


**חוּץ מִן הַחֲסִית.** שׁוּם וְלוּף וּבָצָל וְכַרְתִי קְרוּיִם חֲסִית, דְּאַגַּב חוֹרְפַיְהוּ יְהָבֵי טַעֲמָא בְּכָל מִידֵי דְנִכְבָּשִׁין עִמָּהֶן. וּכְשֶׁהֵן שֶׁל תְּרוּמָה אוֹסְרִים בֵּין יָרָק בֵּין חֲסִית שֶׁל חֻלִּין. וּשְׁאָר יְרָקוֹת שֶׁל תְּרוּמָה אֵינָן אוֹסְרִים כְּשֶׁהֵן נִכְבָּשִׁים, אֲבָל מְבֻשָּׁלִים אוֹ נִשְׁלָקִים אוֹסְרִים:


חוץ מן החסית – garlic and LOF/a plant similar to colocasia with edible leaves and root and bearing beans, and onion and leeks are called חסית/leek plants, for along with their sharpness, they give a taste in everything that are pickled with them. And when they are of heave-offering, they forbid, whether it is unconsecrated vegetable, whether leek , and the rest of the vegetables of heave-offering do not prohibit when they are pickled, but if they are cooked or boiled, they prohibit.



### Mishnah 11

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Terumot 10:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/10:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Terumot/r/10:11:1)


**רַבִּי יוֹסֵי אוֹמֵר כָּל הַנִּשְׁלָקִים עִם הַתְּרָדִים אֲסוּרִים.** רַבִּי יוֹסֵי פָּלֵיג אַתַּנָּא קַמָּא, וְסָבַר דְּכָל שְׁאָר יְרָקוֹת אֵינָן אוֹסְרִים אֲפִלּוּ נִשְׁלָקִים, חוּץ מִן הַתְּרָדִים שֶׁל אִסּוּר, כְּגוֹן שֶׁל תְּרוּמָה וְשֶׁל כִּלְאֵי הַכֶּרֶם, שֶׁהֵן נוֹתְנִים טַעַם בִּשְׁלִיקָתָן:


רבי יוסי אומר כל שנשלקים עם התרדים אסורים – Rabbi Yosi disputes with the first Tanna/teacher and holds that all the rest of the vegetables do not prohibit even when they are boiled , except from the phohibited beats such as of heave-offering and mixed seeds of a vineyard that give taste in their boiling.
